To evaluate defenses under realistic conditions, we introduce LC-Inj-SQuAD, a long-context benchmark with an average of 4,032 words per context. We evaluate on four datasets spanning short-context QA (Inj-SQuAD, Inj-TriviaQA), long-context QA (LC-Inj-SQuAD), and agent tool-call injection (InjecAgent). Across all four, MINE achieves the best accuracy--security trade-off among training-free defenses. It is also the only defense that stays usable against a fully adaptive attacker, generalizes to agent tool-call injection, and achieves the lowest inference latency on long contexts.

</details>


### [51] [IRTIQA: Evolving in English, Evaluated in Urdu -- Does Agent Self-Evolution Transfer from English to Urdu and Code-Switched Input?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34303)
*Abdullah Hashmat,Danish Javed,Usman Naseem,Agha Ali Raza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 IRTIQA 基准（DROP、SNIPS、AppWorld、SkillsBench 的对齐英语/乌尔都语翻译/乌尔都语代码混合三版本），用以检验自我进化能力能否从英语迁移到乌尔都语；结论是自我进化普遍提升能力但难以弥合语言鸿沟，语言对等需作为显式设计目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体越来越多地从自身经验中学习（self-evolution），但其学习与评测几乎全在英语中进行，因此需要回答"自我进化能否跨语言迁移"这一问题，并为此构建缺少的多语言评测资源。

Method: 构建 IRTIQA 基准：基于矩阵式语言代码混合流水线并辅以人工校验，生成 DROP、SNIPS、AppWorld、SkillsBench 的英语、乌尔都语翻译与乌尔都语代码混合对齐变体；在此基准上对比三种自我进化框架（Tool-R0 自博弈强化学习、AgentEvolver 经验引导强化学习、Autogenesis 协议级进化）及其未进化基座模型。

Result: 自我进化常能提升能力：AgentEvolver 使 AppWorld 目标完成率从接近 0 升至各条件下 20-36%，Autogenesis 将 SkillsBench 通过率由 16.48% 提至 22.22%；但语言鸿沟几乎未缩小——进化后系统英语仍更强（DROP F1 差距最高达 0.26），多步任务差距最大，代码混合表现为独立条件而非退化的翻译；在共享 Gemma-4 31B 基座上，差距在进化前已存在，且基于 RL 的进化可能对乌尔都语推理的伤害大于英语。

Conclusion: 语言对等不会从自我改进中自动涌现，必须作为显式的设计目标；同时代码混合应被视为独立评测条件，而非翻译质量的下游表现。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly learn from their own experience, the tasks they learn from and are evaluated on are almost always in English. We ask whether self-evolution transfers from English to Urdu, and release IRTIQA, a benchmark of aligned English, Urdu-translated, and Urdu code-switched variants of DROP, SNIPS, AppWorld, and SkillsBench, built with a matrix-language code-switching pipeline and human verification. Using IRTIQA, we evaluate three self-evolving frameworks, Tool-R0 (self-play RL), AgentEvolver (experience-guided RL), and Autogenesis (protocol-level evolution), against their unevolved backbones. Self-evolution often raises capability: AgentEvolver lifts AppWorld goal completion from near zero to 20-36% across all conditions, and Autogenesis improves SkillsBench pass rate from 16.48% to 22.22%. But it rarely closes the language gap. Evolved systems remain stronger in English, by up to 0.26 DROP F1, the gap is largest on multi-step tasks, and code-switching behaves as a distinct condition rather than degraded translation. On a shared Gemma-4 31B backbone, the gap exists before evolution, and RL-based evolution can hurt Urdu reasoning more than English. Language parity must therefore be an explicit design target, as it does not emerge from self-improvement alone.

</details>


### [52] [Puzzling-Bench: Evaluating Language Models on 23,777 Human-Written Puzzles with Community-Verified Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34331)
*Abhinav Sharma,Li Li,Chien Van Nguyen,Sai Karthik Navuluru,Bo Ni,Xiangbo Gao,Xiaolei Huang,Yue Zhao,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Tyler Derr,Yu Wang,Hongjie Chen,Zhengzhong Tu,Thien Huu Nguyen,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Puzzling-Bench：23,777 道社区验证答案的谜题基准，含提示消融、类别分析与污染检测切片；九个模型最高 74.8%，17 题难核击败全部模型，图像缺失代价 24–35 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理评测多依赖标准题库，易受数据污染且缺乏多轮提示、类别与多模态维度的系统评估；作者希望用真实社区谜题（含答案、提示、标签、时间戳）构建可做提示消融、类别分析与污染检查的基准。

Method: 从 Puzzling Stack Exchange（2014–2026）收集 23,777 道谜题（谜语、密码、逻辑网格、侧向思维、文字游戏、趣味数学，三分之一含图像），每题附作者认可答案、LLM 抽取并人工抽检（50 题）的参考答案、作者提示、社区标签与创建日期；保留 254 道晚于九个模型中六个训练截止期的"滚动切片"作污染检测。评测九个大模型，采用两级评分（归一化精确匹配 + LLM 判官），并用来自两个模型家族的三名判官验证。

Result: 判官一致性 95–97%（kappa 0.82–0.96），单模型分数变动不超过 1.6 分，无同家族偏好；最佳模型 GPT-6.1 在共享 255 题集上达 74.8%；17 题难核击败全部九模型；模型失败高度相关，贪心集成中第二名仅贡献 1 题，而九模型 oracle 达 91%；100 题多模态试点中，去掉题目图像使两个最佳模型分别下降 24 与 35 分。

Conclusion: 谜题基准揭示了当前模型在非常规推理上的共同短板与失败同质性，集成收益有限而理论上限高，图像信息对多模态推理至关重要；数据集、评分流程与逐条溯源信息全部开源。

Abstract: We introduce Puzzling-Bench, a benchmark of 23,777 puzzles with community-verified answers drawn from the Puzzling Stack Exchange archive (2014-2026): riddles, ciphers, logic grids, lateral-thinking problems, wordplay and recreational mathematics, a third of them with images. Every puzzle carries the answer accepted by its author, a short reference answer taken from it (LLM-extracted for the evaluation sets and checked on a 50-puzzle human review), the author's own hints, community tags and a creation date, so that the benchmark supports hint ablations, category analysis and a rolling post-cutoff slice (254 puzzles posted after the training cutoff of six of the nine models we evaluate, including the best one) as a contamination check. We evaluate nine frontier and open models with a two-tier scorer (normalized exact match, then an LLM judge) and validate the judge with three judges from two model families: they agree on 95-97% of verdicts (kappa 0.82-0.96), move no model by more than 1.6 points, and show no own-family preference. The best model, GPT-6.1, reaches 74.8% on a shared 255-puzzle set; a hard core of 17 puzzles defeats all nine models; model failures are strongly correlated, and the second-best model contributes a single puzzle to a greedy ensemble, yet an oracle over all nine reaches 91%. On a 100-puzzle multimodal pilot, withholding the question's image costs the two best models 24 and 35 points. We release the dataset, the scoring pipeline and all per-row provenance.

</details>


### [53] [Learning from Unreliable Operator References: Claim-Level Rewards for Customer Support Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34341)
*Dmitrii Stoianov,Olga Tsymboi,Danil Taranets,Daniil Strizhakov,Ilya Buryak,Almaz Dautov,Dmitry Fatnev,Arthur Babkin,Aleksandr Fida,Daniil Dryabin,Maksim Shilenko,Nikita Surnachev,Anatolii Potapov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将人工客服回复视为待验证的声明而非模仿答案：未验证声明用于修订知识库与工具，已验证声明作为声明级GRPO奖励的覆盖目标，按事实精度打分。离线基准上幻觉标注率8.6%（SFT基线13.6%）；随机A/B测试中无需人工转接的会话占比由63.3%升至80.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即便正确的人工客服回复也不适合作为客服智能体的训练目标：约四分之一的回复事实性论断在知识库与工具记录中缺乏完整支撑。若直接模仿，会把无依据内容当作正确答案学习，导致幻觉。

Method: 把客服回复当作待验证的声明：将声明与知识库、工具记录逐一核对，未验证声明推动知识库与工具的修订；已验证声明作为声明级覆盖目标，构造GRPO奖励，基于智能体检索到的证据对其事实精度评分。

Result: 离线基准上，GRPO智能体8.6%的文本回答被标记为幻觉，监督微调基线为13.6%。与既有流程式机器人进行的随机A/B测试中，无需人工客服即关闭的会话占比从63.3%提升到80.8%。

Conclusion: 以“验证声明”替代“模仿回复”的数据使用范式，配合声明级事实精度奖励，可在降低幻觉的同时显著提升自动解决率，并形成对知识库与工具的反馈闭环。

Abstract: Even a correct operator reply can be an unreliable training target for a customer-support agent. About a quarter of the factual claims in these replies lacked full support in the agent's knowledge base and tool records. We treat operator replies as claims to verify rather than answers to imitate. Unverified claims guide revisions to the knowledge base and tools. Verified claims serve as coverage targets in a claim-level GRPO reward that scores factual precision against the evidence the agent retrieved for its answer. On an offline benchmark, 8.6% of the GRPO agent's text answers were flagged for hallucinations, compared with 13.6% for the supervised fine-tuning baseline. In a randomized A/B test against the existing procedural bot, the deployed system increased the share of conversations closed without a human operator from 63.3% to 80.8%.

</details>


### [54] [Under the Influence -- of What? A Controlled Evaluation of Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34373)
*Johannes Gabriel Sindlinger,Arzu Burcu Güven,Rob van der Goot,Anna Rogers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估LLM预训练场景下影响函数（influence functions）所识别的"有影响力"样本是否真的与模型输出具有相关性质，通过受控数据划分的重训练实验进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影响函数被视为识别哪些训练样本对模型输出负责的有前景方法，但此前在LLM预训练层面的实证评估仅假设其按预期工作，缺乏对"影响力样本"是否真正具备与输出相关属性的系统检验。

Method: 基于受控数据划分的重训练（re-training with controlled data splits），在GPT-2类模型（68M–5.6B训练token）上考察三种主流Hessian近似方法（GradSim、TrackStar、K-FAC），覆盖两类设定（语言现象与事实知识）。

Result: 平均而言，"有影响力"的样本确实相关，但并不总是直接具备目标属性；相关性的比例与类型随设定不同而变化。

Conclusion: 影响函数的有效性在LLM预训练层面是有条件的：其识别结果仅为间接或部分相关，且依赖具体任务设定，因此不宜默认其按理论预期发挥作用。

Abstract: Influence functions are a promising way to identify which training examples are responsible for a model's output. However, so far empirical evaluations on LLM pretraining level only assumed that they work as intended. This is the first work to systematically evaluate whether the 'influential' examples actually have properties relevant to a language model output, based on re-training with controlled data splits. We investigate three prominent Hessian approximation methods (GradSim, TrackStar, K-FAC) in two settings (linguistic phenomena and factual knowledge) in GPT-2 type models (68M-5.6B training tokens). We find that on average, the `influential' examples are indeed relevant, but they do not always bear the target properties directly, and the ratio and kind of relevance varies by setting.

</details>


### [55] [When Skills Fail to Compose: Compatibility and Retention in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34398)
*Yan Lin,Zhongliang Guo,Jingyu Sun,Qiyan Zhao,Ruihong Zeng,Ying Chen,Zhengtao Yao,Yan Wen,Kunyu Peng,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在冻结语言模型上独立学习的软提示技能，单次调用组合准确率最高仅7.6%，而两次调用加显式中间输出可达88.2%–100%；相同操作的技能块并非可互换，修复与排练也存在保留-迁移冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅具备技能获取能力不足以构建可复用技能库：任务熟练度并不规定可组合接口。作者希望面向"自我演化智能体"探讨独立习得的技能能否真正组合，从而把评估重心从单一获取扩展到兼容性、保持与迁移。

Method: 以冻结语言模型上的软提示技能为对象，给定技能身份与执行顺序；在四个数字任务基座与三个记录编辑模型上，对比单次调用与两次调用（加算力并强制显式中间输出）的组合准确率；分析干扰是否可由块长度与逐token范数解释；在Qwen基座上做后验替换实验（用独立学习的同操作技能块替换冻结的已学伙伴）；并考察修复对原技能的损伤、排练对保持的影响，以及内部干预对失败机制的约束。

Result: 单次调用平均组合准确率≤7.6%，两次调用为88.2%–100%；中性插入训练提升鲁棒性但仍残留显著干扰，且块长与逐token范数在两个数字基座上无法解释；替换同操作伙伴后中位组合准确率从98.0%/95.3%降至12.7%/0.5%，而每个替换块单独使用≥90%；修复可改善组合却损害原技能；Qwen2.5-0.5B上排练改善保持但迁移有限；内部干预只能约束解释，未能识别共同失败机制。

Conclusion: 可组合性不是技能获取的副产品：同任务熟练度下技能块存在强干扰与不可互换性。构建自演化智能体技能库时，必须将兼容性、保持与迁移与获取一并评估；显式中间输出（两段式调用）是当前提升组合成功率最直接的手段。

Abstract: Skill acquisition alone does not establish a reusable skill library: task proficiency does not specify a compositional interface. Motivated by agents that acquire and refine skills, we study independently learned soft-prompt skills on frozen language models, with skill identities and execution order supplied. Across four digit-task bases and three record-editing models, mean composition accuracy in one call is at most 7.6%, compared with 88.2% to 100% over two calls that add computation and an explicit intermediate output. Neutral-insertion training improves robustness but leaves substantial interference, which block length and per-token norm alone cannot explain on two of four digit bases. Blocks of the same operation need not be interchangeable: after training next to frozen learned partners, a post hoc test on two Qwen bases shows that replacing those partners with independently learned blocks of the same operations lowers median composition accuracy from 98.0% and 95.3% to 12.7% and 0.5%, although every replacement scores at least 90% alone. Repair can improve a composition while impairing the original skill, and rehearsal improves retention while transfer remains limited on Qwen2.5-0.5B. Internal interventions constrain explanations without identifying a shared failure mechanism. These findings motivate evaluating compatibility, retention and transfer alongside acquisition when building skill libraries for self-evolving agents. Code is available at https://github.com/YanLin-Quinne/SkillCompatibility.

</details>


### [56] [Mitigating Hallucinations in Medical Question Answering on ESC Cardiology Guidelines: Evaluation and Retrieval Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34427)
*Ilkin Masimov,Rufana Baghirli,Muhammad Rashid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对"验证-修订"式幻觉缓解流程进行了逐条主张的人工审核，发现被标记的主张中仅44.7–50.6%确为真实幻觉，验证器可靠性而非生成本身是该流程的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学问答中"验证-修订"流程几乎都把验证器输出当作金标准，这一假设很少被检验；作者希望在指南支撑的临床场景中，用人工判断逐条衡量该流程的真实失效模式。

Method: 构建160题数据集（100个来自89例去标识病例的患者情景题＋60个事实型题），覆盖四部近期ESC心脏病学指南，经执业内科医师审核；检索语料为627条人工核验的指南记录，提供两种切块粒度。五阶段流程在4种检索配置×3次独立运行下执行（共1920次臂运行）；对全部606条首轮被驳斥主张逐条人工审核，由独立评审复判906条决策，并由专家盲审100条被接受的主张。

Result: 被标记主张中仅44.7–50.6%为真实幻觉；修正回路在85.2–95.1%的情形下收敛，但最终真实错误中有35%由修正环节引入。单次运行的真实幻觉召回率为35.6–41.2%，三次运行并集为71–80%；验证器一致性中等（kappa=0.505）。盲测估计被接受主张的漏检率为3.2%（95% CI 0.7–6.7%）。最终真实错误率为答案的1.7%。

Conclusion: 主张级验证可将幻觉内容降低约一个数量级，但限制因素在于验证器可靠性而非生成模型，因此验证器的可靠性必须被测量而不能被默认。

Abstract: Background: Large language models produce hallucinations in medical question answering (QA). Verify-and-revise pipelines that check atomic claims against retrieved guideline evidence are a standard mitigation, but evaluations almost universally treat the verifier's output as ground truth, an assumption rarely tested. Objective: To measure, claim by claim against human judgement, how such a pipeline fails in a guideline-grounded clinical setting. Methods: We built a dataset of 160 questions (100 patient vignettes from 89 de-identified cases, 60 fact-based) over four recent ESC cardiology guidelines, all approved by a practising internist, and a retrieval corpus of 627 hand-verified guideline records at two chunk granularities. A five-stage pipeline was run under four retrieval configurations and three independent runs (1920 arm-runs); all 606 first-pass refuted claims were manually audited, an independent judge re-judged 906 decisions, and the expert blindly reviewed 100 accepted claims. Results: Only 44.7-50.6% of flagged claims were true hallucinations. Correction loops converged in 85.2-95.1% of cases, yet 35% of genuine final errors were introduced by correction. Single-run true-hallucination recall was 35.6-41.2%, the three-run union 71-80%; verifier agreement was moderate (kappa = 0.505). The blind probe estimated the accepted-claim miss rate at 3.2% (95% CI 0.7-6.7%). Final genuine-error rate: 1.7% of answers. Conclusion: Claim-level verification reduces hallucinated content by an order of magnitude, but verifier reliability, not generation, is the limiting factor and must be measured, not assumed.

</details>


### [57] [Where Does the Asymmetry Enter? Staging a Fairness Audit Across a Fused Multimodal Moderation Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34491)
*Kardelen Subasi,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对已发表的TikTok多模态审核分类器进行事后审计，以源语言为子组轴，将显著性检验分别施加于"可读输入""分支编码""最终决策"三个阶段，发现不对称在早期（准入截断、文本分支编码）即显著，而在决策层因最小组样本过少而检测不到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频审核由融合视频、语音与屏显文本的单一分类器统一裁决，其跨语言公平性往往仅由一个总体准确率衡量；而该指标与"误差几乎完全集中于某一语言群体"是相容的。现有审计只读取模型决策，即长流水线的末端、检验样本最少的一环，因而系统性低估子组差异。

Method: 研究的问题是：在融合流水线中，子组不对称可在何处被检出。作者采用事后审计设计，对已发表的TikTok分类器做三阶段检验：模型可读取的输入（准入/截断）、各分支（尤其文本分支）的编码表示、以及最终决策；三阶段统一采用同一显著性标准，仅使用日志中的决策、标签与融合前特征，以源语言作为子组轴。并在准入阶段做机制归因，区分分段（segmentation）与冗长（verbosity）两种解释。

Result: 不对称在"决定模型可读内容"的阶段显著：语言无关的截断上限使越南语片段的准入量少于英语片段，原因在于分段而非冗长。不对称同样在文本分支的编码中显著。而在决策层该不对称不可检出，因为最小组仅剩12个可检验样本。

Conclusion: 仅读取决策的审计恰在最薄弱的证据处取证；将同一检验前移至上游阶段，可恢复出决策层视角判为噪声的差异。跨语言公平性审计应覆盖准入与表示阶段，而非止于输出。

Abstract: Almost every short-video upload now passes through a classifier that fuses video, speech, and on-screen text into one decision about whether it may be seen. It moderates every language community on the platform at once, and fairness across them is judged, if at all, from one accuracy score. That score is compatible with error falling almost entirely on one language group. An audit that goes looking reads the model's decisions, the last stage of a long pipeline and the one whose test has the fewest items. We ask where in a fused pipeline a subgroup asymmetry can be detected at all: in what the classifier may read, in what its branches encode, or in what it decides. We answer this with a post-hoc audit of a published TikTok classifier, holding all three stages to one significance standard using only its logged decisions, labels, and pre-fusion features, with source language as the subgroup axis. The asymmetry is significant at the stage deciding what the model may read, and again in what the text branch encodes. At the earlier stage, a language-blind cap admits less of a Vietnamese clip than an English one, for reasons of segmentation, not verbosity. The asymmetry is undetectable at the decision, where the smallest subgroup leaves twelve items to test. An audit reading only decisions looks where its evidence is thinnest; the same test staged upstream recovers a disparity the decision-level view returns as noise.

</details>


### [58] [FlyingStart: Learned Cross-Token Warm Starts for Fixed-Point Decoding in Recurrent-Depth Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34492)
*Runguo Li,Dezhen Lu,Zixiang Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FlyingStart线性热启动：利用前一个token收敛时的隐状态（经学习坐标变换）作为下一token迭代初始点，在不改变迭代与停止规则前提下减少循环次数、提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环深度语言模型每次生成token都从随机噪声开始迭代至收敛，每个token都要支付完整收敛代价；现有推理方法只关注"何时停止迭代"，而忽略了"从哪里开始迭代"这一独立杠杆。

Method: 基于"token不动点可由上一token停止状态经学习坐标变换预测"的观察，设计FlyingStart：在冻结模型上以闭式解拟合的线性热启动，开销不足一次循环的5%；迭代过程与退出判据不变，因此停止准则从任意起点认证到不动点的同一距离。

Result: 在Huginn-0125上以14–19%更少的解码层数匹配最强免训练基线精度；两个服务引擎中批量吞吐提升16–18%，在自推测解码基础上再叠加10–14%。收缩性分析可预测所研究四个模型的循环节省量（含一次评估前即记录的预测），并在Huginn上逐token成立。

Conclusion: 迭代起点是与停止规则独立的有效优化维度；热启动收益随模型收敛所需循环数增加而增大，为循环深度模型推理加速提供理论可预测、即插即用的方案。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth language models generate each token by iterating a shared block of layers until its hidden state converges. Inference methods for these models decide when to stop the iteration, yet every token still starts from random noise and pays the full cost of converging. We show that where the iteration starts is a second, independent lever. A token's fixed point is predictable from the state at which the previous token stopped, after a learned change of coordinates, and we introduce FlyingStart, a linear warm start that is fitted in closed form on the frozen model and costs under 5% of one loop. The iteration and its stopping rule are unchanged, so the exit criterion certifies the same distance to the fixed point from any start: a better start removes loops at unchanged accuracy. On Huginn-0125, FlyingStart matches the accuracy of the strongest training-free baseline with 14--19% fewer decoding layers, raises batched throughput by 16--18% in two serving engines, and adds a further 10--14% on top of self-speculative decoding. A contraction analysis predicts the number of loops saved on all four models we study, including one where the prediction was recorded before any evaluation, and token by token on Huginn, and it identifies when a warm start pays off: the saving grows with the number of loops a model needs to converge.

</details>


### [59] [AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34529)
*Nikolay Skachkov,Ekaterina Redina,Maksim Konoplev,Evgenii Uskov,Veronika Zykova,Ivan Tsarikov,Artemii Zakharov,Mariia Godunova,Dmitrii Lunin,Maria Nikiforova,Valerii Levanov,Anton Kalsin,Daniil Panteleev,Alexey Gerasev,Ilia Kopytin,Yermek Kapushev,Egor Liakhov,Irina Lialikova,Tatiana Taekina,Anastasia Volotova,Assol Kubaeva,Kirill Alekseev,Pavel Otlivanchik,Mukhammadfirdavs Kosimov,Vladislav Negodin,Ivan Vinogradov,Anton Andriushchenko,Maksim Nikitin,Daniil Sukhoi,Alexander Mazitov,Maksim Abraham,Philip Zmushko,Maksim Ignatov,Ivan Sapozhkov,Timofey Byzov,Edgar Shmavonian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布 AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base：总参80B、激活约3B的MoE基座模型（Apache 2.0），结合Kimi Delta Attention与全注意力、Attention Residuals，并用Muon训练，在零样本推理及俄语事实性基准上超过更大规模的开源基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个从零训练、参数量适中（80B总参/3B激活）但性能可对标更大规模模型的开源基座语言模型，同时补齐俄语事实性知识评测的空白，并研究训练损失作为下游性能代理的局限性。

Method: 架构上采用Kimi Delta Attention与全注意力混合、Attention Residuals，并以Muon优化器训练；训练流程含17.5万亿token通用预训练、两阶段上下文扩展、以及专门的推理与智能体交互阶段；详细描述数据采集与增强流水线、评测协议、架构消融与缩放律实验；提出在MoE输出上增加辅助损失以在预测学习率下稳定训练；采用领域特定数据选择与增强，并在预训练中引入智能体轨迹。

Result: 零样本推理在同类开源基座模型中领先：IMO-AnswerBench pass@8 达88.7%，LiveCodeBench v5-v6 pass@8 达82.9%；发布俄语基准WikiWebFacts（86.5%）与HardMultiQA（67.9%），优于DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Base（83.2% / 65.4%，284B总参/13B激活）和Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-Base（72.8% / 54.5%，120B总参/12B激活）。

Conclusion: held-out训练损失不能可靠代理下游性能；MoE输出辅助损失可提升大学习率下的训练稳定性；领域数据选择与增强提升事实性知识、数学与编程能力，预训练中引入智能体轨迹可在SFT后提升智能体基准表现；小激活量的MoE设计可在多项基准上超过更大模型。

Abstract: We present AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base, a mixture-of-experts language model with 80 billion total parameters and approximately 3 billion active parameters per token, trained from scratch at Yandex and released under the Apache 2.0 license. The model combines Kimi Delta Attention with full attention, uses Attention Residuals, and is trained with Muon. Training comprises general pretraining on 17.5 trillion tokens, two context-extension stages, and a dedicated stage for reasoning and agentic interaction. We describe the data collection and augmentation pipelines, evaluation protocols, architectural ablations, and scaling-law experiments. We identify the limitations of held-out training loss as a proxy for downstream performance and introduce an auxiliary loss on mixture-of-experts outputs to stabilize training at the predicted learning rate. Domain-specific data selection and augmentation improve factual knowledge, mathematics, and programming skills, while agent trajectories introduced during pretraining improve performance on agentic benchmarks after supervised fine-tuning. In zero-shot reasoning evaluation, the model achieves leading results among open base models of comparable size, with 88.7% pass@8 on IMO-AnswerBench and 82.9% pass@8 on LiveCodeBench v5-v6. To evaluate Russian-language factual knowledge, we introduce and release two benchmarks with their evaluation protocols: WikiWebFacts, which tests short factual questions drawn from encyclopedic sources and search queries, and HardMultiQA, which tests more demanding factual questions inspired by user interactions. Our model scores 86.5% on WikiWebFacts and 67.9% on HardMultiQA, compared with 83.2% and 65.4% for DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Base (284B total and 13B active parameters) and 72.8% and 54.5% for Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-Base (120B total and 12B active parameters), outperforming both larger models on these benchmarks.

</details>


### [60] [EPPC-CARE: Staged Hierarchical Annotation of Communication Behaviors in Secure Patient-Provider Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34539)
*Samah Fodeh,Sreeraj Ramachandran,Ganesh Puthiaraju,Sarah Schellhorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出并评估了EPPC-CARE框架，用于从患者门户消息中自动识别沟通行为（文本片段+类别码+子类码三元组），在六个开源大模型上均优于EPPC-OASIS，三元组F1平均提升2.90个百分点（最佳64.4%），且与人类专家标注一致性相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者门户消息蕴含医患沟通动态的重要信息，但人工提取成本高、劳动强度大；同时已有自动化方法（如EPPC-OASIS）难以将分类结果与支撑原文对应，不利于人工核验。

Method: 基于867条消息获得5,516条参考标准标注，每条标注为（文本片段span、沟通类别Code、子类Sub-code）三元组；EPPC-CARE采用顺序识别三元组各组成部分的策略，在六个开源权重LLM上与监督微调及EPPC-OASIS等模型比较，并在100条消息上与人类专家标注对比。

Result: EPPC-CARE在全部六个模型上均优于EPPC-OASIS，三元组F1平均提升2.90个百分点，最高达64.4%；在专家评估子集上，EPPC-CARE与人类专家相对参考标准的一致性相当，差异略偏向EPPC-CARE。

Conclusion: 顺序识别三元组组件可跨模型家族提升性能，并保留每次识别的支撑文本；EPPC-CARE将沟通码/子码与源文本关联，支持临床信息学中自动化标注的人工核验，为更大规模医患沟通研究及人在回路标注流程奠定基础。

Abstract: Objectives: Patient portal messages provide invaluable information about communication dynamics between patients and healthcare providers, but extracting it manually is labor-intensive. We developed and evaluated EPPC-CARE, an automated framework for identifying communication behaviors together with the supporting text for each classification. Materials and Methods: From 867 messages, we obtained 5,516 reference standard annotations. Each annotation is a triplet of communication-related text (span), a broader communication category (Code), and a specific subcategory (Sub-code). We evaluated EPPC-CARE's identified triplets across six open-weight large language models (LLMs) against supervised fine-tuning and communication-based models such as EPPC-OASIS. We also compared EPPC-CARE with human expert annotations on 100 messages. Results: EPPC-CARE improved performance over EPPC-OASIS across all six models. EPPC-CARE's Triplet F1 improved by 2.90 percentage points on average, with a best score of 64.4%. On the human expert evaluation subset, EPPC-CARE and the human expert showed comparable agreement with the reference standard, with differences favoring EPPC-CARE. Discussion: These findings suggest that identifying triplet components (span, code, subcode) sequentially can improve performance across model families while preserving the supporting text behind each identification. EPPC-CARE links the communication code and subcode with the source text (span), supporting human verification of automated annotations in clinical informatics. EPPC-CARE may therefore make larger-scale studies of patient-provider communication more feasible and provide a foundation for human-in-the-loop annotation workflows. Conclusion: EPPC-CARE offers a more reliable and reviewable approach for automated characterization of communication behaviors in secure patient-provider messaging.

</details>


### [61] [Technical Report MEG2text: Beyond Alignment in Non-Invasive Brain-to-Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34686)
*Yiqian Yang,Yiqun Duan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MEG2text非侵入式脑到文本解码系统，通过训练/评测修正、脑-文本对齐、类别平衡词读出与目标词语言模型融合，在LibriBrain100词排序任务上取得64.12%平均Top-10平衡准确率，较MEG-XL提升16.92个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式脑到文本的核心难点在于将学到的脑-语言表征转化为准确的词预测，而既有工作（如MEG-XL）在训练与评测流程、解码规则（读出层）方面可能存在实现缺陷与表征利用不足。

Method: MEG2text系统包含四部分：修正后的训练与评测流程、脑-文本对齐、类别平衡的词级读出分类器，以及目标词语言模型融合；并通过控制变量实验与候选级分析、相同特征对比实验分离各组件贡献。

Result: 在四个语料库上均取得提升，LibriBrain100词排序任务平均Top-10平衡准确率64.12%，较MEG-XL提高16.92个百分点；控制实验显示实现修正与学习式读出均有贡献；候选级分析发现部分词得分具信息量却持续负于竞争候选；相同特征对比表明学习分类规则可在不改变表征的情况下提升解码。

Conclusion: 解码规则是非侵入式脑到文本中的关键环节，系统级改进能更充分利用已对齐的神经特征，未来应同时重视表征学习与解码器设计。

Abstract: Non-invasive brain-to-text seeks to recover language from brain activity without surgery. A central challenge is turning learned brain-language representations into accurate word predictions. We present MEG2text, a decoding system that combines corrected training and evaluation, brain-text alignment, a class-balanced word readout, and target-word language-model fusion. On LibriBrain100's word-ranking task, MEG2text reaches 64.12% mean Top-10 balanced accuracy, improving on published MEG-XL by 16.92 percentage points across four corpora, with gains on each corpus. Controlled comparisons show that both implementation corrections and a learned readout contribute to this improvement. Candidate-level analysis reveals that some words receive informative scores yet consistently lose to competing candidates, while an identical-feature comparison shows that learning the classification rule improves decoding without changing the representation. These findings identify the decoding rule as a consequential part of non-invasive brain-to-text and show how system improvements can make better use of aligned neural features.

</details>


### [62] [PoVi: Agentic Poetry-to-Video Generation with Knowledge Grounding and Figurative Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34690)
*Yuxia Zhu,Shiying Zhang,Lingzhuang Sun,Song Yang,Siheng Wu,Xinglong Xu,Sizhe Qiu,Hao Liang,Cheng Tan,Asher Qin,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 PoVi 基准与智能体框架，用于"有据可依"的诗到视频生成，将诗歌语义重构为有证据边界的表示，并逐级转化为解说、阐释、角色素材与镜头级视觉规划，再进行与解说对齐的视频生成；在三种规划骨干与四种视频生成器上稳定优于单次调用生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诗歌到视频生成不止是文本到视觉场景的翻译：诗性语言高度压缩、依赖历史文化知识、常以隐喻与意象等非字面方式表意，直接调用文生视频模型难以完成语义落地与视听组织。

Method: 构建 PoVi 基准与智能体框架，从语义落地、语篇连贯、视听实现三个维度评测；将诗歌语义重构为证据受限（evidence-bounded）的表示，流水线式生成解说、阐释、角色素材、镜头级视觉方案，最后做与解说对齐的视频生成。

Result: 在三种规划骨干网络与四种视频生成器组合下，PoVi 一致优于单次调用生成；增益最大的是隐含背景知识与隐喻关系两类情形。

Conclusion: PoVi 为超越直接文生视频合成的"知识落地、长时程"多模态生成提供了可研究的测试平台。

Abstract: Poetry-to-video generation requires more than translating text into visual scenes. Poetic language is highly compressed, often depends on historical and cultural knowledge, and frequently conveys meaning through metaphor, imagery, and other non-literal expressions. We introduce PoVi, a benchmark and agentic framework for grounded poetry-to-video generation, evaluating semantic grounding, discourse coherence, and audiovisual realization. PoVi reconstructs poem semantics into an evidence-bounded representation and progressively transforms them into narration, interpretation, character assets, and shot-level visual plans, followed by narration-aligned video generation. Across three planning backbones and four video generators, PoVi consistently improves over single-call generation, with the largest gains on implicit background knowledge and metaphorical relations. PoVi provides a testbed for studying knowledge-grounded, long-horizon multimodal generation beyond direct text-to-video synthesis.

</details>


### [63] [Bigger Model or Bigger Table? How Much Backbone a Chorus N-gram Module Replaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34725)
*Szymon Kocur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在1.34B token的1918年前波兰语语料上，Chorus N-gram模块（哈希bigram/trigram表）以约等于主干大小的表规模，最多只等价于1.34倍（小模型）至1.16–1.20倍（大模型）的同损失普通Transformer，每增加一个参数最多替代0.31个主干参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化Chorus N-gram模块究竟能替代多少Transformer主干参数，即"参数替换比"，而非仅报告困惑度提升。

Method: 将token bigram与trigram的哈希表加入输入和注意力；在1.34B token的1918年前波兰语印刷文本上训练16M–349M参数模型；对每类模型单独做学习率搜索；以相同held-out loss反推等价的普通模型规模；表规模设为与主干相当；另设事实测验对比。

Result: 逐模型学习率搜索使47M处的Chorus增益缩小约三分之一；表与主干同规模时，16M处等价于1.34倍普通模型，107M–349M处等价1.16–1.20倍；每新增参数最多替代0.31个主干参数；训练速度慢于同规模普通模型、快于同损失普通模型；事实测验上两者差异与同模型两个随机种子间差异相当。

Conclusion: Chorus的收益可通过调参显著缩水，参数替代效率有限（≤0.31），且结论仅基于单一语料、8000词表和五分之一个epoch，外推需谨慎。

Abstract: We ask how much of a transformer's backbone the Chorus N-gram module replaces. Chorus adds hashed tables of token bigrams and trigrams to its input and attention. We train models of 16M to 349M parameters on 1.34 billion tokens of Polish printed before 1918 and find the size of a plain model with the same held-out loss. A separate learning-rate search for each kind of model cuts Chorus's gain at 47M by about a third. With tables about as large as the backbone, Chorus then matches a plain model 1.34 times its size at 16M and 1.16 to 1.20 times from 107M to 349M. With these tables, each added parameter replaces at most 0.31 backbone parameters. Chorus trains slower than a plain model of its size and faster than one of the same loss. On a fact quiz the two differ about as much as two seeds of one model. The results rest on one corpus, one 8,000-token vocabulary and a fifth of an epoch.

</details>


### [64] [Collect for Coverage, Replay with Evidence: Adaptive and Efficient On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34756)
*Haowen Hou,Gen Li,Ruilin Li,Yicheng Liu,Zhen Huang,Shuai Dong,Congcong Wang,Kaiwen Tuo,Hongrui Wu,Zelin Peng,Kanghui Tian,Ji Zhao,Dianyi Wang,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对长程智能体在线策略蒸馏成本高的问题，提出CARE-OPD，通过"学生种子化教师补全+置信度门控"扩大历史覆盖并过滤不可靠更新，再用"事后重放分配"依据下游执行证据筛选关键决策点，在长程任务上优于在线OPD与已有前缀重放基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在长程智能体上代价高昂，每次更新都依赖新环境 rollout，长尾轨迹造成大量空转；前缀重放虽可通过复用存储历史提高效率，但存在两点局限：(1) 轨迹收集阶段，纯教师轨迹缺少学生诱导的历史，而扩大历史覆盖又会降低教师可靠性；(2) 轨迹复用阶段，基于固定位置的重放调度无法识别可能出现在任意轨迹深度的关键决策。

Method: 提出CARE-OPD（Collect for coverAge, Replay with Evidence）：(1) 采用学生种子化（student-seeded）的教师补全，并由响应级置信度门控过滤不可靠更新，以扩大历史覆盖同时保证教师可靠性；(2) 采用事后（hindsight）重放分配，利用下游执行证据选择具有实际影响的回合进行重放。

Result: 在软件工程与深度研究等长程任务上，CARE-OPD优于在线OPD及先前的前缀重放基线；在SWE-bench Verified上任务成功率达59.5%，相对异步在线OPD端到端训练时间减少45%，环境动作数减少44%。

Conclusion: CARE-OPD通过"覆盖导向的收集 + 证据驱动的重放"同时缓解前缀重放在长程场景下的收集与复用缺陷，在提升长程任务成功率的同时显著降低训练时间与环境交互开销，为长程智能体的高效策略蒸馏提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fully online on-policy distillation (OPD) is costly for long-horizon agents because each update depends on fresh environment rollouts. Prefix replay offers an efficient alternative by sampling current-student responses from stored histories, reducing idle time for long-tail rollouts and allowing each collected trajectory to support multiple updates. However, our analysis of the complete prefix replay pipeline identifies two limitations in long-horizon settings. In trajectory collection, teacher-only trajectories miss student-induced histories, while broader history coverage can reduce teacher reliability. In trajectory reuse, fixed position-based schedules cannot identify critical decisions that may occur at any trajectory depth. We introduce CARE-OPD (Collect for coverAge, Replay with Evidence) to address both limitations. Student-seeded teacher completions and response-level confidence gating expand history coverage while filtering unreliable updates. Hindsight replay allocation then uses downstream execution evidence to select consequential turns. Across long-horizon tasks Software Engineering and Deep Research, CARE-OPD outperforms online OPD and prior prefix replay baselines. On SWE-bench Verified, it raises task success to 59.5% while reducing end-to-end training time by 45% and environment actions by 44% relative to asynchronous online OPD.

</details>


### [65] [DFly2: Turbocharging Long-Context Speculative Decoding by Sharing the Target's Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34776)
*Hong Liu,Rui Cen,Junhan Shi,Lin Niu,Runzhi Fan,Zhaojing Zhou,Haodi Lei,Jiebin Zhang,Saiyong Yang,Junqiang Zheng,Zhenghai Xue,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DFly2：一种块并行的投机解码草稿模型，通过共享目标模型的KV缓存与索引器选出的稀疏位置，自身不做打分、不维护缓存，在高并发长上下文场景显著提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生稀疏目标模型（如DeepSeek-V4、Hy4）已用稠密注意力的二次代价换取稀疏注意力，但投机解码的草稿模型仍自建全提示或滑动窗口的上下文缓存，额外带来显存、prefill时间、前缀缓存命中率与解码并发的开销。读取目标缓存的草稿模型虽省去自有缓存，却只解决"用哪个缓存"而非"用哪些位置"，仍读取全部或近邻窗口，且非按当前query定制；而逐query在草稿端计算位置选择，代价随上下文增长。作者观察到：原生稀疏的目标模型本就为每个query在它已维护的缓存上选好位置，草稿模型的两个决策（缓存与选择）其实已被目标模型做完并付过账。

Method: 先做实证测量：在DS-V4.1-Flash上，把目标模型一步之前做出的选择直接用于下一步，仍覆盖后续每个token所选位置的近九成，说明选择具有一定跨步可迁移性。据此提出DFly2：块并行草稿结构，每个草稿层读取其配对目标层缓存的KV（KV sharing），并只在这些层索引器所选的固定数量、可位于上下文中任意位置的稀疏位置读取（Index sharing）；因此草稿端不运行打分pass，也不持有任何自有缓存。在DS-V4.1-Flash、GLM-5.3-Flash、Hy4-flash三种稀疏注意力与架构各异的模型上，与同一训练配方下的DFlash、DSpark对比。

Result: 在通用、长上下文与agentic基准上，DFly2每步接受token数均高于DFlash与DSpark。相对较强基线的领先幅度从通用负载的3.9%–10.5%，扩大到长上下文与agentic负载的10.0%–31.5%。在DS-V4.1-Flash高并发下、每用户解码速度相当时，两个64K数据集上吞吐分别提升62%与81%。

Conclusion: 草稿模型的缓存与位置选择可由稀疏目标模型"顺带"提供，且跨步仍有效；这种零额外缓存、零打分开销的共享设计在架构不同的多个稀疏模型上通用，且收益在长上下文、agentic与高并发场景更为突出。

Abstract: To avoid the quadratic cost of dense attention over long contexts, models such as DeepSeek-V4 and Hy4 have turned natively sparse, and speculative decoding, which matters most where decoding is slowest, increasingly runs on them. Their drafters, however, still keep a context cache of their own, over the whole prompt or a sliding window. Beyond memory, such a cache costs prefill time, prefix-cache hits and decode concurrency. Drafters that read the target's cache avoid one of their own, but settle only which cache, not which positions, and still read everything or a recent window. Neither rule is made for the query at hand, and computing a selection per query in the drafter would cost more as the context grows. Yet a natively sparse target already picks positions for each query, over a cache it keeps anyway. Both of the drafter's choices are therefore already made, and paid for, by the target: its cache and its selection. We measure how far one selection carries: made a step early, it still covers close to nine tenths of what each later token selects on DS-V4.1-Flash. We present DFly2, a block-parallel drafter that shares the target's sparsity. Each draft layer reads the KV its paired target layer cached (KV sharing), at the positions that layer's indexer selected, a fixed number anywhere in the context (Index sharing). DFly2 thus runs no scoring pass and keeps no cache of its own. On DS-V4.1-Flash, GLM-5.3-Flash and Hy4~flash, whose sparse attention and architecture differ, it accepts more tokens per step than DFlash and DSpark trained on the same recipe, over general, long-context and agentic benchmarks. Its margin over the stronger baseline widens from 3.9%-10.5% on general workloads to 10.0%-31.5% on long-context and agentic ones. At high concurrency on DS-V4.1-Flash, at matched per-user decode speed, its throughput is 62% and 81% higher on two 64K sets.

</details>


### [66] [PersonaFactory: Stylistic User Prompt Augmentation with Synthetic Persona Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34785)
*Bich Ngoc Doan,Hanna Yukhymenko,Imanol Schlag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PersonaFactory，一个端到端管线：生成详细用户人设（persona），并以其视角改写已有用户提示，构建了含 74 万+结构化合成人设、覆盖 85 种语言、约 124 万条人设风格合成对话的资源库，并验证了其在风格可控性、意图保持与下游助手人设控制上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在 AI 开发流水线中日益替代真实用户（规模化生成训练对话、驱动对齐与评测），但现成模型合成的用户提示呈单语化、风格扁平化，无法复现真实的人-AI 交流机制，导致合成数据缺乏用户多样性。

Method: 构建 PersonaFactory 端到端管线：先生成细粒度用户人设（背景与交流行为等结构化属性），再以人设视角对现有用户提示进行风格化增广；采用基于属性的结构化风格表示，并用 LLM-judge 与启发式指标评估增广保真度、意图保持及训练数据对助手人设控制的下游影响，辅以人类专家评估。

Result: 结构化、基于属性的风格表示相较现有自由文本人设描述，在相近风格保真度下实现逐维度（per-axis）控制与更好的意图保持；专家评估认为增广对话可信，意图保持、风格自然度与多语真实性评分良好；在能力基准上与原始训练数据相当，在同等回复长度下于真实用户提示上更受偏好，且无需显式引导即可更贴近用户回复风格。

Conclusion: PersonaFactory 表明结构化人设驱动的提示增广可规模化生成多语言、风格多样的合成用户对话，兼顾可控性、忠实度与下游实用性，为以合成用户替代真实用户的数据构建提供可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly replace real people in the AI development pipeline, as they are used to generate training dialogues at scale and to drive alignment and evaluation. However, out-of-the-box models produce monolingual and stylistically flat texts as synthetic user prompts, failing to replicate the real user-AI communication mechanics. We present PersonaFactory, an end-to-end pipeline that generates detailed user personas and uses them to augment existing user prompts from their point of view, producing a bank of over 740000 structured synthetic personas detailing their background and communication behaviors, and a corpus of roughly 1.24 million persona-styled synthetic conversations spanning 85 languages. We evaluate the effect of personal features on style adoption and intent preservation, using an LLM-judge and heuristic metrics to assess augmentation fidelity together with the downstream impact on assistant persona control through training data. Results show that our structured, attribute-based style representation enables per-axis control and better intent preservation at comparable style fidelity than free-text persona descriptions used in existing methods. A human expert evaluation found the augmented conversations plausible, with favorable ratings for intent preservation, style naturalness, and multilingual authenticity. Finally, PersonaFactory conversations achieve comparable performance to original training data on capability benchmarks, are preferred on real user prompts at equal reply length, and adapt their reply style more closely to users without explicit steering.

</details>


### [67] [VulnSafe: Measuring Vulnerability-Aware Relational Safety in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34819)
*Jiaming Luo,Jiacheng Ji,Haoan Jin,Xiaoning Sun,Qiuzhi Xiong,Yuxin Zhang,Hanhui Xu,Mengyue Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出中文能力评测基准 VulnSafe，以"脆弱性感知回应能力"（VARC）五维框架评估大模型的关系性安全，而非仅测拒答合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感支持型交互要求模型不仅避免明显有害内容，还要识别脆弱性相关风险、调节关系姿态、维护用户自主性并给出情境适配支持；现有安全基准多测量孤立违规，其分数代表何种底层回应能力并不清晰。

Method: 采用证据中心设计，构建 VARC 框架，将关系性安全分为风险感知、安全保持、自主性保护、支持性回应、边界管理五个构念；用 41 个情境、2,050 条均衡提示及情境化评分细则诱发构念相关行为，并区分主动"应为"行为与禁止性"不应为"行为。

Result: 在八个代表性大模型上得到各具差异的能力画像，且主动"应为"维度的模型间差距大于禁止性要求维度。

Conclusion: VulnSafe 使关系性安全可解释为多维模型能力，而非仅等同于拒答合规。

Abstract: Emotionally supportive interactions require large language models not only to avoid overtly harmful content, but also to recognize vulnerability-related risk, regulate their relational stance, preserve user agency, and provide context-appropriate support. Existing safety benchmarks often measure isolated violations, leaving unclear what underlying response competence their scores represent. We introduce VulnSafe, a Chinese capability assessment centered on Vulnerability-Aware Response Competence (VARC). VARC organizes relational safety into five complementary constructs: Risk Perception, Safety Preservation, Autonomy Protection, Supportive Response, and Boundary Management. Following an evidence-centered design, 41 scenarios elicit construct-relevant behavior through 2,050 balanced prompts and scenario-specific scoring rubrics. The assessment further separates proactive should-do behavior from prohibitive should-not behavior. Across eight representative LLMs, results show distinct competence profiles and larger disparities than prohibitive requirements. VulnSafe therefore makes relational safety interpretable as a multidimensional model capability rather than as refusal compliance alone.

</details>


### [68] [The Linguistic Knowledge Benchmark: Do Language Models Know the Foundations of Early-Grade Literacy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34823)
*Maxime Lelièvre,Alasdair Mackintosh,Dominic Andrews,Tobias Murray,Moritz Boos,Oliver G. B. Garrod*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Linguistic Knowledge Benchmark及一套可复现、高度自动化的构建流水线，将课程文件、教师指南和学生课本转化为九大语言领域×七个年级固定网格上的审校题库，并在斯瓦希里语和英语上实例化以评测121/119个模型。结果显示前沿模型准确率超90%，但小型、低成本及移动端可部署模型准确率骤降，斯瓦希里语尤甚，说明最适合低资源课堂的模型恰在其服务语言上能力最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中低收入国家（LMICs）的教学语言在语言模型训练数据中代表性不足；在模型能支持这些语言的低年级读写教学之前，需先检验其是否具备读写教学所依赖的各类语言学知识，而此前缺乏针对性与可扩展的评测手段。

Method: 构建Linguistic Knowledge Benchmark及可复现、大部分自动化的流水线：整合LLM驱动的来源调研、特征归并、题目生成与质量控制，加入受项目反应理论（IRT）启发的题目筛选步骤，并经最终专家审校；题库组织为九个语言领域×七个年级的固定网格。实例化于斯瓦希里语（肯尼亚，135题）与英语（英国，137题），分别评测121与119个模型。

Result: 前沿模型两种语言准确率均超90%；小型廉价模型准确率陡降且斯瓦希里语更明显。参数量低于100亿的最佳两个模型在英语达91.7%和73.0%，斯瓦希里语仅51.9%和40.7%；可在手机/平板硬件部署的模型平均准确率英语47%、斯瓦希里语24%。

Conclusion: 对低资源课堂最实用的模型，恰在其应服务的语言上能力最弱。作者发布每语言20题样本、全部流水线提示词、开源代码与公开排行榜，作为"测量与生成可解码文本以支持基础读写"的三级评测阶梯的第一级。

Abstract: Many languages of instruction in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) are poorly represented in the data used to train language models. Before these models can support early-grade literacy in such languages, we need to know whether they hold the linguistic knowledge of each language that literacy teaching relies on. To test this, we present the Linguistic Knowledge Benchmark and the reproducible, largely automated pipeline that turns curriculum documents, teacher guides and pupil books into a vetted question set organised on a fixed grid of nine linguistic domains and seven grade levels. The pipeline combines LLM-driven source survey, feature consolidation, item generation and quality control with an item-selection step inspired by item response theory and a final expert review. We instantiate it for Kiswahili (Kenya; 135 items) and English (United Kingdom; 137 items) and evaluate 121 and 119 models respectively. Frontier models exceed 90% accuracy in both languages, but accuracy falls steeply for smaller and cheaper models, and more so in Kiswahili. The two best models under 10 billion parameters reach 91.7% and 73.0% in English but only 51.9% and 40.7% in Kiswahili, and the mean accuracy of models deployable on mobile and tablet hardware is 47% in English against 24% in Kiswahili. The models most practical for low-resource classrooms are therefore the least capable in the languages they would serve. We release a 20-item sample per language, all pipeline prompts, open-source code, and a public leaderboard. This is the first rung of a three-level evaluation ladder for measuring and generating decodable text to support foundational literacy.

</details>


### [69] [Repairing the Trustworthiness of Distilled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34831)
*Kumar Shubham,Devendra Vyas,Vidushi Agarwal,Jayin Khanna,Prathosh AP*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于近端Bregman响应函数（PBRF）的定向修复框架，用代理数据集估计曲率以摆脱对原始蒸馏语料的依赖，并通过odds-ratio目标扩展到偏好监督，在保持下游性能的同时提升蒸馏模型的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏虽可用小模型部署大模型，但压缩过程会在保持下游性能的情况下引入可信度回退；修复困难在于原始蒸馏语料常不可得，而直接偏好优化（DPO）可能改变从教师模型继承的能力。

Method: 以近端Bregman响应函数（PBRF）为核心构建定向修复框架：借助代理/替代数据集估计PBRF所需的曲率，从而去除对原始迁移语料的依赖；并将框架通过odds-ratio目标扩展至基于偏好的监督，使其可利用偏好/非偏好回答对进行优化，同时对蒸馏模型的改动施加约束。

Result: 在预训练与后训练模型上均改善了定向可信度行为，且基本保持下游性能；跨不同蒸馏模型，可信度回退最多可改善8%，困惑度下降不足1%。

Conclusion: 该方法为重新蒸馏或无约束偏好优化提供了一种实用替代方案，可在无原始蒸馏语料的条件下实现定向可信度修复并保持模型能力。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation enables the deployment of large language models through smaller student models, but the compression process can introduce regressions in trustworthiness even when downstream performance is preserved. Repairing such failures is challenging because the original distillation corpus is often unavailable, while direct preference optimization may alter capabilities inherited from the teacher. We propose a targeted repair framework based on the proximal Bregman response function (PBRF) that addresses both limitations. We estimate the curvature required for PBRF using a surrogate proxy dataset, removing dependence on the original transfer corpus, and extend the framework to preference-based supervision through an odds-ratio objective. This enables the use of preferred and dispreferred response pairs while constraining changes to the distilled model. Our results show that the proposed approach improves targeted trustworthiness behaviors for both pre-training and post-training models while largely preserving downstream performance, providing a practical alternative to re-distillation or unconstrained preference optimization. Our experiment results show that across different distilled models, the trustworthy regression can be improved by up to 8% with less than 1% decrement in perplexity.

</details>


### [70] [Proverbs As A Window On Collective Prescriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34832)
*Tomás Lejarraga,Dirk U. Wulff,Ralph Hertwig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出计算框架，用大语言模型分析近5万条英语谚语，量化其主题分布与规范劝诫倾向，发现谚语多教导可控领域中的审慎、诚实、长期取向，而在真正权衡的维度上给出相互矛盾的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谚语被视为传统智慧的载体，长期影响人类推理，但其集体编码的规范性建议一直缺乏大规模量化刻画，缺少对"文化谚语智慧"整体图景的实证描绘。

Method: 构建计算分析框架，处理约50,000条英语谚语；借助大语言模型并以其与人类判断的一致性进行验证，映射谚语的主题景观、评估其规范性与劝诫强度，并在人类判断与行为的关键维度上量化其推荐方向。

Result: 多数谚语具有规范性，集中在个体可控领域（如准备、审慎行动）；涉及命运、死亡等不可控情境的谚语多为描述而非劝诫。规范性谚语立场一致时，倾向谨慎胜过冒险、长期胜过短期、诚实胜过欺骗、友善胜过恶意，并中等程度偏向怀疑而非信任；在真正的权衡维度（尤其自主与服从）上则给出相互矛盾的建议。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了文化谚语智慧中集体规范建议的定量图谱，揭示出一个以审慎、诚实、前瞻为核心的指导内核，并在情境决定最佳选择之处呈现出多样化建议。

Abstract: Proverbs, as repositories of traditional wisdom, have informed human reasoning for millennia. Yet the collective prescriptions they encode have remained largely unquantified. We introduce a computational framework to analyze nearly 50,000 English-language proverbs. Using large language models, validated against human judgments, we map the topical landscape of proverbs, assess their prescriptive power, and quantify their recommendations on key dimensions of human judgment and behavior. We find that most proverbs are prescriptive, offering guidance in domains under individual control, such as preparation and prudent action, whereas proverbs about uncontrollable circumstances, such as fate and mortality, tend to describe rather than prescribe. When prescriptive proverbs take a consistent stance, they favor caution over risk, long-term over short-term outcomes, honesty over deception, and kindness over malice, and show a moderate tendency toward suspicion over trust. On dimensions involving genuine trade-offs, especially autonomy versus compliance, proverbs provide contradicting advice rather than a single recommendation. This analysis provides a quantitative map of the collective prescriptions in a culture's proverbial wisdom, revealing a core of prudent, honest, and forward-looking guidance alongside varied advice where circumstances dictate the best course of action.

</details>


### [71] [DRBench Pro: A Knowledge-Centric Benchmark for Professional Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34852)
*Han Huang,Yuchen Yan,Xingyu Wu,Yijun Li,Chao Deng,Jie Liu,Jin Ma,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出 DRBench Pro，一个以结构化知识覆盖为核心评测目标的深度研究智能体基准。它从维基百科构建本地知识树，用采样子图生成研究查询与基于证据的评分量表（rubric），每条 rubric 对应知识树中的一个关系，从而同时度量研究的广度（主要研究方向）与深度（相关知识链），二者几何平均构成整体覆盖率。基准含 24 个领域、144 个案例、44,102 条 rubric；评测 16 个智能体，最佳整体覆盖率为 57.04%，自动判定与人工标注高度一致，且智能体排名在不同指标配置下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准虽在多个维度上评估报告质量，但缺乏以知识为中心的评价：即报告覆盖了哪些研究方向、以及对这些方向探究到何种深度。为此需要把结构化知识覆盖作为首要评测目标。

Method: 构建自动化流程：从维基百科抽取本地知识树，采样子图生成研究查询与基于证据的评分量表；每条 rubric 与知识树中的一条关系对应，保留被评估知识的结构。智能体仅获得研究查询。基于该结构度量主要研究方向上的广度与相关知识链上的深度，并以两者的几何平均计算整体覆盖率。

Result: 基准覆盖 24 个领域、144 个案例、44,102 条 rubric。对 16 个深度研究智能体的评测显示，各智能体在广度与深度上优势各异，表现最佳者整体覆盖率达 57.04%；rubric 判定与人工标注高度一致，智能体排名在测试的多种指标配置下保持稳定。

Conclusion: DRBench Pro 将结构化知识覆盖确立为深度研究智能体的核心评测维度，可分解诊断广度与深度能力；其评测具有较好的人机一致性与指标稳健性，为后续研究提供了可复用的基准框架。

Abstract: Deep research agents gather information from multiple sources and synthesize it into reports on complex, open-ended questions. Existing benchmarks assess report quality along several dimensions, but lack a dedicated knowledge-centric evaluation of which research directions a report covers and how deeply it explores them. We introduce DRBench Pro, a benchmark that makes structured knowledge coverage the primary evaluation target. Our automated pipeline builds local knowledge trees from Wikipedia and uses sampled subgraphs to generate research queries and evidence-grounded rubrics. Each rubric corresponds to a relation in the tree, preserving the structure of the knowledge being evaluated. Agents receive only the research query. This structure allows us to measure breadth across major research directions and depth along related knowledge chains, with their geometric mean giving overall coverage. The benchmark includes 144 cases across 24 domains and 44,102 rubrics. Evaluating 16 deep research agents reveals different strengths in breadth and depth, with the best-performing agent reaching 57.04% overall coverage. Rubric judgments agree closely with human annotations, and agent rankings remain stable across the tested metric configurations.

</details>


### [72] [$μ$-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-User Task Completion in Shared Interaction Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34920)
*Qihao Lin,Guanxu Chen,Shuhang Zheng,Xiaoya Lu,Jingyi Yang,Terumi Chiba,Wenyi Yu,Chao Zhang,Zhuan Shi,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 μ-Bench，一个面向共享空间中多用户语音智能体的闭环全双工联合任务评测基准，包含 20 个领域、100 个场景、800 个用例，并以联合任务成功率（JTS）及授权/线程/协调（ATC）诊断定位失败原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享空间中的语音智能体需同时满足多用户相互依赖的请求，并维护每个用户的权限、隐私与任务约束；但现有评测未覆盖参与者在会话过程中实时反应的情形，覆盖面有限。

Method: 采用闭环、全双工多用户语音交互设置：模拟参与者基于相互隔离的目标、知识与权限档案行动，并对智能体语音及工具执行结果实时适应；在 20 个领域构建 100 个场景，变化群体规模、声学条件与追问需求，形成 800 个用例（408 含工具、392 无工具）。评测指标为 Joint Task Success（要求所有用户目标、隐私边界与终止状态同时成立），Pure JTS 进一步禁止未授权工具调用；ATC 诊断用于定位失败环节。

Result: 在 13 个语音智能体上，闭源系统在无工具与含工具条件下成功率峰值分别为 62.24% 与 65.44%，开源权重模型均低于 20%。ATC 诊断显示开源模型主要落后于多轮归属（attribution）与线程化（threading）；控制对比表明群体规模增大时，即使能力较强的系统其联合任务成功率也持续下降。

Conclusion: 通过将反应式多用户模拟与可验证的会话级结果相结合，μ-Bench 为共享语音智能体提供了严格的测试平台。

Abstract: Voice agents in shared spaces must satisfy interdependent multi-user requests while upholding per-user authority, privacy, and task constraints. Existing evaluations offer limited coverage when participants react throughout a session. We introduce $μ$-Bench, a benchmark for joint task completion in closed-loop, full-duplex multi-user voice interaction. Simulated participants act from isolated profiles of goals, knowledge, and permissions, adapting to the agent's speech and tool outcomes. Across 20 domains, 100 scenarios vary group size, acoustics, and follow-up demands to form 800 cases (408 tool-augmented, 392 tool-free). Joint Task Success (JTS) requires all user goals, privacy boundaries, and terminal states to hold jointly, while Pure JTS further disallows unauthorized tool calls; Attribution, Threading, and Coordination (ATC) diagnostics localize failures. Across 13 voice agents, proprietary systems peak at 62.24\% tool-free and 65.44\% tool-augmented success, while open-weight models remain below 20\%. ATC diagnostics show that open-weight models lag primarily in multi-turn attribution and threading, and controlled contrasts reveal that larger groups consistently degrade joint task success across capable systems. By coupling reactive multi-user simulation with verifiable session-level outcomes, $μ$-Bench provides a rigorous testbed for shared voice agents.

</details>


### [73] [Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces for Hard Math Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34928)
*Lukas Aichberger,Adam Golinski,Arno Blaas,Arwen Bradley,Jacob Springer,Mehrdad Farajtabar,Michael Kirchhof,Sinead Williamson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DART轨迹挖掘方法：从被拒绝的推理轨迹出发，定位高不确定性步骤并生成多样化的替代推理路径，从而在同等生成轨迹数量下比MC采样多恢复37%的难题，并产出更有效的后训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖"最终答案通过验证器"的接受轨迹进行数学推理后训练，在难题上独立蒙特卡洛采样命中率极低，接受轨迹稀缺，导致难题覆盖不足、后训练数据受限。

Method: DART将被拒绝的轨迹分解为推理步骤，按位置加权不确定性排序；对高排名步骤，借助参考答案引导（reference-answer guidance）和元思考（meta-thought）生成刻意差异化的替代候选，并补全生成完整轨迹。

Result: 在贪心解码未解出的DeepMath-103K问题上，匹配生成轨迹数量时DART比MC采样多恢复37%的问题；每恢复问题的估计生成成本几乎相同，而每条接受轨迹成本低30%。用DART挖掘的轨迹后训练Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct，在MATH-500与AIME共六项评测中有五项取得最高点估计。

Conclusion: 重新利用被拒绝轨迹可扩展问题覆盖并提升后训练数据质量，DART是一条成本有效的轨迹挖掘与数据构造路径。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) for mathematical reasoning often relies on accepted traces whose final answers pass a verifier. For hard math problems, however, such traces can be rare under independent Monte Carlo (MC) sampling. To address this, we propose Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces (DART), a trace-mining method that starts from a rejected trace, decomposes it into reasoning steps, and ranks them by position-weighted uncertainty. It then generates deliberately distinct alternative candidates for highly ranked steps using reference-answer guidance and a meta-thought before completing the resulting traces. On greedy-unsolved DeepMath-103K problems, DART recovers 37% more problems than MC sampling at a matched number of generated traces. Its estimated generation cost per recovered problem is nearly identical, while its cost per accepted trace is 30% lower. Post-training Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on traces mined by DART achieves the highest point estimate in five of six evaluations across MATH-500 and AIME benchmarks. These results demonstrate that revisiting rejected traces with DART can expand problem coverage and provide effective post-training data.

</details>


### [74] [Tokenisation is not in APX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34938)
*Violeta Kastreva,Philip Whittington,Dennis Komm,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文证明：除非 P=NP，不存在多项式时间算法能得到与最优解相差常数因子内的分词器（tokeniser），即分词问题不属于 APX 类；且在一元字母表上 direct 分词仍是 APX-难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词器（tokenisation）在实践中广泛使用，但其优化目标（压缩率或 unigram 对数似然）的理论可近似性此前缺乏系统的复杂性刻画，作者希望明确该问题在近似复杂性层级中的位置。

Method: 通过复杂性理论中的归约（reduction）方法，将 NP-难问题归约到分词问题的近似判定版本，分情形覆盖 direct 与 bottom-up 两类分词方式、压缩与 unigram 对数似然两类目标函数、以及二元及以上字母表与一元字母表两类输入。

Result: 在 direct 与 bottom-up 分词、压缩目标与 unigram 对数似然目标、二元及更大字母表下，均证明不存在常数因子近似多项式算法（非 APX 类）；在一元字母表下，direct 分词为 APX-难，因而不存在多项式时间近似方案（PTAS）。

Conclusion: 分词问题的近似复杂性极差：一般情况下连常数近似都不可得，一元字母表特例亦无 PTAS，这为实践中启发式分词算法的使用提供了理论解释，并划定了可期望算法性能的理论边界。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that, unless P=NP, no polynomial-time algorithm can find a tokeniser whose objective is within any constant factor of the optimum, meaning that tokenisation is not in the APX complexity class. We prove this for direct and bottom-up tokenisation, under both a compression or unigram log-likelihood objectives, and for binary or larger alphabets. We further show that, even over unary alphabets, direct tokenisation is APX-hard, and thus admits no polynomial-time approximation scheme.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [Context Scaling: A New Frontier of AI Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33160)
*Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"上下文缩放"（context scaling）概念，指出AI部署失败多源于决策所需状态与可用上下文之间的错配，并主张以"模型—基础设施"对为分析单元，用三类预算框架组织研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署AI的许多失败并非源于推理能力弱，而是任务所依赖的状态（用户状态、代码版本、工具轨迹、传感器读数、环境变化）在系统行动时不可得、失真或选择不当；现有研究偏重训练缩放与决策时缩放，缺少针对"决策前系统能提供什么"的系统性投入视角。

Method: 将模型表述为受约束的P_θ(d|c)，在训练预算B_θ、决策时预算B_d、上下文基础设施预算B_c三预算框架下界定context scaling；区分部署状态与模型可见上下文，提出"有效上下文价值"这一任务与干预依赖的归因度量，归纳可用性、保真度、选择、利用四类失败；以五操作上下文生命周期组织现有机制，并提出受控预算干预与效用—成本曲线作为评估方法。

Result: 给出三预算交互的概念框架，明确仅B_c作用于决策前可提供的信息；建立失败类型学与五阶段生命周期映射；提出可控干预实验设计与utility-cost曲线评估范式，使上下文投入可与训练、推理投入在同一效用尺度下比较。

Conclusion: 未来议程应聚焦三点：基于落地实证的报告规范、交互数据与全成本的基础设施建设、以及面向持久自适应系统的安全与治理；核心结论是应将模型与上下文基础设施作为整体单元来设计与评估。

Abstract: Many failures of deployed AI arise not from weak inference but from a mismatch between the state a task depends on and the context available when the system acts. Relevant user state, repository revisions, tool traces, sensor readings, or environment changes may be unavailable, distorted, or poorly selected. We call the effort to reduce this bottleneck context scaling. We frame a model as $P_θ(d\mid c)$ within a system governed by three budgets: training $B_θ$, decision-time $B_d$, and context-infrastructure $B_c$. Pretraining scaling invests in $θ$; decision-time scaling searches, refines, or verifies decisions under supplied context; context scaling improves the system's capacity to acquire, represent, deliver, evaluate, and persist deployment state for model use. The three programs interact, but only $B_c$ targets what the system can make available before a decision. This view makes the model-infrastructure pair the unit of analysis. We define effective-context value as a task- and intervention-dependent attribution, separate deployment state from model-visible context, and identify availability, fidelity, selection, and utilization failures. We then organize current mechanisms through a five-operation context lifecycle and propose controlled budget interventions and utility-cost curves for evaluation. The resulting agenda centers on grounded reporting, interaction-data and full-cost infrastructure, and safety and governance for persistent, adaptive systems.

</details>


### [76] [Constraint-Aware Test-Time Scoring for LLM and VLM Candidate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33161)
*Rangarajan Ramesh,Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自适应评分框架：起草模型生成候选，配置模型选择并加权检查项，评分模型评估工具无法计算的性质；硬检查与候选比较结合带来主要增益，而软检查的逐任务配置无显著价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM/VLM 生成多个候选答案后选取最优者的现有方式（模型自置信度、学习型评判模型、领域检查）各有缺陷：流畅但不安全、数学合理但物理不可行、被判高分却违背任务要求。

Method: 构建组合少量必过硬检查与较广软检查（分数混合为单一候选得分）的评分框架，含三部分：生成候选的起草模型、为各任务选择并加权检查项的配置模型、评估无接地工具可计算属性的评分模型。在 CWEval、LIBERO（含安全变体）、LiveCodeBench、HotpotQA 四项任务上评测并做消融。

Result: 消融显示最大增益来自硬检查与候选比较的结合：在首个采样答案常出错或不安全的任务上，联合正确性与安全性最高提升 +0.21（检索增强问答），代码生成安全性最高提升 +0.28（CWEval）；增益大小随首样本质量而变化，首样本已较强时降至近零或略负。逐任务软检查配置层相较更简单方案无可测增益。

Conclusion: 建立统一评分框架，并通过消融表明增益来源是接地硬检查而非软因子配置。

Abstract: When an LLM or VLM produces several candidate answers, picking the best one usually relies on the model's own confidence, a learned judge model, or a domain check. Each has a known weakness: an answer can be fluent but insecure, mathematically plausible but physically infeasible, or rated highly by a judge while quietly breaking a task requirement. We propose a scoring framework that combines a small set of hard checks every answer must pass with a wider set of softer checks whose scores are blended into one number per candidate. This adaptive scoring framework consists of three components: a drafting model that produces the candidates, a configuring model that selects and weights the checks for each task, and scoring models that rate properties no grounded tool can compute. We apply our proposed method to four tasks: writing code that is both correct and secure (CWEval), generating robot manipulation plans (LIBERO with added safety variants), passing competitive-programming hidden tests (LiveCodeBench), and answering questions from retrieved documents (HotpotQA). An ablation study isolates the contribution of each component and finds that the largest gains come from combining hard checks with candidate comparison: on tasks where the first sampled answer is often broken or unsafe, this pairing lifts joint correctness-and-safety by up to +0.21 (retrieval-augmented QA) and code-generation safety by up to +0.28 (CWEval), with the size of the gain tracking how weak the first sample is, falling to near zero or slightly negative where it is already strong. The per-task layer that selects and weights the softer checks adds no measurable value over simpler alternatives. This builds a unified scoring framework, together with an ablation that identifies the grounded hard checks, not the soft-factor configuration, as the source of the gain.

</details>


### [77] [AutoformBench: A Lean Formalization Benchmark for Advanced Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33165)
*Charles Arnal,Ahmad Rammal,Niket Patel,Fabian Gloeckle,Bartosz Piotrowski,Amaury Hayat,Julia Kempe,Rémi Munos,Vivien Cabannes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AutoformBench：341 个 Lean 4 形式化任务的基准，源自 24 个高深数学文献，考察智能体将非形式证明翻译为 Lean 并自行补全缺失定义、引理与基础设施的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测多提供孤立定理陈述，且其支撑理论大多已在 Mathlib 中就绪，无法反映真实数学形式化中"搭建中间理论"的核心难点；因此需要一个更贴近实际形式化流程的评测基准。

Method: 构建 341 个 Lean 4 任务，来自 24 个高深数学来源；由有 Lean 经验的数学家人工形式化并审核每个目标陈述，仅把对应证明留给被测智能体。每题置于更完整的数学开发上下文：智能体获得非形式证明，须在 Lean 中构造缺失定义、中间引理与支撑性基础设施后完成证明；并在其上比较前沿模型表现。

Result: 各前沿模型呈现较为一致的难度前沿：解题成败与数学领域关系不大，更取决于模型构建缺失形式化基础设施的能力。

Conclusion: 真实形式化能力的关键瓶颈在于"补全基础设施"而非单纯定理证明；AutoformBench 提供了针对该能力的评测框架，数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of producing machine-checked Lean proofs of advanced mathematical results. Real mathematical formalization, however, is not isolated theorem proving: it requires building the definitions and intermediate lemmas that connect a result to a formal library. Existing evaluations often provide isolated theorem statements in settings where much of this supporting theory is already available in Mathlib. We introduce AutoformBench, a benchmark of 341 Lean 4 formalization tasks drawn from 24 advanced mathematical sources. Lean-experienced mathematicians formalized and audited each target statement, leaving the corresponding Lean proof to the evaluated agent. Each problem is embedded in a broader mathematical development: agents receive the informal proof but must translate it into Lean while constructing the missing definitions, intermediate lemmas, and supporting infrastructure. We compare the performance of frontier models on these problems. We find a largely shared difficulty frontier: success depends less on mathematical domain than on models' ability to construct missing formal infrastructure. Data and code at https://github.com/facebookresearch/autoformbench

</details>


### [78] [General-Purpose Decision Models for AI Configuration Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33190)
*Jingzhi Gong,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了通用决策模型（以 Jev 为代表）与常规方法及 LLM 在八个 AI 调参任务中作为优化器、性能预测器和区域引导者三种角色的表现：常规优化器在 6/8 任务中排名第一，传统预测器在 7/8 任务中优于 Jev，但作为区域引导者时 Jev 能找到困惑度更低的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 配置调参在有限预算下寻找提升系统性能的设置，现有调参器采用不同的采样与搜索策略，决策策略的选择直接决定调参质量与成本。作者希望探究通用决策模型能否指导调参，并明确其在何种角色下才有价值。

Method: 以已部署的通用决策模型 Jev 为代表性对象（可返回结构化选择与数值评分），在八个 AI 调参任务上，分别将其用作优化器、性能预测器和区域引导者，与常规方法及一个 LLM 进行对照实验比较。

Result: 常规优化器在 8 项比较中的 6 项排名第一；传统预测器在 7 项中优于 Jev；但在区域引导角色下，Jev 找到的配置困惑度低于原生调参器所选配置，在可接受额外决策时间与 API 费用时可获得质量增益。

Conclusion: 通用决策模型并非全面优于常规方法，其价值取决于所承担的角色：应优先保留传统预测器，将通用决策模型用于区域引导，并在调参质量与成本之间做权衡，据此给出了决策角色选择的可操作建议。

Abstract: AI configuration tuning searches for settings that improve the performance of AI systems under a limited budget. Existing tuners use different sampling and search policies, making the choice of decision strategy central to both tuning quality and cost. We investigate whether general-purpose decision models can guide tuning, using Jev as a deployed representative that returns structured choices and numeric scores across diverse tasks. To assess where it helps, we compare its use as an optimizer, performance predictor, and region guider against conventional methods and a large language model (LLM) on eight AI tuning tasks. Conventional optimizers attain rank 1 in six of eight comparisons. Similarly, established predictors outperform Jev in seven. Through region guidance, however, it finds configurations with lower perplexity than those selected by the native tuner, offering a quality gain when additional decision time and API fees are acceptable. These findings yield actionable recommendations on selecting the decision role, retaining conventional predictors, and balancing tuning quality against cost.

</details>


### [79] [What, Who, and When: Structured Harm Assessment for Mental Health Safety via Short Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33263)
*Catarina Botelho,António Farinhas,John Mendonça,Areti Vassilopoulos,Inês Gonçalves,Maya D'Eon,Hugo Pitorro,Laura Cabello,Carrie Spadine,Nuno M. Guerreiro,Ricardo Rei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出临床导向的三维危害分类体系（危害是什么、涉及谁、何时发生），将危害刻画与干预决策解耦，并据此训练35B-A3B推理型守卫模型MindGuard2，在公开基准与真实对话上以约1/10延迟达到与GLM-5.3相当的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于心理健康支持日益增多，安全风险突出。现有guard模型主要做有害内容类别识别，忽视判断恰当回应所需的上下文信息，也无法区分"危害识别"与"干预决策"，导致误报与不必要干预，损害支持效果与用户信任；同时公开基准标注可能偏离真实场景中的危害分布。

Method: 1) 构建临床导向分类体系：三轴刻画危害（what/who/when），并将危害刻画与干预决策分离，使安全策略以临床专家编写、可更新可审计的显式指令形式置于模型权重之外；2) 由临床专家验证分类体系，并验证一组LLM评判者以实现可扩展标注；3) 用该体系重标注公开基准，统计危害分布差异；4) 训练MindGuard2（35B-A3B推理型守卫模型）：先对简短推理轨迹做SFT，再用带推理长度惩罚的强化学习优化，在保留推理收益的同时降低推理开销。

Result: 重标注发现公开基准中很少包含"指向用户自身的危害"，而该类危害占真实对话中近一半。MindGuard2在两个公开基准与真实对话上取得与规模大得多的GLM-5.3相当的平衡准确率，中位延迟约低10倍，推进了准确率-延迟帕累托前沿；在高召回下仍保持低假阳性率。

Conclusion: 将危害刻画与干预决策分离、并以显式临床指令外置安全策略，是可行且实用的安全护栏设计路径；基于合成数据训练的MindGuard2在准确率-延迟权衡与召回-误报权衡上均具优势。作者发布该SOTA守卫模型、合成对话及公开基准的新体系标注。

Abstract: The growing use of large language models (LLMs) for mental health support raises important safety concerns. Existing guard models primarily identify categories of harmful content, often overlooking the context needed to determine an appropriate response. We introduce a clinically grounded taxonomy that characterizes harm along three dimensions: what the harm is, who is involved, and when it occurs. It further separates harm characterization from intervention decisions, thus enabling safety policies to be specified outside the model weights through explicit, clinician-curated instructions that can be updated and audited. We validate the taxonomy with expert clinicians and validate a panel of LLM judges to scale annotation. Relabeling public benchmarks in this taxonomy shows that they rarely contain harm directed at the user, which accounts for nearly half of the harm in real conversations. We then train MindGuard2, a 35B-A3B reasoning-based guard model using supervised fine-tuning on short reasoning traces followed by reinforcement learning with a reasoning-length penalty, to minimize inference overhead while preserving the benefits of reasoning. On two public benchmarks and real-world conversations, MindGuard2 achieves balanced accuracy comparable to the substantially larger GLM-5.3 at approximately 10x lower median latency, advancing the accuracy-latency Pareto frontier. Furthermore, it achieves low false-positive rates at high recall, helping balance the need to detect unsafe situations against the risk of unnecessary interventions that may disrupt support and undermine user trust. We release a safety guardrail model with SOTA performance trained on synthetic data, together with synthetic conversations and annotations of public benchmarks in our taxonomy.

</details>


### [80] [Certify What You Plant: Shortcut-Free Proof Depth in Logical Reasoning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33303)
*Baptiste Juran,Dimitri Kachler,Damien Sileo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以"每个证明必须经过的步骤、最短证明、极小证明数与不可或缺前提"来度量逻辑题的真实难度，并基于经独立检查器验证的证书构建"每个证明都具有指定结构"的程序化生成题目，发现现有八个数据集的标注深度普遍高估难度；在训练中混合多种经认证题型（含前向生成无法产生的模型检验题）的迁移效果优于FLDx2式推导数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化生成的逻辑题常用"植入证明"（planted proof）的深度标注难度，但植入证明只是众多可能证明之一：题目可能存在更短的证明或多个互不相关的证明，因此标注深度会高估解题实际所需。作者希望客观量化"题目相对于给定推理规则集真正需要什么"。

Method: 1）对每个问题计算四类量：所有证明必经的步骤、最短证明、极小证明的数目、不可或缺的前提；并导出可供独立检查器验证的证书。2）用该框架审计八个数据集。3）借助"每套规则集只需证明一次的捷径目录"（shortcut catalog），构造使每个证明都具备指定结构的新题目，并完整检查、附证书。4）在Qwen3（0.6B–8B）上用不同数据混合进行训练对比，包括前向生成无法产生的模型检验题。

Result: 审计发现：ProofWriter与Blocksworld的深度是被强制的；FLDx2中标为深度5的题目实际只需约两次特定规则应用；ZebraLogic的答案不需要任何特定步骤。等规模训练下，经认证题型（含模型检验题）的混合数据比FLDx2风格的推导数据迁移更好，且最佳混合为跨深度、跨题型的组合。

Conclusion: 仅以植入证明深度设定难度会系统性高估题目要求；应以可验证的证明证书与结构化难度度量替代。题目类型与深度的多样化混合比单一推导式数据更能提升推理能力迁移。

Abstract: Procedurally generated logic problems are used to both evaluate and train reasoning in language models. Their difficulty is usually set by planting a proof, that is, by sampling a proof of the desired depth and stating its premises as the problem. However, a planted proof is only one possible proof. A problem can also have shorter proofs, or several unrelated ones, and its stated depth then overstates what solving it requires. We measure what a problem actually requires, relative to a stated set of inference rules. For each problem, we compute the steps every proof must make, the shortest proof, the number of minimal proofs and the indispensable premises, and export certificates that an independent checker verifies. Audits of eight datasets find depth that is forced (ProofWriter, Blocksworld), problems labelled depth 5 that require about two specific rule applications (FLDx2), and answers that need no particular step (ZebraLogic). We then construct problems whose every proof has a requested structure, using a catalog of shortcuts proved once per rule set. Every constructed problem is checked in full and comes with its own certificate. In training, the best data mixes depths and problem types. At equal size, such mixtures of certified problem types, including model-checking problems that forward generation does not produce, transfer better than FLDx2-style derivation data for Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 8B.

</details>


### [81] [The Platonic Representation Hypothesis in Jacobian-Space: Language and Vision Models Agree Approximately Up to a Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33304)
*Ross Murphy,Gabriel Brostow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文在J-space（Jacobian透镜所定义的隐藏态方向集合）中检验"柏拉图表示假说"(PRH)，发现视觉与语言模型的J-space不仅在表示上、而且在"如何作用于表示"上趋同，且主要相差一个旋转；跨模型的旋转后词向量可因果地操控视觉语言模型的预测、图像生成的物体及ImageNet分类分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作仅在激活层面（表示内容）验证PRH，未检验模型是否在"表示如何影响输出"这一功能/作用层面也趋同；作者希望把PRH从"如何表征世界"推广到"如何作用于世界"。

Method: 在transformer某一选定层用Jacobian透镜为词表（或图像类别）中每个词定义隐藏空间向量——即最能提升该词（该位置或之后）生成倾向、或最提升该类别预测的方向，全体构成J-space；在视觉与语言模型间比较J-space，并用少量词拟合单一旋转来映射两模型的J-space，再在未拟合的词/类上检验；进而做因果干预：将文本语言模型的词向量旋转后注入视觉语言模型的视觉patch token、图像生成器的文本条件、ImageNet分类器（无文本训练）中观察效果。

Result: 跨视觉与语言模型在J-space中观察到PRH证据；J-space基本收敛至一个旋转——在词子集上拟合的单次旋转可近似把一模型的J-space映射到另一模型，并泛化到未参与拟合的词与类。因果验证：旋转后的词向量注入视觉语言模型的patch token会改变其对图中物体的判断，且仅作用于注入的图像区域；注入图像生成器的文本条件会改变生成图像中的物体；注入ImageNet分类器可把该物体类分数提升到几乎最高，反之亦然。

Conclusion: 模型的趋同不仅是表示层面的，也是作用层面的：不同模态模型在表示与输出影响上共享近似旋转对齐的结构，支持并深化了柏拉图表示假说，并为跨模型、跨模态的表示对齐与可控干预提供依据。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) posits that AI models converge toward a shared representation of the world, even when trained on different data modalities. Previous work has found evidence for the PRH in the activations of vision and language models. In this work, we ask whether vision and language models also converge in how they act on these representations: precisely, in how these internal representations affect the models' output. At a chosen layer of a transformer, the Jacobian lens gives every word in the vocabulary (or class in an image classifier) a vector in the hidden state: the vector that most increases the model's tendency to generate that word, at that position or later, or to predict that class. Collectively, these vectors make up what is referred to as the model's J-space. We find evidence of the PRH in J-space across vision and language models, and furthermore, we find that J-spaces mostly converge up to a rotation. That is, a single rotation, fitted on a subset of words, approximately maps one model's J-space onto another's, and so applies to words and classes it was not fitted on. A word's vector, rotated from a text-only language model's J-space into the visual patch tokens of a vision-language model, changes the object the vision-language model says is in the image (and only in the image region where it is added). Rotated into an image generator's text conditioning, a word's vector changes the object in the generated image. Rotated into an ImageNet classifier (trained without text), it raises that object's class score above almost all others, and vice versa. This suggests that models converge not only in how they represent the world, but also in how they act on it.

</details>


### [82] [Brand Recall and Category Accuracy in Language Models: An Audit of 1,000 B2B SaaS Brands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33322)
*Ben Moore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在333个产品类别的1000个B2B SaaS品牌上，将"能否生成品牌名(召回)"与"能否正确归类(类别准确率)"分开测量，发现二者存在系统性差异，主张报告指标时应分别呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能说出某品牌名，并不代表它对该品牌进行了正确分类；将"召回"与"分类正确"混为一谈会误导对模型知识的评估。作者希望用分离的度量方式厘清这两种能力，并检验其与品牌特征（技术覆盖度、自有内容资产）以及情感表征的关系。

Method: 在Gemma 4 12B上、不使用外部检索，对333个类别下1000个B2B SaaS品牌：类别提示直接诱导品牌生成以测召回；另设五候选类别探针，观察参考标签排序以测类别准确率，并按获胜者与次优者的对数概率边际(>0.6)分层；跨四代Gemma比较召回；用探索性回归关联技术覆盖度、自有资产复合指标与两类准确率；另构建基于表征的情感度量，与客户评分做线性相关检验(n=921)。

Result: 类别提示诱导出510个品牌(召回51.0%)；五候选探针中787个品牌参考标签排第一、194个干扰项排第一、19个区分度不足，在981个可区分样本上错误率19.8%，边际>0.6的547个品牌错误率降至3.5%。四代Gemma召回分别为39.8%、44.3%、51.1%、51.0%。技术覆盖度与召回相关、自有资产复合指标与类别准确率相关，但两者调整变量集不同，不构成因果结论。表征情感度量与客户评分几乎无线性相关(r=0.03)。

Conclusion: 召回与类别准确率应分别报告；上述边际分层属队列内测量而非校准置信度；未能诱导出品牌名不能推断其不在模型参数或训练数据中；探索性回归仅供假设生成。

Abstract: A language model can name a brand without classifying it correctly. This study measures these outcomes separately for 1,000 B2B SaaS brands across 333 product categories. On Gemma 4 12B, with no external retrieval, category prompts elicit 510 brands (51.0%). A separate five-candidate category probe ranks the reference label first for 787 brands, a distractor first for 194, and yields little separation for 19. Among the 981 separated rankings, the error rate is 19.8%. It falls to 3.5% for the 547 brands with a winner-to-runner-up log-probability margin above 0.6. These are within-cohort measurements, not calibrated confidence estimates. Exploratory regressions associate technical coverage with recall and an owned-properties composite with category accuracy, but the composites use different adjustment sets and do not establish causal effects. Across four Gemma generations, measured recall is 39.8%, 44.3%, 51.1% and 51.0%. A separate representation-based sentiment measure has little linear association with customer ratings (r=0.03, n=921). The findings support reporting recall and category accuracy separately. Failure to elicit a brand does not establish its absence from model parameters or training data.

</details>


### [83] [Confidence scores for gated automation of large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33394)
*Luiz G. A. Alves,Aaron Vermeersch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作用监督学习训练模型预测LLM答案的正确性，在冻结的2B骨干最终层隐状态上拟合线性置信度打分头，并训练纯文本元模型作为闭源模型的替代方案；置信度打分头在多种基线中表现最优，能在高准确率目标下自动化最大比例的工作量，但跨数据集迁移能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体虽具备较强自动化决策能力，却常以高置信度输出错误答案，限制了其在影响个人与组织的决策场景中的应用。可靠的门控需要细粒度的置信度分数，而现有估计往往缺失或过于粗糙，难以支撑有效阈值判定。

Method: 采用监督学习训练正确性预测模型：在冻结LLM骨干的最终层隐状态上拟合线性置信度打分头；同时训练纯文本元模型，作为无法访问隐状态的黑盒闭源模型的替代方案。实验基于单一2B参数骨干，评测数据中约98%为SQuAD分级答案，其余为TruthfulQA。

Result: 置信度打分头优于所有基线与元模型，在高准确率目标下可自动化的任务比例最大；提示式置信度分数虽看似校准良好，却缺乏阈值化所需的分辨率；对未见数据集的迁移性一般，但打分头仍是最稳健的方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的置信度打分框架提升了LLM智能体自动化的安全性，并可推广用于对安全性、合规性等其他属性进行门控。

Abstract: AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated decision-making. However, LLMs may produce incorrect answers with high confidence, limiting their applicability in decisions that affect individuals and organizations. Reliable confidence scores are necessary to gate such decisions, and existing estimates are frequently unavailable or too coarse to support effective gating. This work uses supervised learning to train models to predict the correctness of LLM-generated answers, enabling automated confident responses and routing uncertain ones for human review. A linear confidence score head is fitted to the final-layer hidden state of the frozen LLM backbone, and text-only metamodels are trained as an alternative for closed models. All results come from a single 2B-parameter backbone, on graded answers that are about 98% SQuAD and the remainder TruthfulQA. Against multiple baselines, the confidence score head outperforms all baselines and metamodels, automating the largest fraction of the workload at high accuracy targets. Prompted confidence scores can appear well calibrated yet lack the resolution needed for thresholding. Although transferability to unseen datasets is modest, the confidence score head remains the most robust approach. The proposed confidence scoring framework enhances the safety of automating LLM-based agents and can be adapted to gate other properties, including safety or compliance.

</details>


### [84] [Partial Routing: Trace-Tuned Delegation for Resource-Efficient Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33402)
*Siddharth Arya,Kshitij Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"partial routing"（部分路由）：让前沿主导模型在执行中把可分解的子任务委托给更廉价的worker模型，无需训练，由δ控制委托程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型路由通常在"整个任务"层面二选一（简单任务给便宜模型），但单一agentic任务往往混合易/难子任务，整任务级选型低效。

Method: 从两模型历史运行中识别重复出现的子任务，度量各模型在这些子任务上的质量与成本，生成带参数δ的委托指导；执行时由lead模型自主决定何时、委托哪部分，不训练任何模型。

Result: 在ViBench与Terminal-Bench 4.0留出任务上，平均表现优于lead单独及任务级路由；δ=0.75时ViBench从70.2升至78.5且成本降19%，Terminal-Bench通过率54%→61%且成本相当；仅提供子任务分解（不给worker）亦降本并提升Terminal-Bench通过率。

Conclusion: 基于历史运行分析可实现更高效规划与更有效委托，成本与实现开销都很小。

Abstract: AI models vary enormously in capability and price. Model routing exploits this by sending easier tasks to cheaper models. However, a single agentic task often combines easy and difficult subtasks, making one model choice for the entire task inefficient. We introduce partial routing, which guides a frontier lead model to delegate suitable subtasks to a cheaper worker model. From both models' past runs, we identify recurring subtasks and measure each model's quality and cost on them. These measurements produce delegation guidance, with a parameter, $δ$, controlling how much is delegated. The lead decides when and what to delegate during execution, without training either model. On held-out tasks from ViBench and Terminal-Bench 4.0, partial routing achieves higher mean task performance than the lead alone and task-level routing. With $δ= 0.75$, it raises the lead's ViBench score from 70.2 to 78.5 at 19% lower cost, and its Terminal-Bench pass rate from 54% to 61% at similar cost. Giving the lead only the subtask breakdown, without a worker, also reduces cost on both benchmarks and raises the Terminal-Bench pass rate. These results show that analysis of past runs can support more efficient planning and more effective delegation, with little cost and implementation overhead.

</details>


### [85] [Collaborative Language Models for Semantic IoT Data Processing: Validation through Zero-Shot Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33411)
*Mohsen Shirali,Kean Ooi,Anna Knab,Mohammadreza Fani Sani,Estefania Serral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将协作式语言模型用作零样本语义抽象层，把IoT低层传感器观测转换为高层事件/活动标签，提出五阶段工作流，并在CASAS Aruba数据集上以11种配置评估，发现协作结构比模型数量更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT系统产生的是低层传感器观测，无法直接用于过程分析，需先转化为语义有意义的事件；同时希望探索无需任务特定参数微调、使用现成模型的模块化语义事件抽象方案。

Method: 提出五阶段工作流：语义映射、语义聚合、时间与上下文聚合、专门化推理、最终事件抽象；产出的事件记录与上下文可表示为供下游过程挖掘使用的事件日志。在CASAS Aruba智能家居数据集上以人类活动识别任务评估11种工作流配置。
