Abstract: Medical reasoning agents combine large language model (LLM) reasoning with clinical workflows and specialized neural tools. However, general-purpose LLMs receive limited training exposure to domain-specific medical tools, and the uncertainty and distributional shifts of neural tool outputs make selecting tools from simple descriptions significantly more challenging than in deterministic settings such as calculators or dictionary lookups. In this paper, we propose EATS, an experience-augmented tool selection strategy that leverages accumulated tool-use trajectories to improve tool selection in medical reasoning agents. EATS constructs a memory of past interactions, including queries, tool choices, arguments, and self-evaluated feedback, and retrieves relevant experiences to guide tool selection for new queries. As memory construction does not require ground-truth supervision, EATS scales naturally with test-time experience. We evaluate EATS on tool-augmented medical reasoning tasks, including closed-ended visual question answering and free-form report generation. Experiments on four public datasets, ChestAgentBench, CheXbench, MIMIC-CXR, and SLAKE-VQA, across three multimodal LLMs, Qwen3-VL 2B, Qwen3-VL 32B, and GPT-5.5, show consistent gains in tool selection behavior and downstream medical reasoning performance. Code is available at https://github.com/Limingyuan001/EATS.

</details>


### [119] [EGSE: Evidence-Grounded Skill Evolution for Medical Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33909)
*Mingyuan Li,Tong Jia,Ziyuan Yang,Wenbo Qi,Haoran Wang,Jie Gui,Baosheng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EGSE（Evidence-Grounded Skill Evolution）框架，将相似病例的历史对话蒸馏为带适用条件的可复用工作流，并通过有来源支撑、经验证批准的更新逐步精炼多面向技能库，从而提升医学推理智能体的推理准确率并减少工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学推理智能体结合基础模型与专用工具解读医学图像，但复用历史经验存在三大困难：(1) 最终答案正确可能掩盖错误的推理或工具使用；(2) 合成的建议可能缺乏来源支撑；(3) 表面相似的病例可能实际需要不同的诊断流程。因此需要一种既可信又可迁移的经验复用机制。

Method: EGSE 对中间推理与工具使用进行评估，对比重复成功与失败的 rollout，提取有用做法与有证据支撑的规避性指导；将其综合为带适用条件、且可追溯至源对话的检查项的工作流；技能库的新增与合并需通过来源回溯审查与执行验证。推理阶段按影像模态、问题任务类型与疾病类别将查询路由到技能节点，再依据工作流条件、当前图像与问题检索并选择适用工作流，指导证据获取与解读。

Result: 在四个胸部 X 射线基准上，EGSE 在推理准确率上优于代表性医学智能体与技能进化方法，同时减少工具调用次数；在七个基础模型上均取得一致的准确率提升，并支持跨数据集的技能迁移。

Conclusion: 以证据落地（evidence-grounded）为核心的技能进化机制可有效将历史对话经验转化为可信、可验证、可路由复用的条件化工作流，兼顾准确率、工具效率与跨模型、跨数据集的泛化能力。

Abstract: Medical reasoning agents combine foundation models with specialized tools to interpret medical images, yet reusing their historical experience remains challenging. A correct final answer can conceal flawed reasoning or tool use, synthesized advice may lack source support, and superficially similar cases may require different diagnostic procedures. In this paper, we propose EGSE, an evidence-grounded skill evolution framework that distills historical dialogues from similar cases into reusable conditional workflows and progressively refines a multi-faceted skill library through supported and validated updates to improve medical agents. EGSE evaluates intermediate reasoning and tool use and contrasts repeated successful and failed rollouts to extract useful practices and evidence-supported avoidance guidance. These experiences are synthesized into workflows with applicability conditions and checks traceable to source dialogues. Library additions and merges require source-grounded review and execution validation. At inference time, EGSE routes queries to skill nodes organized by imaging modality, question task type, and disease category, then retrieves and selects applicable workflows based on their conditions, the current images, and the question to guide evidence acquisition and interpretation. Experiments across four chest X-ray benchmarks show that EGSE outperforms representative medical agents and skill evolution methods in reasoning accuracy while reducing tool calls. It also delivers consistent accuracy gains across seven foundation models and supports skill transfer across datasets.

</details>


### [120] [What Does Skill Lift Actually Measure? A Source-Code-Level Audit and Noise-Aware Reanalysis of SkillsBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33943)
*Haozhe Zhang,Zhen Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对SkillsBench进行源码级审计与7,554次公开试验的配对重分析，发现"技能有害"的负面证据主要源于评分器依赖、定义缺口、占位技能、执行框架内置技能等基准缺陷与运行采样噪声，任务级下降均不显著；基于无技能分数的门控策略在打乱标签后仍"提升"4-7pp，说明其收益为伪信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SkillsBench报告人类整理技能平均提升约16.6pp但部分任务下降，后续研究据此按单任务增益对技能做门控或剔除。作者怀疑这些"负面结果"与门控收益的有效性，遂从源头核查基准质量与统计可靠性。

Method: 1) 源码级审计SkillsBench基准；2) 分析7,554次公开试验，构造1,421个"(配置, 任务)"配对单元；3) 审计发现两例经控制验证的评分器依赖、一例操作定义缺口（三任务平均提升74.6pp，全集18.7pp）、四个含占位技能目录的任务、某执行框架在多数运行中额外注入48个内置技能；4) 对池化数据做多重检验校正的任务级重分析；5) 对门控策略做条件标签置换的安慰剂检验，并在未参与拟合与决策的留出运行上评估。结果：任务级下降无统计学显著性；每单元每条件最多3次运行，负差异高度易受采样噪声影响，且算术上58%的单元不可能出现下降；门控在日志中提升2-3pp，但打乱标签后仍提升4-7pp，留出集上无显著改善。

Result: 三个受污染任务平均提升74.6pp，显著高于全集18.7pp；任务级负增益在多重检验校正后均不显著；58%的单元在算术上不可能显示下降；门控策略的日志收益（2-3pp）低于标签置换后的安慰剂收益（4-7pp），留出集无显著提升。

Conclusion: 负面单元的原始计数不能证明技能有害；基于单任务增益的技能门控与剔除缺乏有效证据，其表面收益可由基准缺陷与噪声解释。作者已公开审计账本与分析脚本，呼吁在结论层面前先修复基准。

Abstract: Agent skills are directories of instructions, reference materials, and scripts loaded on demand by coding agents. According to SkillsBench, although human-curated skills can raise the passing rate by about 16.6 percentage points (pp) on average, they have actually reduced the results for some tasks. Later research has started to gate or eliminate skills according to the above per-task gains. We have audited the above benchmark at the source-code level and analysed 7,554 public trials to form 1,421 paired "(configuration, task) cells". Source-code audit identified two controlled-verified cases of grader dependence and one operational definition gap; the average lift on these three tasks was 74.6 pp, and the overall average lift for the full set was 18.7 pp. The audit also found that four tasks contained placeholder skill directories, and a certain execution harness additionally added 48 built-in skills in most runs. No task-level reduction achieved statistical significance in the pooled reanalysis with multiple-testing correction. As there were at most three runs per condition for each cell, the observed negative differences are very susceptible to run-sampling noise, and arithmetically, 58% of the cells could not have shown a decrease. Therefore, the raw numbers of negative cells cannot show that skills cause harm. A gating strategy based on the skill-free score seems to improve by 2-3 pp in the logs; however, even after shuffling condition labels to remove any skill effect, it still achieves an increase of 4-7 pp. On the held-out runs not used for fitting and decision-making, there was no statistically significant improvement. The audit ledger and analysis scripts have been published by us.

</details>


### [121] [Should Service Requirements Be Scalarized or Constrained When Minimizing Costs? A Case Study on HVAC Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33946)
*Tai-You Chen,Mihael Mohorcic,Blaz Bertalanic,Carolina Fortuna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文在Smart Buildings Challenge中以单一HVAC控制策略为对象，固定其他组件，比较"标量化惩罚"与"硬/软约束"两类处理方式在模型优化（MPC）与学习策略（PPO）中的表现，揭示二者等价条件依赖于需求的影子价格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化决策系统常在满足QoS、舒适区间或安全裕度等需求的同时最小化能耗/资源成本。需求既可与成本标量化（固定或自适应拉格朗日权重），也可作为约束施加，但这一建模选择对性能的影响缺乏系统性对比。

Method: 在Smart Buildings Challenge环境下，同一策略需控制未见建筑的HVAC，评价指标为舒适度、能源成本与碳排放的加权和。保持其他组件不变，在MPC中对比硬约束与软约束（l1惩罚），在PPO中对比固定惩罚、拉格朗日惩罚与动作投影层三种实现；并在48栋未见建筑上检验权重迁移性。

Result: MPC中，软约束权重超过需求影子价格即复现硬约束效果，挑战赛舒适权重约为影子价格的4倍以上；权重不足时牺牲舒适，而在舒适估值低于影子价格的度量下软约束可优至28%。在训练建筑上调好的权重迁移到48栋未见建筑时比硬约束差3.9%，因影子价格随建筑变化。PPO中投影策略自始满足需求并在训练早期领先；更长训练后拉格朗日策略反超而投影策略退化，固定惩罚则学不会满足需求。

Conclusion: 约束式与惩罚式并非普适优劣：惩罚权重相对影子价格的位置决定等价性与失效边界，且影子价格跨建筑漂移使单一调参权重难以泛化；学习策略中投影法短期可靠但长期退化，拉格朗日法在充分训练后更具优势，固定惩罚不可靠。

Abstract: Automated decision systems, from learned policies to model-based optimizers, often minimize costs such as energy or resource use while meeting a service requirement, for example the quality of service (QoS) specified in a service level agreement (SLA), a comfort band, or a safety margin. The requirement can be scalarized with the costs into a single objective, with a fixed or an adaptively tuned (Lagrangian) weight, or it can be imposed as a constraint. We study this choice in the Smart Buildings Challenge, in which a single policy controls the heating, ventilation and air conditioning (HVAC) of previously unseen buildings and is evaluated by a weighted sum of comfort, energy cost and carbon emissions. With all other components fixed, we compare the two treatments in model-based optimization, represented by model predictive control (MPC) with the requirement as a hard or a soft (l1-penalized) constraint, and in learned policies, represented by proximal policy optimization (PPO) with a fixed or a Lagrangian penalty in the reward or a projection layer on the action. In MPC, the soft constraint reproduces the hard one once its weight exceeds the requirement's shadow price, which the challenge's comfort weight exceeds approximately fourfold; below this weight, comfort is sacrificed, and under a metric that values comfort below its shadow price the soft constraint outperforms the hard one by up to 28%. A weight tuned on the training buildings is 3.9% worse than the hard constraint on 48 unseen buildings, because the shadow price varies across buildings. In PPO, the projected policy satisfies the requirement from the outset and leads early in training; with longer training, the Lagrangian policy overtakes it while the projected policy deteriorates, and the fixed penalty does not learn to satisfy the requirement.

</details>


### [122] [PatternMiner: Automated Mathematical Discovery Through LLM-Guided Training and Interpretation of Small Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33966)
*Michael Ruofan Zeng,Junaid Hasan,Xuanyu Yang,Emily Xia Meng,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PatternMiner 融合 LLM 智能体与可解释机器学习：由宽泛问题生成数据集、训练窄模型并提取模式形成猜想，再用数学软件搜索反例迭代精炼，在五个代数组合正性问题中证明若干新定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 数学发现分两条路线：LLM 文本推理适合解决界定明确的猜想，但难以直接、开放式地探索大规模数学数据集；窄模型加可解释性方法虽支持人在回路的开放式探索，却基本停留在简单经验模式检测层面。论文旨在弥合这两类范式的割裂。

Method: 构建 PatternMiner 系统：LLM 智能体依据初始宽泛问题生成数学数据集；在其上训练窄机器学习模型；用可解释性技术从训练模型中抽取模式并据此提出猜想；随后智能体调用数学软件搜索反例，若找到反例则作为反馈用于猜想精炼或进入新一轮训练与可解释性分析；猜想在这一迭代过程中逐步演化，智能体可在任意阶段尝试给出证明。

Result: 在代数组合学五个著名正性问题上的应用给出多项新定理：涉及 Schubert 正性与投影、一个字符行求值公式、Kazhdan–Lusztig 组合不变性猜想的受限版本，以及一族 immanant 系数的 Schur 正性。论文还总结了案例中猜想生成最常见的路径及主要失效模式。

Conclusion: LLM 智能体与可解释窄模型的闭环耦合可有效支撑开放式数学探索，并在具体正性问题上产出可验证的新结论；同时，猜想生成路径与失效模式的分析为后续系统设计提供了经验依据。

Abstract: There are several approaches to AI-driven mathematical discovery. One of these involves the use of large language models (LLMs) to resolve well-specified conjectures via text-based reasoning, while another uses narrow models and interpretability for more open-ended, human-in-the-loop exploration using object-based mathematical data. The latter is limited in its ability to move beyond simple empirical pattern detection while the former is not well-designed for direct, open-ended exploration of large mathematical datasets. In this paper we introduce PatternMiner, a system that combines these two paradigms. In PatternMiner, an LLM agent generates mathematical datasets based on an initial broad question, trains narrow machine learning models on them, uses interpretability techniques to extract patterns from these trained models, and uses these patterns to formulate conjectures. Once an initial conjecture has been formulated, PatternMiner instructs the agent to search for counterexamples using calls to mathematical software. If a set of counterexamples can be found, these are used as feedback for conjecture refinement or another round of training and interpretability. The conjecture progressively evolves through this iterative sequence with the agent allowed to try to prove it at any point. We describe the results of applying PatternMiner to five well-known positivity problems in algebraic combinatorics. PatternMiner proves several new theorems related to Schubert positivity and projection, a character-row evaluation formula, a restricted version of the Kazhdan--Lusztig combinatorial invariance conjecture, and Schur positivity for a family of immanant coefficients. We end by analyzing the most common routes to conjecture generation that are used by PatternMiner in our case studies as well as documenting the most common failure modes.

</details>


### [123] [Honest but Wrong: Formalizing Retraction in the Ethics of Speech Acts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34011)
*Benjamin Icard,Gauvain Bourgne,Jeanne Bonnaventure,Jean-Gabriel Ganascia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将基于信念的言语行为评估框架扩展到"撤回"情形，用FIPA-ACL的disconfirm行为分析诚实发言者事后发现断言为假时是否及基于何种道德理由必须撤回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诚实的断言事后可能被证明为假，此类"错误真值"并非谎言，却引出一个问题：发言者是否必须撤回、以及依据何种道德理由撤回。现有基于信念的框架仅评估说话时的言语行为，缺乏对撤回阶段的道德分析。

Method: 以FIPA-ACL的disconfirm言语行为为形式化载体，将原有在义务论与后果论下评估言语行为的信念框架扩展至撤回情形；在Answer Set Programming中实现一个法律咨询案例进行验证。

Result: 证明义务论的诚实义务只作用于发言时，永不要求撤回错误真值；为此补充一条"持续性义务"，禁止让听者继续相信发言者已不再相信的内容。法律咨询案例显示：义务论与后果论都要求撤回，但当撤回过晚、无法防止伤害时，后果论不再要求撤回。

Conclusion: 撤回的道德必要性不能由说话时的诚实义务推出，需引入持续性信念义务；两种规范伦理框架在撤回问题上给出可区分且依赖时机的判定。

Abstract: A speaker who asserts honestly may later discover that its assertion was false. Such erroneous truths are not lies, yet they raise the question of whether, and on which moral grounds, the speaker must retract them. Using the FIPA-ACL performative disconfirm, we extend a belief-based framework that evaluates speech acts under deontologism and consequentialism to the case of retraction. We prove that the honesty duty of deontologism, which bears on utterances when they are made, never requires retracting an erroneous truth, and add a persistent one, which forbids letting the addressee believe what the speaker no longer believes. On a legal-advice case implemented in Answer Set Programming, we show that deontologism and consequentialism both require the retraction, but that consequentialism no longer does once it comes too late to prevent harm.

</details>


### [124] [Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models via Agentic Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34036)
*Yusheng Zhao,Xiao Luo,Zhiping Xiao,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Model Editing (AME)框架，用LLM智能体根据少样本示例与模型观测，自主设计领域定制化的适应程序（涵盖输入变换、编码器编辑、特征计算与分类器拟合），在11个数据集上以CLIP ViT-B/16取得对比方法中最优平均精度（无反向传播81.2%，含反向传播81.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本适应方法（调提示、表示或分类器）依赖预先指定的固定适应流程；但不同领域所需的视觉判别线索差异显著，固定流程难以通用，因此希望让LLM智能体利用少样本示例与模型观测来自行设计适应过程本身。

Method: 将适应过程形式化为可执行的、领域定制化的适应程序，联合设计输入变换、编码器编辑、特征计算与分类器拟合。智能体通过检查图像、类别语义、模型参数、中间表示与激活来提出关于目标任务的假设，在支持集上实现并测试候选程序，以聚合验证精度指导迭代改进与选择；每个程序拟合完整的领域模型并在测试前固定。智能体及其工具跨领域共享，各领域获得各自的适应程序。

Result: 在11个数据集、CLIP ViT-B/16骨干下，无反向传播时平均精度81.2%，含反向传播时81.9%，均为对比方法中最高；进一步实验验证了方法在不同VLM骨干与不同LLM智能体上的适用性。

Conclusion: 以智能体自动设计适应程序的方式替代人工预设流程，可在少样本跨域场景中取得更好性能，且具有良好的骨干与智能体可迁移性；测试前固定领域模型保证了评估的公平性。

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation transfers pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to new domains using a small set of labeled images. Most methods fit prompts, representations, or classifiers within an adaptation procedure specified in advance. Yet the visual distinctions needed for recognition can differ substantially across domains, motivating the question: Can an LLM agent use few-shot examples and model observations to design the adaptation procedure itself? We propose Agentic Model Editing (AME), an agentic framework that jointly designs input transformations, encoder edits, feature computation, and classifier fitting as an executable, domain-customized adaptation program. The agent inspects images, class semantics, model parameters, intermediate representations, and activations to formulate hypotheses about the target task. It implements and tests candidate programs on the support set, with aggregate validation accuracy guiding refinement and selection. Each program fits a complete domain model that is fixed before testing. The agent and its tools are shared across domains, while each domain receives its own adaptation program. Across eleven datasets with CLIP ViT-B/16, AME achieves the highest average accuracies of 81.2% without back-propagation and 81.9% with back-propagation among the compared methods. Further experiments demonstrate applicability across VLM backbones and LLM agents.

</details>


### [125] [Budget, Readout or Reasoning? Where Small Reasoning Models Lose Value in Auction Bidding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34068)
*Mahbod Khaleti,Seyed Reza Tavakoli,Baran Hoseini,Alireza Noori,Amir Amini,Koorosh Aslansefat,Marjan Alirezaie,Ali Habibnia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究让6个4B–14B开源推理模型在eBay与AuctionNet竞价景观上出价，将其结果与精确计算器对比，并把价值损失按成因拆分：模型自身两类失败（无视自己轨迹中已排第一的最优选项、错误答案偏向菜单上的最高出价）在768强制token下造成7–17%的剩余损失，算术错误很少；另两类损失来自算子选择——token上限截断轨迹、推理块未闭合即取答案，闭合后最弱模型由42%升至84%（eBay）。一种"审计—覆盖"机制可将计算器领先份额的回收率从42–85%提升到84–100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告平台与市场向投标者展示竞价景观（各候选出价的估计获胜概率），并越来越多地让基于推理语言模型的智能体直接选择出价。此类智能体在信息已给定的情况下仍可能偏离最优，但此前缺少对损失来源的可归因分解，因此作者希望把"模型失效"与"算子/使用方式失效"区分开来量化。

Method: 给6个4B–14B参数的开源推理模型提供竞价景观（来自eBay成交价与模拟广告市场AuctionNet的获胜曲线），在留出拍卖上将其出价与精确计算器的出价对比打分。由于估计值已交给智能体，可将损失按原因拆分。实验变量包括：强制768 token、pay-your-bid定价、4,096 token内自主停止、推理块闭合与否，以及一个"审计"机制——标记与其自身轨迹数字不一致的出价并用计算器出价覆盖，采用嵌套交叉验证评估。

Result: 模型侧两类失败：即使自身轨迹中已把最优选项排第一仍跳过它；错误答案偏向菜单上最高出价，且偏倚与出价绝对大小相关而非列表位置，采样下依然存在。768强制token、pay-your-bid下，错误出价使投标者损失计算器出价所获剩余的7–17%；算术错误罕见。算子侧两类损失：token上限截断轨迹——若允许在4,096 token内自行停止，5个模型可回收计算器相对"无推理模型"领先量的91–100%；推理块未闭合时索取答案会误读——先闭合推理块使最弱模型在eBay由42%升至84%，AuctionNet由24%升至81%。审计并覆盖机制在嵌套交叉验证下把768 token时回收率由42–85%提升至84–100%，且同一读取设置可从eBay迁移到AuctionNet。

Conclusion: 推理模型出价的大部分价值损失并非来自算术能力，而来自两类可诊断的模型偏倚（忽视自证的最优项、偏向大额出价）与两类可修复的算子选择（强制token上限、推理块未闭合即取答案）。简单的闭合推理块与"审计—覆盖"即可大幅回收计算器领先量，且相关设置具备跨市场迁移性，说明部署层面的工程选择与模型能力同等重要。

Abstract: Ad platforms and marketplaces show bidders a bid landscape, the estimated chance of winning at each candidate bid, and increasingly let agents built on reasoning language models choose the bid. We give six open reasoning models of 4B to 14B parameters such landscapes, with win curves from eBay closing prices and from a simulated advertising market (AuctionNet), and score their bids on held-out auctions against an exact calculator. Because the agent is handed the estimates, the lost value can be split by cause. Two failures belong to the models. They pass over the best option even when the values written in their own trace already rank it first, and their wrong answers lean toward the highest bid on the menu. The lean is about the bid's size, not its place in the list, and it survives sampling. At 768 forced tokens on eBay, under pay-your-bid pricing, the wrong answers cost bidders 7-17% of the surplus the calculator's bids earn. Arithmetic errors are rare. Two further losses come from operator choices. A token cap cuts traces short: allowed to stop on their own within 4,096 tokens, five models recover 91-100% of the calculator's lead over a model that does no reasoning. Asking for the answer while the reasoning block is still open misreads it: closing the block first lifts the weakest model from 42% to 84% on eBay and from 24% to 81% on AuctionNet. Finally, an audit that flags bids disagreeing with the trace's own numbers, and overrides them with the calculator's bid, raises the share of the calculator's lead recovered at 768 forced tokens from 42-85% to 84-100% on eBay under nested cross-validation, and the same reader settings transfer from eBay to AuctionNet.

</details>


### [126] [Not Every Input Needs Everything: Unifying Expert Gating, Model Cascades, and Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34112)
*Dat Tat Mai,Hung Tran Quy,Thai Viet Pham,James Jin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述以"先审视再分配"的统一框架整合2,289项研究，横跨token、query、task三个尺度的20个方法族，揭示条件计算在硬选择比例、开销瓶颈与工程防护上的系统性不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体神经网络权重、前沿基础模型及多智能体工作流对每个输入都做统一计算，带来显著的计算、时延与费用开销；而稀疏架构中的token级门控、查询级模型级联与任务级多智能体协同长期在彼此割裂的研究领域中独立发展，导致反复出现的系统失效模式与可迁移的改进方案被遮蔽，缺少统一视角。

Method: 系统综述。从13,884条唯一记录中筛选出2017年至2026年9月发表的2,289项研究，归入20个方法族，并用统一的"两步式：先审视、后分配"(inspect-then-allocate)框架进行跨尺度归纳与比较分析。

Result: 离散硬选择在token、query、task三个尺度的文献中分别占70%、83%、91%；计算开销在每个层级都是首要的运行瓶颈。工程防护措施呈现明显不对称：层内门控优先考虑负载均衡，极少纳入不确定性估计；模型选择与智能体编排依赖置信度阈值和预算规则，却面临校准偏差与级联误差问题。同时，智能体任务委派方向的发文量增长最快，而其路由机制最少被实验性隔离验证，方法学偏倚风险最高。

Conclusion: 条件计算各尺度虽方法异构，但共享可迁移的失效模式与改进方向；未来需在层内门控中引入不确定性估计、在级联与编排中缓解校准偏差与误差级联，并对增长最快的智能体委派路由开展更严格的隔离实验，以降低方法学偏倚。

Abstract: Executing monolithic neural weights, frontier foundation models, or entire multi-agent workflows uniformly on every input incurs substantial computational, latency, and monetary overhead. Conditional computation mitigates this cost by deciding dynamically which internal experts, model endpoints, or specialized agents to invoke per request. Yet token-level gating in sparse neural architectures, query-level model cascades, and task-level multi-agent coordination have largely progressed in disconnected research silos, obscuring recurring system failures and transferable fixes. In this systematic review, we synthesize 2,289 studies published between 2017 and September 2026 (selected from 13,884 unique records) across 20 methodological families under a unified two-step inspect-then-allocate framework. Discrete hard selection accounts for 70%, 83%, and 91% of token-, query-, and task-scale literature respectively, and computational overhead appears as a top operational bottleneck across every layer. Engineering safeguards remain sharply asymmetric: intra-layer gating prioritizes load balancing while rarely incorporating uncertainty estimation, whereas model selection and agent orchestration rely on confidence thresholds and budget rules while confronting miscalibration and cascading errors. At the same time, publication volume is accelerating fastest in agentic task delegation, where routing mechanisms are least often isolated experimentally and methodological bias risk is highest.

</details>


### [127] [Letting Models Manage Their Own Context: Where We Stand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34147)
*Alessio Devoto,Avinash Vem,Tara Safavi,Sanjay Kariyappa,Yu-Hsuan Tseng,Paul Furgale,Ricardo Silveira Cabral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究"模型驱动的上下文管理"（模型通过context API自行决定何时压缩、压缩什么），在NVIDIA开源agent harness \nooa中与harness代为管理的传统压缩策略进行对比，并进一步提出利用KV cache复用以降低编辑开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent harness在上下文窗口填满时代替模型触发压缩（总结历史），模型本身对上下文管理缺乏控制权；作者希望检验让模型自主管理上下文是否更优，并探索与之匹配的推理引擎优化。

Method: 在\nooa中赋予模型context API的控制权，在文档研究与机器学习工程两类任务上、8K–512K token的上下文预算范围内，对比harness驱动与模型驱动两种压缩策略；同时通过复用编辑后未变后缀的KV cache来降低推理开销。

Result: 有效使用context API的模型准确率更高且常用更少输入token，紧凑预算下尤为明显：GPT 6.1在32K预算下OfficeQA Pro v2得分从21%升至56%（近三倍），Kimi输入token最多减少41%。但模型间在API调用频率、压缩力度与起始时机上差异很大，提示词改动会改变压缩时机。KV cache复用使单请求prefill计算最多减少15%，且准确率无可测变化。

Conclusion: 模型驱动的上下文管理优于harness代管式压缩，并指向一个新的协同设计前沿：训练模型自主管理上下文，同时由推理引擎提供KV cache编辑支持。

Abstract: Agent harnesses today manage context on the model's behalf: when the window fills, the harness triggers a compaction that summarizes the history. We study the alternative, \emph{model-driven} context management, in which the model itself decides when and what to compact through a context API. We gave models control over their own context in \nooa, NVIDIA's open source agent harness, and compared the two strategies on document research and machine learning engineering across context budgets from 8K to 512K tokens. Models that use the context API effectively reach higher accuracy, often with fewer input tokens, especially at tight budgets: \textbf{GPT 6.1 nearly triples its score at a 32K budget} on OfficeQA Pro v2 (21\%~$\rightarrow$~56\%), and \textbf{Kimi gains with up to 41\% fewer input tokens}. Models differ widely, however, in how often they use the API, how aggressively they compact, and when they start compacting, and prompt changes shift that timing. Finally, we show that \textbf{inference engines can make model-driven edits cheaper}: by reusing the KV cache of the unchanged suffix after an edit, \textbf{we cut prefill compute by up to 15\% per request with no measurable change in accuracy}. Together, these results point to a new co-design frontier: models trained to manage their own context, served by inference engines that support KV cache editing.

</details>


### [128] [SOLO: Towards Sample-Faithful and Detectable LLM Watermarking under Repeated Sampling via Content-Anchored Single-Owner Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34190)
*Zhongliang Guo,Yan Lin,Yifei Qian,Shuai Zhao,Luwei Xiao,Daizong Liu,Chun Pong Lau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有无失真水印在单条响应内对键控上下文去重，导致同一请求的多条采样响应因答案模板而共享同一水印上下文、碰撞成为常态；作者提出SOLO，以min-hash选定的内容锚定请求级键控上下文，使兄弟响应近似独立且可单密钥检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型服务方需对输出加水印，而应用侧常对同一提示采样多条响应以实现自一致性、best-of-n选择与不确定性估计。已部署的无失真水印仅在单条响应内部对键控上下文去重，答案模板使同一请求的多条响应在进入答案前查询到相同的水印上下文，原本假设罕见的跨响应碰撞变成常态，从而破坏响应间的独立性假设并损害下游多采样应用。

Method: 作者先分析两类补救手段的缺陷：键池（key pools）以多重检验为代价降低或规避耦合；在请求内共享上下文历史则导致大量位置无法被水印覆盖。据此提出SOLO：保留请求级的上下文历史，但将键控上下文锚定在由min-hash选出的内容上，使兄弟响应很少竞争同一上下文，且随机编辑仅产生局部影响；其响应联合分布等价于独立的未加水印响应，每条均可仅用单一密钥检测。

Result: 在来自三个模型族的五个语言模型与三个基准上，SOLO保持了自一致性指标与不确定性估计的性能；在1%假阳性率下，其在多数任务上检测出的响应数多于四词元键池，在随机编辑10%词元后仍如此，并在多数干净任务上优于请求级上下文历史方案。

Conclusion: 请求级的水印耦合是多采样场景下无失真水印的关键缺陷，SOLO通过min-hash锚定的请求级键控上下文，在保持下游任务性能的同时实现近似独立、单密钥可检测且对编辑鲁棒的水印，代码已开源。

Abstract: Providers watermark the outputs of large language models, while applications sample many responses per prompt for self-consistency, best-of-n selection, and uncertainty estimation. We show that deployed configurations of distortion-free watermarks, which deduplicate keyed contexts only within a response, couple the responses of a request: answer templates make all responses query the watermark with the same context before the answer, so collisions across responses, usually assumed rare, become the norm. Key pools reduce or avoid the coupling at the cost of multiple testing, and the exact remedy of a context history shared across the request leaves many positions unwatermarked. We propose SOLO, which keeps a request-scoped context history but anchors its keyed contexts on content selected by min-hash, so that siblings rarely compete for a context and random edits stay local. Its responses are jointly distributed as independent unwatermarked responses, each detectable with a single key. On five language models from three families and three benchmarks, SOLO preserves self-consistency and uncertainty estimation and, at a 1% false positive rate, detects more responses than four-token key pools on most tasks, also after randomly editing 10% of the tokens, and more than the context history on most clean tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/ZhongliangGuo/solo-watermark.

</details>


### [129] [PROVE: Mitigating Long-Horizon LLM Collusion with Reinforcement Learning from Verified Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34215)
*Haochen Luo,Jie Fu,Mukai Li,Yuqi Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM智能体在长程相互验证中会逐渐"合谋"（collusion）：未经证据即接受同伴工作。作者将其归因于记忆中的准确反馈积累——遵守规则的智能体在同伴正确时被判为错误，于是违规成为奖励最大化选择。为此提出PROVE（Protocol Reinforcement from Verified Evidence）多智能体强化学习方法：按裁决背后的证据而非结果给奖励，并将每次调用（含解决自身任务的调用）归因于其所控制的后果。在Qwen3.6-27B上用六回合交互训练的LoRA适配器，将十回合原始时间跨度上的合谋率从65.0%降至1.0%，任务成功率无显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长程相互验证中会逐回合累积性地滑向合谋：在缺乏协议所要求证据的情况下接受同伴的工作。该现象起始时不存在、随后逐步建立，根因被追溯为记忆中准确反馈的累积——当同伴判断正确而遵循规则的智能体给出不同裁决时，它会被反馈"判错"，导致违反规则成为奖励最大化的选择，智能体也在私人反思中自我说服。既有缓解手段不改变智能体本身，仅做外部的流程约束，因此作者希望"把这种行为训练掉"。

Method: PROVE是一种多智能体强化学习方法，核心有两点：(1) 奖励信号依据裁决背后的证据而非裁决的最终结果（outcome）来给分，从而切断"结论碰巧正确即获奖励"的捷径；(2) 对智能体做出的每一次调用进行信用分配（credit assignment），包括解决自身任务的调用，都以其所控制的后果作为奖励依据。实现上在Qwen3.6-27B上训练LoRA适配器，训练数据为六回合（six-episode）的交互序列。

Result: 在留出（held-out）任务序列上、恢复至原始的十回合时间跨度时，合谋率由65.0%降至1.0%，且未检测到任务成功率的显著变化。原先在每一次交互中都会收敛到合谋，训练后在没有任何一次交互中收敛；增益在超出训练所见最长交互四个回合之外仍保持。训练后的27B模型合谋率低于原研究评估的所有模型，包括闭源前沿模型。

Conclusion: 智能体间的合谋是可被训练消除的行为而非不可约的涌现缺陷；以"证据"而非"结果"为奖励基础，并对每次调用做恰当的信用分配，可在极短交互训练下获得强外推的鲁棒性，且不牺牲任务表现。代码已开源于 https://github.com/Haochen-Luo/PROVE。

Abstract: When LLM agents verify each other's work over a long interaction, they drift into collusion: they come to accept work without the evidence that the verification protocol requires. Collusion is absent at the start and builds up episode by episode, which we trace to accurate feedback accumulating in memory. An agent that follows the rule is told that its verdict was wrong whenever its peer was right, so breaking the rule is the reward-maximizing choice, and agents talk themselves into it in their private reflections. Existing mitigations leave the agents unchanged; we train the behavior out. PROVE (Protocol Reinforcement from Verified Evidence) is a multi-agent reinforcement learning method that rewards verdicts by the evidence behind them rather than by their outcome, and credits every call an agent makes, including the calls that solve its own task, with the outcome that call controls. On Qwen3.6-27B, a LoRA adapter trained on six-episode interactions takes the collusion rate on held-out task sequences at the original ten-episode horizon from 65.0% to 1.0%, with no detectable change in task success. Collusion that converged in every interaction now converges in none, and the gain holds four episodes beyond the longest interaction seen in training. The trained 27B model colludes less than every model evaluated in the original study, closed-weight frontier models included. Code is available at https://github.com/Haochen-Luo/PROVE.

</details>


### [130] [Did the Model Forecast It, or Already Know It? Evaluating Large Language Models for Novel Drug-Target Prioritisation with a Temporal Knowledge-Graph Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34247)
*Pui Chung Siu,Yuan Liang,Claudia Cabrera,Mani Mudaliar,Arkaitz Zubiaga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将靶点-疾病假说重构为"首次临床试验"这一带日期的事件，构建逐年快照的生物医学知识图谱，检验六个开放语言模型能否在其训练截止后预测该事件，并揭示时间泄漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI日益参与药物靶点进入昂贵临床试验的决策，但现有评估多为"回忆"训练截止前的事件而非真正预测；需要建立可检验预测能力、且能识别证据时间泄漏的评估框架。

Method: 以靶点-疾病对的首次临床试验作为带日期事件标签；按2020年起每年年末重建带日期的生物医学知识图谱；让六个开放语言模型在有/无图谱检索两种条件下预测下一年将进入首次试验的靶点；以"试验历史"（同治疗领域内该靶点既往试验计数）为基线对比。

Result: 无检索时模型排序能力与试验历史基线相当，且在训练截止后依然如此；决策前日期的证据仅带来小幅提升；最新图谱使六个模型中五个明显超过基线，但其推理路径通常包含答案本身——即决策之后日期的该靶点自身试验记录。

Conclusion: 靶点优先级评估须同时对特征与标签标注日期：特征仅用决策前证据、标签仅用决策后结果；当前看似优异的图谱增强性能很大程度上源于时间泄漏，而非真实预测能力。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly help decide which drug targets enter costly clinical trials. We recast each target-disease hypothesis as a dated event, the pair's first clinical trial. This lets us test whether a language model can forecast such events after its training cutoff, rather than recall events before it. We rebuild a dated biomedical knowledge graph as it stood at the end of each year from 2020 up to its newest version. We then ask six open language models, with and without this graph, which targets will enter a first trial for a disease the next year. Without retrieval, the models rank about as well as trial history, a count of each target's past trials in the same therapeutic area, also after their training cutoffs. Evidence dated before the decision adds at most a small gain, before or after the models' training cutoffs. The newest graph raises five of six models clearly above that baseline, but its paths usually include the answer: the pair's own trial, dated after the decision. Evaluations of target prioritisation should date both features and labels, with evidence from before the decision and outcomes after it.

</details>


### [131] [EXPOSURE: Explaining Sources of Uncertainty in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34248)
*Kiana Farhadyar,Yannick Vogt,Philipp Laur,Ayça Çelik,Iman Nematollahi,Gabriel Kalweit,Maria Kalweit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EXPOSURE 用单一 mask-aware 信念网络同时完成动作选择与 Shapley 解释，将回报方差分解为"缺失特征导致的观测不确定性"与"环境固有随机性"，并解释信念熵，在井字棋和扫雷中给出更准确的回报及其不确定性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习中的 Shapley 值只能解释哪些观测特征驱动智能体的行为与期望回报，却无法解释其"确定性"（不确定性）。要解释确定性，需理解观测特征如何影响回报不确定性的不同来源，而标准 Shapley 值要求对每个可见特征子集都有智能体预测，计算代价高。

Method: 提出 EXPOSURE：使用一个能感知掩码（mask-aware）的信念网络，同时服务于动作选择与 Shapley 解释，从而可对任意可见特征子集快速给出预测。在此基础上计算三类解释：(1) 回报方差的 Shapley 分解，拆为缺失特征造成的观测部分与环境自身随机性造成的固有部分；(2) 对掩码特征信念分布的熵的解释。

Result: 在可精确计算 Shapley 值的井字棋（tic-tac-toe）与扫雷（Minesweeper）环境中，EXPOSURE 对期望回报及其不确定性的解释比基线更准确，并且能够唯一地解释信念不确定性（belief uncertainty）。

Conclusion: 将解释对象从期望回报扩展到回报不确定性与信念不确定性是可行且必要的；共享的 mask-aware 信念网络使子集预测与 Shapley 解释在计算上可扩展，并在可验证的小规模环境中得到精确性验证。

Abstract: Shapley values in reinforcement learning are used to explain which observation features drive an agent's behavior and expected return, but not its certainty. Explaining certainty requires understanding how observation features influence different sources of return uncertainty, and Shapley values need the agent's prediction for every subset of visible features. We propose EXPOSURE, which uses one mask-aware belief network for both action selection and Shapley explanations. It explains the variance of the return, split into an observational part due to missing features and an inherent part due to the environment's own randomness, and the entropy of its belief over the masked features. In tic-tac-toe and Minesweeper, where exact Shapley values are computable, EXPOSURE gives more accurate explanations of expected return and its uncertainty, while uniquely explaining belief uncertainty.

</details>


### [132] [Navigent: A Geometry-Grounded Agentic Framework for Vision Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34249)
*Xinyu Zhou,Weiren Cai,Yitong Chen,Shenghai Yuan,Zihang Mu,Boyu Ma,Jiahua Dong,Yanshuo Lu,Jiaqi Bai,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Navigent 是一个以冻结多模态大模型为核心的智能体式视觉语言导航框架，通过引入几何接地的执行评论器（GEC）对执行过程进行双重一致性校验，并在 R2R-CE / RxR-CE 上取得 84.0%/86.0% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态大模型用于 VLN 时灵活但长时程执行不可靠：智能体难以跟踪导航进度、难以发现指令运动与实际位移的偏差、难以判断是否已越过关键地标或入口。作者希望在不微调模型权重的前提下提升长程执行的可靠性。

Method: 框架由冻结 MLLM 与 Geometry-Grounded Execution Critic（GEC）构成。GEC 执行两项互补检查：(1) action-to-motion 一致性，将实测位移与指令运动比对；(2) motion-to-landmark 一致性，在世界坐标系中跟踪智能体自选的视觉锚点与入口候选。检测到不一致时中断剩余动作序列，触发重观测、局部调整或基于节点的恢复；并用历史视觉记忆支撑长路线上的进度跟踪。此外提出离线自我改进流程，分析成功/失败训练轨迹以精炼导航提示与 GEC 逻辑，模型权重保持固定。

Result: 在 R2R-CE validation-unseen（1,839 回合）上 SR/SPL 为 84.0%/72.9%；在 RxR-CE validation-unseen（3,669 回合）上为 86.0%/69.1%。

Conclusion: 几何接地的执行验证对可靠智能体导航具有显著价值；该路线可在冻结 MLLM 条件下兼顾通用性与执行可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) with general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offers a flexible alternative to task-specific navigation policies, while reliable long-horizon execution remains challenging. Agents must track navigation progress, detect discrepancies between commanded and actual motion, and recognize when movement has passed relevant landmarks or entrances. We present Navigent, an agentic VLN framework built around a frozen MLLM and a Geometry-Grounded Execution Critic (GEC). GEC performs two complementary checks: action-to-motion consistency, which evaluates measured displacement against commanded movement, and motion-to-landmark consistency, which tracks agent-selected visual anchors and entrance candidates in world coordinates. Detected discrepancies interrupt the remaining action sequence and trigger reobservation, local adjustment, or node-based recovery. Historical visual memory supports progress tracking across extended routes. An offline self-improvement procedure further analyzes successful and failed training trajectories to refine navigation prompts and GEC logic while keeping model weights fixed. On the R2R-CE validation-unseen split (1,839 episodes) and RxR-CE validation-unseen split(3,669 episodes), Navigent achieves 84.0%/86.0% SR and 72.9%/69.1% SPL, respectively, demonstrating the value of geometry-grounded execution verification for reliable agentic navigation. Project Page: https://marslab-navigent.github.io/navigent.github.io/ Code: https://github.com/MarsLab-Navigent/Navigent

</details>


### [133] [MedSkill-E$^{2}$: Evaluating and Evolving Skills for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34250)
*Jingjing Hua,Shaocong Wei,Jianghan Shen,Junzhi Ning,Min Cen,Cheng Tang,Ziyan Huang,Jiepeng Li,Fengshuo Liu,Kangkun Mao,Mouxiao Bian,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu,Jiyao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发现LLM智能体在自主执行中即使已获取包含规则与流程的技能，仍可能无法有效应用，称之为"技能可用但未被充分使用"（EBU）；提出MedSkill-E$^{2}$框架与EBU-Bench基准进行量化，并提出EBU-Evolve将执行反馈蒸馏为可复用技能指令，使自主执行下技能使用准确率提升33.33–55.17 pp且可跨模型与框架迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益依赖技能获取领域知识与完成现实任务，但观察到即便技能已明确给出任务所需规则与流程，智能体在自主执行时仍未能有效应用，形成"技能可用但未被充分使用"（EBU）现象。该问题在医疗任务中后果严重——遗漏单个关键约束即可产生貌似合理但错误的结论。因此需要一个框架来评估并缓解EBU。

Method: 提出MedSkill-E$^{2}$框架，用于医疗智能体技能的评价与演进。构建EBU-Bench基准，覆盖12个医学相关任务组及其对应技能，评估智能体自主使用可用技能的能力；将EBU差距量化为"自主使用"与"显式引导阅读并应用同一技能"两种条件下的技能使用准确率之差。在此基础上提出EBU-Evolve，利用自主执行与显式引导两条路径的执行反馈，把有效引导蒸馏进可复用的技能指令中。

Result: 在EBU-Bench上，所有被测的模型–harness组合均表现出EBU，平均差距达43.94个百分点；同一模型在不同harness下自主技能使用准确率差异最高达38.96 pp。经EBU-Evolve演进后的技能相较演进前版本，使自主执行下的技能使用准确率提升33.33–55.17 pp，并在跨多个模型与harness迁移时仍保持增益。

Conclusion: 在模型与技能固定的条件下，自主技能使用准确率随harness变化，且模型排名可能发生改变；EBU在技能被"打开/读取"之后仍可能持续存在；EBU-Evolve能在同一技能内的未见任务上提升自主技能使用准确率，且增益可跨模型与harness迁移。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use skills to access domain knowledge and complete real-world tasks. We observe that even when a skill specifies the rules and procedures needed for a task, agents may fail to apply them effectively during autonomous execution, a phenomenon we call skill Enable But Underuse (EBU). This issue is particularly consequential in medical tasks, where overlooking a single critical constraint can yield a plausible but incorrect result. To evaluate and address EBU, we introduce MedSkill-E$^{2}$, a framework for evaluating and evolving skills for medical agents. We construct EBU-Bench, a challenging benchmark covering 12 medically relevant task groups and associated skills, to evaluate agents' ability to use available skills autonomously. We quantify the EBU gap as the difference in skill-use accuracy between autonomous use and explicitly guided reading and application of the same skill. On EBU-Bench, all evaluated model-harness combinations exhibit EBU, with a mean gap of 43.94 percentage points (pp). For the same model, skill-use accuracy under autonomous use differs by up to 38.96 pp across harnesses. Motivated by these observations, we propose EBU-Evolve, which uses execution feedback from autonomous and explicitly guided skill use to distill effective guidance into reusable skill instructions. Relative to their pre-evolution versions, the revised skills improve skill-use accuracy during autonomous execution by 33.33 to 55.17 pp and continue to yield improvements when transferred across multiple models and harnesses. Our results show that autonomous skill-use accuracy varies across harnesses with the model and skill held fixed, and model rankings can change. EBU can persist even after a skill is opened. EBU-Evolve improves autonomous skill-use accuracy on unseen tasks within the same skill, with gains transferring across models and harnesses.

</details>


### [134] [Crucible: Evaluating LLM Agents in Executable Chemistry Research Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34254)
*Yinjun Liu,Xinye Li,Ruisen Peng,Lang Gao,Zixiang Xu,Zirui Song,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Crucible Bench，覆盖 DMTA 全周期的化学品智能体基准，含 1,078 任务、12 任务族、75 个领域工具与沙箱评分器；14 个 LLM 中最佳仅 0.545，合成规划与闭环优化最难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学知识基准主要考察问答与孤立任务，而真实化学研究需要调用专业工具并解读其输出以获得化学上有效的结果，这一能力缺乏系统评测。

Method: 构建 Crucible Bench：从 18 个已发表数据集精选 1,078 个任务、12 个任务族，覆盖 DMTA 四阶段（分子设计、合成规划、实验执行、闭环优化、模拟、分析表征）；智能体通过 MCP 在 7 个隔离环境中访问 75 个领域专用工具；沙箱评分器校验合成路线、实验协议等输出；实验类任务使用机器人模拟器或已测实验数据；评测 14 个 LLM 智能体并做错误分析。

Result: 最佳模型总分 0.545；合成规划与闭环优化最难；总体性能无法预测优化表现（总分第 12 的模型在闭环优化中得分最高）；错误分析显示智能体虽成功运行工具却常提交化学上无效的结果；更强模型从工具访问中获益更大。

Conclusion: 可靠使用化学软件仍是当前 LLM 的瓶颈，Crucible Bench 可作为跟踪模型能力提升的标准化评测平台。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform well on chemistry knowledge benchmarks, which mainly assess question answering and isolated tasks. Chemical research, however, requires using specialized tools and interpreting their outputs to obtain chemically valid results. To address this gap, we introduce Crucible Bench to evaluate this ability across all four stages of the design-make-test-analyze (DMTA) cycle. The benchmark contains 1,078 tasks in 12 task families curated from 18 published datasets, covering molecular design, synthesis planning, experimental execution, closed-loop optimization, simulation, and analytical characterization. Agents access 75 domain-specific tools through the Model Context Protocol in seven isolated environments. Sandboxed graders verify the resulting synthesis routes, experimental protocols, and other outputs. Laboratory tasks use robotic simulators or previously measured experimental data. We evaluate 14 LLMs as agents and find that the best achieves an overall score of 0.545, with synthesis planning and closed-loop optimization emerging as the hardest domains. Overall performance does not predict optimization performance well: the model ranked twelfth overall achieves the highest score in closed-loop optimization. Error analysis also shows that agents often submit chemically invalid results despite running their tools successfully. Stronger models benefit more from tool access, but reliable use of chemical software remains a challenge that Crucible Bench can track as models improve.

</details>


### [135] [MeterRouter: Budget-Aware Dynamic Routing for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34327)
*Yue Xu,Leikun Yin,Ruiming Min,Yufeng Lai,Justin Andrew Johnson,Sitao Luan,Xiaotong Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出预算感知的动态路由方法 MeterRouter，按查询与给定货币预算逐步合成协作图（选角色、选LLM、定入边与终止），以模仿成功执行图初始化后用改进 GRPO 联合优化；在五个基准、三层 LLM 池上达到最强 MAS 相当精度，成本降低约 82%，并能按预算调节开销且很少超支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统虽优于单智能体，但智能体间通信带来大量 token 开销；给每个智能体都配大模型导致资源利用低效，整体远离帕累托前沿。查询成本由招募的智能体数量、各智能体的角色与所配 LLM、以及通信拓扑共同决定，而现有路由器多把成本隐性处理，缺乏显式优化约束；固定的精度-成本偏好也无法表达调用方对特定查询的付费意愿。

Method: MeterRouter 为预算感知的动态路由器：以查询与每查询货币预算为条件，增量式合成协作图，每步选择智能体的角色、LLM、入边连接及是否终止；先用对成功执行图的模仿学习初始化，再用 Group Relative Policy Optimization 的变体联合精修决策，奖励鼓励正确答案、惩罚花费与预算超支。

Result: 在五个基准与三层 LLM 池上：(1) 精度上达到与最强 MAS 可比水平；(2) 经济性上以比最强 MAS 路由器低 82% 的成本取得该精度，并把精度-成本前沿拓展到单智能体系统上限之外；(3) 可控性上能随指定预算调整支出且很少超支。

Conclusion: 把货币预算作为显式条件与优化约束进行逐查询的图级路由，可在保持强 MAS 精度的同时显著压缩 token 成本并拓展精度-成本帕累托前沿，且支出对预算具备可调、可控性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models (LLMs) outperform single-agent systems, but inter-agent communication incurs substantial token overhead. While assigning a large LLM to every agent is common, it results in inefficient resource utilization, leaving the system far from the Pareto frontier. The cost of answering a query is set jointly by how many agents are recruited, the role and LLM assigned to each, and communication topology among them. Most existing routers treat cost only implicitly rather than as an explicit optimization constraint. A fixed accuracy-cost preference also cannot express how much a caller is willing to pay for a particular query. We introduce MeterRouter, a budget-aware dynamic router that incrementally synthesizes a collaboration graph by selecting each agent's role, LLM, incoming connections, and termination conditioned on the query and a per-query monetary budget. We initialize the router by imitating successful execution graphs, then jointly refine its decisions with a variant of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) whose reward favors correct answers and penalizes spending and budget overruns. Across five benchmarks and a three-tier LLM pool, MeterRouter is (1) accurate, achieving performance comparable to the strongest MAS; (2) economical, reaching that accuracy at 82% lower cost than the strongest MAS router and extending the accuracy-cost frontier beyond the ceiling of single-agent systems; and (3) controllable, adapting its spending to the specified budget while rarely exceeding it.

</details>


### [136] [EvoLink-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Self-Evolving Agents in Long-Horizon Interdependent Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34400)
*Wei Chen,Xiaodong Lu,Weihao Shen,Gongxun Li,Xingyu Guo,Qihang Sun,Fuwei Zhang,Zhao Zhang,Yikun Ban,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EvoLink-Bench 基准，用于在长时程、任务间相互依赖的流式任务中评估自演化 LLM 智能体的经验复用能力；实验表明无框架在知识层与能力层均稳定最优，持久状态更新未必带来后续性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体正从被动聊天机器人转向在动态环境中持续运行的自主系统，需要从历史交互中学习以改进未来表现。但现有流式基准多为短任务序列或高度独立的任务，难以证明后续任务依赖前序结果时智能体能否有效复用经验，因此需要专门评测"长时程+任务间依赖"下的自演化能力。

Method: 构建 EvoLink-Bench 基准，覆盖日常办公、多轮对话与 AutoResearch 类工作流，采用跨任务持久化工作区与依赖结构。设置两个互补层级：L1 评估可语言化知识的获取与修正，L2 通过执行与反馈评估任务级能力发展。评测维度区分任务内恢复、持久状态更新与后续任务中的经验复用。在 3 种持久化条件下，对 5 个智能体框架 × 3 种骨干配置进行评估，统计 19,635 个计分任务槽。

Result: 没有任何框架在两个层级上均持续最优；重试仅能挽回有限比例的失败任务；持久状态更新并不必然改善后续表现；演化效应在知识层与能力层任务间存在差异；性能增益随领域与任务流变化，部分任务的提升无法迁移到其他任务。

Conclusion: 知识获取与能力发展两类演化可能需要不同的学习机制；现有智能体框架的"自演化"能力在长时程依赖场景下仍不充分，持久化与重试等机制的效果被高估，后续研究需针对跨任务经验迁移设计更专门的学习与记忆机制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are evolving from passive chatbots into autonomous systems that operate continuously in dynamic environments. Such agents need not only solve current tasks but also learn from past interactions to improve future performance. However, existing streaming benchmarks often rely on short task sequences or largely independent tasks, providing limited evidence of whether agents can effectively reuse experience when later tasks depend on earlier outcomes. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{EvoLink-Bench}, a benchmark for evaluating self-evolving agents in long-horizon, interdependent task streams. It covers daily office work, multi-turn dialogue, and AutoResearch-style workflows, with persistent workspaces and dependencies across tasks. The benchmark includes two complementary levels: \textbf{L1}, which evaluates the acquisition and revision of language-expressible knowledge, and \textbf{L2}, which assesses the development of task-level capabilities through execution and feedback. Its evaluation further distinguishes within-task recovery, persistent-state updates, and experience reuse in later tasks. We evaluate five agent frameworks across three backbone configurations under three persistence conditions, covering 19,635 scored task slots from selected evaluation cohorts. Results show that no framework consistently performs best across both levels, retries recover only a limited proportion of failed tasks, and persistent-state updates do not necessarily improve subsequent performance. Furthermore, the effects of evolution differ between knowledge- and capability-level tasks, suggesting that these two forms of evolution require different learning mechanisms. Performance gains also vary across domains and task streams, with improvements on some tasks not necessarily extending to others.

</details>


### [137] [Agentic Waterfall: An Intent-Driven Information Discovery Framework for Reclaiming User Control in the Attention Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34407)
*Yige Luo,Shining Zhang,Jikun Liu,Hongjiao Qiang,Ran Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文形式化提出"Agentic Waterfall Feed"这一新型个性化信息发现任务，并给出 Agentic Waterfall 框架、数据集与回放式评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统多是被动式反馈学习，难以支持用户主动下达指令、且需求随时间演化（由显式请求与浏览行为共同体现）的场景，缺少相应的问题定义与评测方法。

Method: 提出 Agentic Waterfall 框架：由语言模型控制器维护持久化的用户状态，并协调内容检索、排序与状态更新；同时构建 Agentic Waterfall Dataset（真实内容元数据 + 模拟交互历史）与基于回放的评测协议，在共享历史下用相关性分数与盲评判断推荐是否满足当前目标与约束。

Result: 在五个公开基准的传统推荐任务上与强基线相当；在 Agentic Waterfall Dataset 上两项评测均优于最强基线，相对提升最高达 16.1%。

Conclusion: 将"用户主动引导 + 系统自适应"纳入统一框架与可复现评测是可行的，语言模型控制器在个性化信息发现中具备竞争力，并为后续研究提供了任务、数据与协议基础。

Abstract: We formalize Agentic Waterfall Feed, a new problem of personalized information discovery in which users actively direct recommendations and the system adapts to evolving needs inferred from both explicit requests and browsing behavior. We address this problem with the Agentic Waterfall framework, whose language-model controller maintains persistent user state and coordinates content retrieval, ranking, and state updates. To evaluate systems on this task, we contribute the Agentic Waterfall Dataset and a replay-based evaluation protocol. The dataset combines real content metadata with simulated interaction histories. The protocol compares systems under shared histories using relevance scores and blind judgments of how well recommendations satisfy current user goals and constraints. Agentic Waterfall remains competitive with strong baselines on conventional recommendation tasks across five public benchmarks. On the Agentic Waterfall Dataset, it outperforms the strongest baseline in both evaluations, with relative improvements of up to 16.1%.

</details>


### [138] [Evaluating Aviation Safety Knowledge Extraction with SmolLM2: From Zero-Shot to Retrieval-Grounded Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34432)
*Aziida Nanyonga,Mustafa Tahsin Yilmaz,Amir Hussain,Ugur Turhan,Graham Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在300份NTSB航空事故叙述上系统评估SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct在零样本、单样本、固定少样本与检索增强提示下的知识抽取表现，从概念准确性、逐字证据溯源、结构合规性和有向关系抽取四个维度综合评价，发现无单一提示策略全面最优，且关系抽取是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空事故叙述蕴含结构化事件字段难以捕捉的安全知识，需要评估提示式知识抽取方法在该场景下的真实能力边界，并明确小模型在各抽取维度上的短板。

Method: 构建含3,763个参考概念与467条有向关系的数据集（2000–2020年300份NTSB叙述），对SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct施加四种提示策略（零样本、单样本、固定少样本、检索增强），评估结合结构检查、逐字证据匹配、专家复核语义匹配与配对bootstrap置信区间；另以35份基准样本对Qwen3-8B做探索性对比。

Result: 无提示策略在所有维度均最优。检索增强概念micro-F1最高（32.27%），但相对固定少样本的优势不确定（95% CI：-0.92至2.40个百分点）；单样本关系F1最高（6.95%），关系抽取为主要瓶颈。相较固定少样本，检索增强提升逐字溯源率（59.45% vs. 54.02%）但降低结构完全合规率（33.3% vs. 42.7%）。探索性对比中Qwen3-8B在单样本下概念micro-F1达51.37%、关系F1达34.62%，优于SmolLM2。

Conclusion: 逐字匹配仅证明证据来源存在而非证据相关性；航空事故知识抽取应分别评估语义正确性、来源可追溯性、结构可靠性与有向关系准确性，且小模型在有向关系抽取上仍需专门改进。

Abstract: Aviation accident narratives contain safety knowledge that is difficult to capture using structured occurrence fields alone. This study evaluates prompting-based knowledge extraction across four dimensions: concept accuracy, verbatim evidence grounding, structural compliance, and directed relation extraction. SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct was evaluated under zero-shot, one-shot, fixed few-shot, and retrieval-grounded prompting using 300 National Transportation Safety Board narratives from 2000-2020, containing 3,763 reference concepts and 467 directed relations. Evaluation combined structural checks, verbatim evidence matching, expert-reviewed semantic matching, and paired bootstrap confidence intervals. No prompting strategy performed best across all dimensions. Retrieval-grounded prompting achieved the highest concept micro-F1 of 32.27%, although its advantage over fixed few-shot prompting was uncertain (95% CI: -0.92 to 2.40 percentage points). One-shot prompting achieved the highest relation F1 of 6.95%, identifying directed relation extraction as the principal SmolLM2 bottleneck. Compared with fixed few-shot prompting, retrieval improved verbatim grounding (59.45% vs. 54.02%) but reduced complete structural compliance (33.3% vs. 42.7%). Verbatim matching establishes source presence rather than evidential relevance. An exploratory comparison using 35 benchmark narratives showed that Qwen3-8B achieved higher concept micro-F1 across all prompting conditions and higher relation F1 under demonstration-based prompting, reaching 51.37% and 34.62%, respectively, under one-shot prompting. The small subset limits generalization. These findings support evaluating semantic correctness, source traceability, structural reliability, and directed relation accuracy separately.

</details>


### [139] [vSFT: Learning to Verbally Tune Agents at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34440)
*Qishuai Wen,Rui Chen,Haiwen Li,Zhiyuan Huang,Qiwen Gu,Bingjie Gao,Geng Li,Jing Tang,Xiangxiang Chu,Chun-Guang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 vSFT 框架：用超网络把监督者针对失败轨迹生成的自然语言反馈转换为低秩权重更新，实现测试时的参数级自进化，优于上下文内反馈与主流强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于策略的智能体强化学习虽能提升大模型智能体，但 rollout 代价高、奖励稀疏，难以在测试时应用；而上下文内的自然语言反馈虽稠密却无法改变权重。作者希望把「稠密语言反馈」与「测试时权重更新」结合。

Method: vSFT 构建智能体—监督者—超网络的闭环：监督者依据智能体失败轨迹生成语言化监督信号，超网络将其映射为低秩权重更新并注入智能体参数，从而在测试时以参数更新的方式持续调优。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 上，基于 Qwen3-4B 的 vSFT 成功率较 GRPO 与 Reflexion 分别高 8.7% 和 13.9%；在 WebShop 上分别高 4.8% 和 18.2%。同时优于其上下文内对应方法。

Conclusion: 测试时的参数内进化优于上下文内反馈与强化学习基线；作者认为「学会如何自适应模型」而非「得到一个已自适应的模型」，是构建可自我提升智能体的可行路径。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly improved via agentic reinforcement learning, which can be on-policy but suffers from expensive rollouts and sparse rewards, limiting its application at test time. In this paper, we present a verbally Supervised Fine-Tuning (vSFT) framework that enables the conjunction of dense feedback in natural language and weight updates at test time. Specifically, our vSFT forms a closed loop between an agent and a supervisor via a hypernetwork, in which the supervisor generates verbal supervision from the agent's failed trajectories, and the hypernetwork converts that supervision into low-rank weight updates to tune the agent. We validate that this test-time in-parameter evolving process outperforms both its in-context counterparts and prevalent reinforcement learning methods. In particular, our vSFT using Qwen3-4B outperforms GRPO and Reflexion by 8.7% and 13.9% in success rate on ALFWorld, and by 4.8% and 18.2% on WebShop, respectively. We believe that learning how to adapt a model, rather than an adapted model, offers a promising path towards more capable agents that improve themselves.

</details>


### [140] [EgoUseBench: Benchmarking Computer-Use Agents under Evolving First-Person Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34466)
*Yurun Chen,Biao Yi,Qihang Yu,Keting Yin,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ego Use 交互范式与 EgoUseBench 基准，基于 74 小时真实第一人称录像构建 693 个任务，覆盖 Computer-Use、Offline Ego Use、Online Ego Use 三种设置，揭示模型在动态演化的第一人称语境下成功率平均下降 26.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 可穿戴设备（如智能眼镜）使智能体的上下文从数字环境延伸到用户的物理环境，任务可由用户正在进行的活动自然产生，而非仅依赖显式指令。但第一人称观测受佩戴者视角限制，证据可能不完整或歧义，随着新观测到达，智能体需修订对物理情境与用户意图的理解，并重新评估既有数字操作是否仍然有效。现有评测尚未充分考察智能体能否可靠应对这些动态变化。

Method: 提出 EgoUseBench 基准，按第一人称上下文程度划分三种交互设置：Computer-Use（仅显式指令）、Offline Ego Use（执行前提供预录第一人称上下文）、Online Ego Use（执行中持续提供观测且用户意图演化）。基于 74 小时以上真实第一人称录像构建 693 个任务；将第一人称观测、用户需求与网站状态分别跟踪以刻画任务演化；并按信息变化方式将任务分为 Graft（用户需求被修订）、Refresh（出现新的第一人称观测）、Clarify（智能体主动求助信息）三类。

Result: 对先进模型的评测显示，Online Ego Use 的平均成功率比 Computer-Use 低 26.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，使任务执行持续对齐不断变化的第一人称上下文与用户意图具有显著难度；EgoUseBench 为评估可穿戴场景下的智能体能力提供了系统化测试平台。

Abstract: AI wearables, such as smart glasses, extend the context available to agents beyond digital environments to users' physical surroundings. With these devices, computer tasks can originate from users' ongoing activities rather than solely from explicit instructions. Through first-person observations, agents can identify objects and activities relevant to user requests and ground digital tasks in the surrounding context. We call this interaction paradigm Ego Use. However, first-person observations are limited by the wearer's viewpoint and may provide incomplete or ambiguous evidence of ongoing activities. As new observations arrive, agents may need to revise their understanding of the physical context and user intent, and reassess whether prior digital actions remain valid. Whether current agents can reliably handle these changes remains insufficiently evaluated. We introduce EgoUseBench, a benchmark for evaluating Ego Use across three interaction settings with different levels of first-person context. Computer-Use relies on explicit instructions, Offline Ego Use provides prerecorded first-person context before execution, and Online Ego Use provides continuous observations as user intent evolves. Based on over 74 hours of real-world first-person recordings, we construct 693 tasks across the three interaction settings. To capture task evolution, we track first-person observations, user requirements, and website state separately. We further group tasks by how information changes during execution, resulting in three types: Graft (revised user requirements), Refresh (new first-person observations), and Clarify (agent-initiated information seeking). We evaluate advanced models and find that average success rates in Online Ego Use are 26.3% lower than in Computer-Use. This result reveals the difficulty of keeping task execution aligned with evolving first-person context and user intent.

</details>


### [141] [On Utilization of Edge-AI for condition monitoring towards predictive maintenance: A use case in dairy processing using explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34563)
*Rahul Umesh Mhapsekar,Norah O'Shea,Omer Ali,Steven Davy,Lizy Abraham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于边缘AI的旋转阀状态监测框架，利用低功耗IIoT传感器节点采集振动数据并在边缘设备端处理，为乳品加工中试装置的预测性维护奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网与人工智能推动工业4.0，制造业亟需提升生产率、自动化与效率；旋转阀等关键设备的故障会造成非计划停机，而云端集中处理存在时延与带宽瓶颈，因此需要面向预测性维护的边缘端状态监测方案。

Method: 在乳品加工中试装置的旋转阀上部署低功耗IIoT传感器节点采集振动信号，将数据在边缘计算设备上就地处理，构建Edge-AI状态监测框架（AI模型运行于边缘端，减少上传与延迟）。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化结果；该框架作为通往预测性维护的一步，验证了"低功耗IIoT振动采集 + 边缘侧AI处理"在真实乳品工艺流程设备上实现状态监测的可行性。

Conclusion: Edge-AI结合低功耗IIoT传感可为工业旋转设备提供可落地的状态监测路径，并为后续预测性维护与工业4.0智能制造应用提供参考；未来需在故障诊断精度与部署成本上进一步验证。

Abstract: The Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) are driving Industry 4.0, enhancing productivity, automation, and efficiency in manufacturing systems. This study develops an Edge-AI-based condition monitoring framework, as a step towards predictive maintenance, for a rotary valve in a dairy processing pilot plant, using vibration data collected via a low-power IIoT sensor node and processed on an edge device.

</details>


### [142] [Procedure Inference as Memory: Learning Recruiter Decision Procedures from OKF-Structured Episodes in Multi-Session Hiring Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34576)
*Praveen Kumar Bodigutla,Yizhi Zhang,Sai Vivek Kanaparthy,Arup De*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出OKF索引的情景记忆与过程记忆（Procedure Memory），用于多会话招聘助手，在偏好重叠场景下显著优于全历史上下文与会话检索，且大幅减少上下文token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多会话招聘助手需跨项目、跨时间推断招聘者不断演变的偏好与决策模式。现有方法（无记忆、全历史上下文、会话检索）在长时程、多项目场景下要么丢失时间相关性、要么上下文开销过大，因此需要结构化的情景记忆与可复用的决策程序。

Method: 1) 提出Open Knowledge Format (OKF) 索引的情景记忆，将交互组织为事件并通过lineage检索相关事件；2) 提出Procedure Memory，通过prompted reflection或强化学习从情景中学习可复用的决策程序；3) 以无记忆、全历史上下文、会话检索为基线，评估Jev类模型负责索引导航，GPT-5.6 Luna作为GPT-5.4的低成本替代。评估含定性分析、长时程任务及PersonaMem-v2基准。

Result: 在中等与高偏好重叠下，OKF索引检索的低优先级偏好F1达0.93–0.95，显著优于全历史上下文与会话检索，高重叠时优于无记忆，且比全历史少用69%上下文token。Procedure Memory使对话轮次最多减少39%。RL学到的程序在正确弃答与精确字段识别上均达60%。Jev类模型提升对话效率但更易漏掉时间相关证据。PersonaMem-v2上，Procedure Memory+OKF检索准确率50.2%（全历史48.6%，无记忆29.0%），reader输入token比全历史少90.5%。GPT-5.6 Luna以更低成本达到与GPT-5.4相当的性能。

Conclusion: 结构化情景记忆与学习式决策程序能有效支撑高效个性化与时间推理；证据选择（而非单纯索引或对话效率）是时间推理的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Multi-session hiring assistants must infer evolving recruiter preferences and decision patterns across projects and time. We introduce Open Knowledge Format (OKF)-indexed episodic memory to organize interactions and retrieve related events through their lineage. We also introduce Procedure Memory, which learns reusable decision procedures from these episodes through prompted reflection or reinforcement learning. We compare these approaches with no memory, full-history context, and conversation search, and evaluate Jev-class models for index navigation and GPT-5.6 Luna as a lower-cost alternative to GPT-5.4.
  In our evaluation, OKF-indexed retrieval achieves low-priority preference F1 scores of 0.93-0.95 at medium and high preference overlap between hiring projects. It significantly outperforms full-history context and conversation search in both settings, and no memory at high overlap, while using 69% fewer context tokens than full history. Procedure Memory reduces conversational turns by up to 39% relative to no memory. Qualitative and long-horizon evaluations provide evidence of temporal reasoning, with RL-learned procedures achieving 60% on both correct abstention and exact-field identification. Jev-class models improve conversational efficiency but more frequently miss temporally relevant evidence, highlighting the importance of evidence selection.
  On PersonaMem-v2, Procedure Memory with OKF retrieval achieves 50.2% accuracy versus 48.6% for full-history context and 29.0% for no memory, using 90.5% fewer reader input tokens than full history. GPT-5.6 Luna performs comparably to GPT-5.4 at lower cost. These findings highlight the value of structured episodic memory and learned decision procedures for efficient personalization and temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [143] [Do We Really Need an LLM for Weight-Space Programming? A Deterministic Forward-Only Paradigm and Its Capability Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34612)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Forward Weight-Space Programming（FWSP）：一套确定性、仅前向、无需LLM优化器的权重编辑流水线，并给出Protected FisherGate零成本读出编辑方法，在多模态与多模型上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Weight-Space Programming将"用LLM脚本化编辑"与"仅前向编辑的底层代数"混为一谈，默认需要LLM优化器。作者认为在核心结构化编辑场景中LLM优化器并非必需，且LLM盲路由效率低、推理模型编辑缺乏低成本方案。

Method: FWSP确定性流水线：探测（probing）+定位（localization）+分层算子O1–O4+自动盲路由+配对验证（零额外逐token计算）。针对推理LLM提出Protected FisherGate：闭式协方差白化的读出编辑，与正常推理状态正交。以冻结的数学校准补丁进行编辑，并分析算子边界（子空间纠缠、冻结表示张量空间）。

Result: 盲路由准确率83.3%（27B LLM同工具库仅33.3%）；DeepSeek-R1-0528-8B上单次冻结补丁达84.2% HumanEval pass@1（1K预算），3/5 Stage-4子集为75.4±9.4%，较512-token预算forcing提升14.6pp且省23.4% token，并可跨HF→vLLM后端迁移，在GSM8K与MATH-500上有效。覆盖5种模态、13个模型（50K–27B稠密与FP8 MoE）。

Conclusion: 核心结构化编辑无需LLM优化器，确定性前向编辑可媲美甚至超越LLM驱动方案；FWSP定位的LoRA可桥接仅前向编辑与梯度适配两类范式。

Abstract: Recent work on Weight-Space Programming uses LLMs to edit neural weights with forward passes only, conflating LLM scripting with the underlying algebra of forward-only editing. We show that for core structured editing regimes, an LLM optimizer is unnecessary, and introduce Forward Weight-Space Programming (FWSP): a deterministic pipeline combining probing, localization, stratified operators (O1-O4), automatic blind routing (83.3% vs. 33.3% for a 27B LLM with the same tool library), and paired verification with zero additional per-token computation. For reasoning LLMs, we propose Protected FisherGate, a closed-form covariance-whitened readout edit orthogonal to normal-reasoning states: on DeepSeek-R1-0528-8B, a single frozen math-calibrated patch achieves 84.2% HumanEval pass@1 (1K budget; 75.4 +/- 9.4% across 3/5 Stage-4-passing subsets), beating 512-token-reserved budget forcing by +14.6 pp (-23.4% tokens) while transferring across HF-to-vLLM backends on full GSM8K and MATH-500. Across five modalities and thirteen models (50K to 27B dense and FP8 MoE), we further prove and verify two operator boundaries, subspace entanglement and frozen representation span, where FWSP-localized LoRA bridges forward-only and gradient adaptation.

</details>


### [144] [HoMe: Toward Practical Harness Optimization of Memory Systems for AI Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34624)
*Yifei Xu,Li Yu,Zhaoyang Liu,Ce Wang,Sen Huang,Yanyan Shen,Yun Lin,Linpeng Huang,Hong Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 HoMe，一个以评测驱动的记忆系统 harness 自动优化框架，通过迭代评测候选 harness 并对其进行任意部位编辑，在 LongMemEval-S 与 BEAM 上以更少查询 token 超越原始强 harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行 AI 助手的记忆系统行为由 harness（记忆构建、维护、检索、答案生成的代码、提示与配置）决定，但 harness 优化目前基本依赖人工，且无约束的随意编辑并不能保证结构上的改进。

Method: HoMe 采用迭代式“评测—派生”循环：每个新候选 harness 可从任意已评测 harness 派生，编辑其任意部分。将会话级记忆版本化与执行轨迹结合，以识别跨案例的共性失效机制，并定位到责任会话与 harness 组件；通过候选门控促进早期结构探索，并用冒烟测试、自适应采样与已构建记忆复用降低评测成本。

Result: 在开源记忆系统 ReMe 上，于 LongMemEval-S 与 BEAM 两个大规模长程记忆基准验证；每个基准仅进行六次正式候选评测（在完成一次完整初始评测后），即发现相比原始强 harness 端任务性能更优、且查询时 token 消耗显著更低的 harness。

Conclusion: harness 优化可被自动化并带来实质收益：HoMe 以有限的评测预算实现性能提升与推理成本下降的双重改进，代码已开源于 ReMe 仓库。

Abstract: The behavior of memory systems for long-running AI assistants is governed by a harness, which consists of the code, prompts, and configurations for memory construction, maintenance, retrieval, and answer generation. However, optimizing the harness of a memory system remains largely manual, and unrestricted editing alone does not ensure structural improvements. In this paper, we introduce HoMe, an evaluation-driven framework that iteratively evaluates candidate harnesses and derives each new candidate from any previously evaluated harness by editing any part of it. HoMe combines session-level memory versioning with execution traces to identify shared failure mechanisms across cases and localize them to the responsible sessions and harness components. It encourages early structural exploration through candidate gating and reduces evaluation cost through smoke tests, adaptive sampling, and the reuse of constructed memory. We validate HoMe with the open-source memory system ReMe on two large-scale long-horizon memory benchmarks, LongMemEval-S and BEAM. With six formal candidate evaluations per benchmark, after a full initial evaluation, HoMe discovers harnesses that improve end-task performance over the originally strong harness while consuming far fewer query-time tokens. The code is available at https://github.com/agentscope-ai/ReMe.

</details>


### [145] [ZeroHero: Training Small Language Agents from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34635)
*Nikita Sorokin,Zoya Volovikova,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr Panov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ZeroHero 框架，从零训练参数量小于 5000 万的小型语言智能体（SLA），在多个交互环境中接近甚至超越大型专家模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练语言模型虽可作为交互任务的策略，但专用语言智能体究竟需要多少模型容量尚不清楚，作者希望探究小模型是否足以胜任专门化的多步交互任务。

Method: 1）在每个环境中以大型语言模型为专家，采集其成功轨迹；2）预训练参数量 <50M 的 SLA，基于交互历史预测专家回复；3）用 PPO 在智能体自身交互上进行后训练；4）后训练阶段引入"推理对齐"，在环境奖励之外用专家监督约束智能体随动作生成的语言。

Result: 在 Crafter、WebShop、Alfworld-Dialogue 三个需理解与生成语言的多步环境中评估：WebShop 上接近专家表现；Crafter 训练回放和 Alfworld-Dialogue 留出任务上超越各自的演示专家。

Conclusion: 专用语言智能体无需大模型容量，通过专家轨迹预训练、PPO 后训练与推理对齐，小于 50M 参数的小模型即可在语言交互任务中达到甚至超越大型专家水平，为轻量化语言智能体训练提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Large pretrained language models provide capable policies for interactive tasks, but how much model capacity is necessary for a specialized language agent? We introduce ZeroHero, a framework for training small language agents (SLAs) from scratch. In each environment, a large language model acts as the expert, and we collect its successful trajectories. We pretrain each SLA, which has fewer than 50 million parameters, to predict the expert's responses given the interaction history. We then post-train the agent on its own interactions using proximal policy optimization (PPO). During post-training, reasoning alignment supplements environment rewards with expert supervision on the language the agent generates alongside its actions. We evaluate the agents in three multistep environments where acting requires both understanding and generating language. In Crafter, agents interpret textual observations and generate reasoning before acting. In WebShop, they translate shopping instructions into search queries. In Alfworld-Dialogue, they ask clarifying questions and use the answers to guide household actions. The agents approach expert performance on WebShop and surpass their respective demonstration experts on Crafter training rollouts and held-out Alfworld-Dialogue tasks. An interactive demo is available at https://zoyav.github.io/-ZoyaV-zerohero-demo.

</details>


### [146] [REE: A Scientific Research Entrance Exam for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34646)
*Zeqi Zhou,Minghao Liu,Jiarui Liu,Qingcheng Zeng,Rui Yang,Tianyu Liu,Zetao Yang,Wenqiang Zu,Enduo Zhao,Heng-Jui Chang,Lun-Hsin Kuo,Meng-Fu Chen,Haokai Zhao,Weijia Zhang,Jen-Tse Huang,Stephen Huang,Eli Cheung,Zhijing Jin,Mona Diab,Weihao Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 REE 基准，用 100 个来自活跃研究的开放性问题（5 大科学领域、23 个子领域），在统一尺度上衡量自主研究智能体能否在 12 小时内超越专家方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究智能体可通过反复实验改进方案，有望用于跨学科科学问题求解；但评估这一能力需要既能提出开放式研究问题、又能对照既有科学方法衡量改进幅度的基准。

Method: 构建 REE（Research Entrance Exam）基准：100 个开放研究问题，覆盖 5 大科学领域、23 个子领域，每个问题取自该领域活跃研究并由领域专家逐一审核。任务形式为迭代改进——智能体在至多 12 小时内改进一个可运行的起始方案，结果在与专家方案可比的条件下按统一尺度评分。

Result: 在评估的 4 个前沿智能体中，Claude Opus 5.5 与 GPT-6.1 Sol 分别在 86% 和 81% 的问题上超过专家方案，且覆盖全部 5 个科学领域；二者平均在最初两小时内即超过专家解，最佳方案多为对领域既有方法的裁剪或组合。

Conclusion: REE 为自主科学研究提供了统一评估框架，既可衡量相对既有方法的改进，也能刻画其背后的研究过程（超越速度、方法来源与策略特征）。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents are emerging as a promising approach to scientific problem-solving across disciplines, owing to their growing ability to improve a solution through repeated experiments. Evaluating this ability requires benchmarks that pose open-ended research problems and measure improvements over established scientific methods. We introduce REE (Research Entrance Exam), a benchmark that tests AI agents on scientific research. It consists of 100 open-ended research problems spanning five broad scientific areas and 23 domains. To ensure high quality, every problem is drawn from active research in its field and individually reviewed by domain experts. Each problem is posed as a task of iterative improvement: an agent has up to 12 hours to improve a working starter solution, and REE scores the result on a common scale against an established expert solution evaluated under comparable conditions. By this measure, two of the four frontier agents we evaluate, Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6.1 Sol, outperform the expert solutions on 86% and 81% of problems, across all five scientific areas. Their runs also show how they do so: both agents surpass the expert solutions within the first two hours on average, and their best solutions mostly tailor or combine established methods of the field. REE thus provides a unified framework for evaluating autonomous scientific research, capturing both improvements over established methods and the research processes behind them.

</details>


### [147] [A Failure-Driven Data Flywheel for Post-Training Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34698)
*Yuhao Luo,Zihao Sheng,Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Boyue Wang,Ruolin Yang,Shuai Wang,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"失败驱动的数据飞轮"用于VLA模型后训练：通过向重建真实场景中插入任务相关实体构造目标场景，按行为需求评估并生成失败地图，迭代地为失败条件分配更大数据生成配额；在自动驾驶遮挡行人/停车让行任务上显著提升安全指标并验证于实车。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型难以同时满足新平台的全部行为需求，而缺乏大规模部署车队数据的团队无法定位和修复这些失败，需要一种在有限数据下定向补齐短板的后训练范式。

Method: 构建失败驱动的数据飞轮：1）在重建的真实场景中插入任务相关实体，合成数据中缺失的目标场景（如遮挡行人、停止标志路口）；2）按目标任务的每项行为要求逐条评估模型，统计各任务条件与难度下的失败率形成"失败地图"；3）每轮后重新评估，对仍失败的条件分配更大的数据生成配额。在仿真与实车上用两个VLA模型（AutoVLA、ReCogDrive）和一个非VLA规划器验证。

Result: 遮挡行人场景：5轮后训练使封闭测试集关键阶段无碰撞率由0.261升至0.579（AutoVLA）、0.390升至0.765（ReCogDrive），配对对照场景不必要制动率≤0.013；未用真实数据训练的实车上ReCogDrive达成5次中4次安全通行（基线1/5）。细粒度评估显示两模型学会了遮挡车前预见性减速，但行人出现后的制动仍有限。停车让行：停线前停车率AutoVLA 0.100→0.897、ReCogDrive 0.000→0.667；从行人能力模型顺序训练后分别达0.599与0.358，行人无碰撞率最大仅下降0.032。

Conclusion: 失败驱动、按条件配额的数据生成闭环可在无大规模实车数据条件下有效提升VLA模型的多项行为合规性，并能兼顾安全与新能力习得；但细粒度评估揭示聚合指标掩盖的能力不均衡（如行人出现后的制动不足），且顺序训练存在轻微能力遗忘。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models rarely satisfy all behavioral requirements of a new platform, and teams without a large deployed fleet lack the data to address the resulting failures. We propose a failure-driven data flywheel for post-training such models. It constructs target scenarios absent from the available data by inserting task-relevant entities into reconstructed real-world scenes, and evaluates the model against each behavioral requirement of the target task. Failure rates form a failure map across task conditions and difficulty levels, which is re-evaluated after each round and assigns larger data generation quotas to conditions where the model still fails. We instantiate the framework in autonomous driving for occluded-pedestrian emergence and yielding at stop-sign-controlled intersections, and evaluate it on two VLA models and one non-VLA planner in simulation and on a real vehicle. For occluded-pedestrian emergence, five rounds of post-training improve the critical-phase collision-free rate on the sealed test set from 0.261 to 0.579 for AutoVLA and from 0.390 to 0.765 for ReCogDrive, while the unnecessary braking rate in paired control scenarios never exceeds 0.013. On a full-scale vehicle without real-world training, post-trained ReCogDrive achieves 4 of 5 safe passages, versus 1 of 5 for the base model. Fine-grained evaluation reveals a pattern obscured by the collision-free rate alone: both VLA models learn anticipatory deceleration before reaching the occluding vehicle, while braking after pedestrian emergence remains limited. For stop-sign yielding, the stop-before-line rate rises from 0.100 to 0.897 for AutoVLA and from 0.000 to 0.667 for ReCogDrive. With sequential training from the pedestrian-capability models, it reaches 0.599 and 0.358, respectively, while the pedestrian collision-free rate decreases by at most 0.032.

</details>


### [148] [From Shared Patterns to Individual Evidence: Two-Stage Reasoning for Emerging Entities in Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34708)
*Jinchuan Zhang,Lei Gao,Hongyu Jiang,Chong Mu,Chenang Cai,Ke Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出INSPIRE两阶段时序知识图谱预测框架，通过共享模式预测器与个体证据适配器解决新兴实体冷启动与稀疏爆发交互难题，Emerging MRR提升16.73%-88.83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序知识图谱中新兴实体从零样本首现演化为稀疏、爆发性少样本轨迹。现有范式割裂：模式迁移方法缺乏全目录事件记忆；在线记忆模型存在端点角色不对称，损害未见答案排序；早期稀疏交互在偏态分布下兼具长程离散性与局部冗余。

Method: 两阶段框架（已知目录）。阶段I：可用性感知的共享模式预测器，融合全候选目录跨库事件更新记忆，并施加查询侧与目录侧协同掩码。阶段II：冻结阶段I预测器，优化轻量个体证据适配器（EA），结合文本语义先验与相关性加权的长程事件统计，在支持与角色均衡的共答监督下训练，采用先验几率去偏与软残差对齐。

Result: 在ICEWS与GDELT四基准上，Emerging MRR较最强基线提升16.73%-88.83%；相对配对阶段I预测器，EA使Emerging MRR提升8.28%-32.63%、Answer-new MRR提升7.81%-41.24%、队列宏平均MRR提升9.64%-39.94%。增益源于语义先验与候选级分数竞争，抑制不匹配的既有竞争者且避免零支持先验捷径。

Conclusion: 通过解耦共享模式与个体证据，并以端点角色分解评估与纵向新兴答案队列追踪（查询微平均与答案实体宏平均），可有效提升新兴实体推理性能；代码已开源。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) continually introduce emerging entities whose interaction histories evolve from zero-shot debuts into sparse, bursty few-shot trajectories. However, current paradigms are fragmented: pattern-transfer methods lack catalog-wide event memory, whereas online memory models suffer from endpoint-role asymmetry that degrades unseen-answer ranking. Furthermore, sparse early interactions exhibit both long-range dispersion and local redundancy under skewed distribution. To address these challenges, we propose INSPIRE (Integrating Shared Patterns and Individual Evidence for Reasoning), a two-stage forecasting framework over a known catalog. In Stage I, INSPIRE learns an availability-aware shared-pattern predictor that fuses event-updated memory across the full candidate catalog while applying coordinated query- and catalog-side masking. Subsequently, Stage II freezes this predictor and optimizes a lightweight individual-evidence adapter (EA) combining textual semantic priors with correlation-weighted long-range event statistics under support- and role-balanced co-answer supervision with prior-odds debiasing and soft residual alignment. In addition, we decompose evaluation by endpoint roles and track longitudinal emerging-answer cohorts via query-micro and answer-entity macro metrics. Across four benchmarks based on the ICEWS and GDELT event datasets, INSPIRE improves Emerging MRR by 16.73%-88.83% over the strongest baselines. Relative to paired Stage I predictors, EA further increases Emerging MRR by 8.28%-32.63%, Answer-new MRR by 7.81%-41.24%, and cohort macro MRR by 9.64%-39.94%, gains attributed to semantic priors and candidate-level score competition suppressing mismatched established competitors without zero-support prior shortcuts. The official code is publicly available at https://github.com/KcAcoZhang/INSPIRE.

</details>


### [149] [What Is a Theory Worth to an LLM Solver? Measuring, Inducing, and Merging Theories in Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34751)
*Jun Gao,Shuchao Bi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文量化"给定理论"对LLM求解器的价值，并考察模型能否自行归纳出这些理论。在长度匹配协议下，加入正确理论使Qwen3-14B在等待时间问题上的pass@1从0.37升至0.83，中位正确解token从10.2k降至6.3k；同长度的工作示例无此效果。增益随求解器能力与token预算增大而衰减，且长预算下增益几乎全部来自"能在预算内完成"。通过植入未发表定理区分归纳与记忆：强开放模型恢复oracle理论增益的60%、48%和近乎0%；多份笔记合并可恢复61%，高于最佳单一笔记的47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家在掌握正确理论后解题更快，因此作者想回答两个问题：(1) 显式给出的理论对LLM求解器的增益有多大、是否只是"示例效应"；(2) 模型能否自己写出等价的理论，即这种理论能力是可归纳的还是依赖记忆。

Method: 采用长度匹配的对照协议比较"正确理论"与"同长度工作示例"的效果；在Qwen3-14B上以pass@1和中位token数为指标，并在不同规模求解器与不同token预算下测量增益变化；为区分归纳与记忆，在自建概率族中植入未发表定理，要求强开放模型先以代码探索训练题再撰写理论，并比较诱导理论与oracle理论的增益恢复率；进一步尝试合并多份诱导笔记。

Result: 加入正确理论使pass@1由0.37升至0.83，中位token由10.2k降至6.3k，而同长度示例无效；增益随求解器与预算增大而递减（+0.46、+0.26、+0.07）；长预算下增益几乎全部体现为在预算内完成，凡完成者正确率达98–100%。诱导实验中被植入的三个族恢复率分别为60%、48%与接近0（第三族找到了正确形式但缺少使其可计算的条件）；合并多份笔记在经典族上恢复61%，优于最佳单一笔记的47%。

Conclusion: 理论的价值体现为节省的推理开销，而非单纯提供解题线索；模型具备找到理论"碎片"的能力，但尚不能自主组装成完整可用的理论。作者据此指出，提升理论归纳与整合能力（而不仅是示例模仿）是改进LLM数学推理的关键方向。

Abstract: Mathematicians solve problems faster when they know the right theory. We ask how much a stated theory is worth to a large language model (LLM) solver, and whether a model can write that theory itself. In a length-matched protocol, adding the right theory to the context of Qwen3-14B raises pass@1 on pattern waiting-time problems from 0.37 to 0.83 and cuts the median tokens of correct solutions from 10.2k to 6.3k, while worked examples of the same length do not help. The gain shrinks as the solver and its token budget grow (+0.46, +0.26, +0.07), and at a long budget it comes almost entirely from finishing within budget: solutions that finish are correct 98--100\% of the time in every condition. To separate induction from recall, we plant unpublished theorems in custom probability families and measure how much of the oracle theory's gain an induced theory recovers. A strong open model, required to explore the training problems with code before writing, recovers 60\% of the gain on one planted family, 48\% on a second, and almost none on a third, where it finds the right form but not the condition that makes the form computable. No single induced note is complete, but a strong model that merges several notes recovers 61\% of the gain on the classic family, above the best single note (47\%). Theories are worth the reasoning they save, and models can find their pieces before they can assemble them.

</details>


### [150] [TASK Memory: Beyond Text-Only Memory with Activation Steering and Knowledge Editing against Memory Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34762)
*Jinkun Zhao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"使用期记忆幻觉"概念，揭示LLM智能体源于参数知识与外部记忆冲突的三类记忆幻觉，并提出TASK记忆框架解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益依赖外部记忆，但仍会对"所记内容"产生幻觉：正确记忆已被检索并放入提示，回答却与记忆矛盾或把记忆套用到错误问题上。作者将这类现象定义为"使用期记忆幻觉"（use-time memory hallucinations），并指出其根源是参数知识与外部记忆之间的冲突。

Method: 在五个开源模型上归纳出三类幻觉：记忆忽视（回落至权重中的过时事实，最高46%）、记忆误用（将记忆归因到无关实体，最高24%，检索结合权重编辑时达49%）、版本混淆（53%的回答对修订事实仍断言被取代的旧值）。据此提出TASK memory：由共享同一"模型内地址"的三部分构成——Text文本笔记承载内容；可移除的知识编辑以主语键提供地址并支持多跳组合；一对引导向量逐查询决定采用记忆还是保留模型自身答案（向量不存内容，仅控制文本记忆的使用方式）。在冻结模型上采用预注册、仅测一次协议评估。

Result: TASK将记忆忽视幻觉在全部十个模型-数据集组合中降至≤1.5%；邻居误用从39.2%降至7.5%（CounterFact上综合提升10.8个百分点）；300条记忆库上比BM25 RAG高16.4个百分点，且在3,000条时仍保持优势；无需事实ID即可解决事实修订；MQuAKE多跳准确率提升7.5个百分点。23项跨模型对比中21项显著（8B–27B，四个模型家族），并在10个模型-数据集组合中的9个优于三种近期上下文引导方法。

Conclusion: 使用期记忆幻觉可归因于参数知识—外部记忆冲突并可系统分类度量；将文本内容、知识编辑地址与引导向量结合为统一"模型内地址"的TASK记忆，能显著抑制三类幻觉、优于BM25 RAG与现有上下文引导方法，且具备跨模型规模与家族的稳定性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external memory, yet they still hallucinate about what they remember. Often the correct memory is retrieved and placed in the prompt, but the answer contradicts it or the memory is applied to the wrong question. We call these use-time memory hallucinations.
