Across five open models, we find that they arise from the conflict between parametric knowledge and external memory and take three forms: memory-ignoring, where the model returns to a stale fact in its weights (up to 46% of queries); memory-misapplying, where it attributes the memory to unrelated entities (up to 24%, and 49% when retrieval is combined with weight editing); and version confusion, where 53% of answers about revised facts assert the superseded value when both versions remain stored. We propose TASK memory, which goes beyond text-only memory by combining a Text note with Activation Steering and Knowledge editing that share one in-model address. The text holds the content. A removable knowledge edit supplies the address through its subject key and supports multi-hop composition. A pair of steering vectors decides, query by query, whether the model adopts the memory or keeps its own answer; the vectors store no content and control how the text memory is used. On frozen models under a pre-registered, test-once protocol, TASK reduces memory-ignoring hallucinations to at most 1.5% in all ten model-dataset cells and cuts neighbour misuse from 39.2% to 7.5% (+10.8 pp composite on CounterFact). It improves a 300-memory library over BM25 RAG by 16.4 pp, an advantage that holds at 3,000 memories, resolves fact revisions without fact IDs, and adds 7.5 pp of multi-hop accuracy on MQuAKE. 21 of 23 cross-model comparisons (8B-27B, four families) are significant, and TASK beats three recent context-steering methods in 9 of 10 model-dataset cells.

</details>


### [151] [Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent: Self-Structuring Learning through Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34841)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"自结构化学习"设定与"知识库梯度下降"方法，用问答对指导持久化知识库结构（图模式或链接文档组织）的迭代编辑，并在文档级实验中验证结构改进可提升留出问题上的答案F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的应用依赖知识库结构来决定如何检索与连接回答问题所需信息，但知识库结构本身通常由人工预先设计。本文研究能否在给定问题与期望答案的条件下，自动构建并持续改进这一可编辑结构。

Method: 形式化"自结构化学习"（Self-Structuring Learning）设定：问题与期望答案指导持久知识库的构建，其结构可为图模式或链接知识文档的组织。提出知识库梯度下降（KBGD）：诊断期望答案与当前知识库所得回答之间的差异；每轮提出一个结构编辑、重建知识库，若学习损失下降则采纳该编辑。通过对候选编辑随机采样，将编辑带来的损失变化表达为关于"选中该编辑概率"的导数；由于组合编辑的作用相互影响，将联合编辑作为单一候选进行评估。Schema Descent 将该方法实例化为图模式编辑。

Result: 在由研究论文组成的大文档实验中，更新后的知识卡片与图模式在留出的生成问题上取得比初始结构更高的平均 Answer F1，且每个文档上的图模式均有提升。在人类问题上，图模式的平均 F1 上升而知识卡片下降，说明更新后的结构更适配其学习来源的那类问题。

Conclusion: 知识库结构可通过问题驱动的迭代编辑被有效学习与改进，但所学结构带有问题分布偏置，其收益与所训练问题类型绑定；这提示知识库结构化需明确面向目标查询分布设计。

Abstract: We consider the problem of constructing knowledge bases for applications based on large language models, in which the structure of a knowledge base determines how an application can retrieve and connect the information needed to answer a question. We study Self-Structuring Learning, a setting in which questions and expected answers guide the construction of a persistent knowledge base whose editable structure can be a graph schema or the organization of linked knowledge documents. We propose Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent, which diagnoses discrepancies between expected answers and responses obtained through the current knowledge base. At each round, the method proposes a structural edit, rebuilds the knowledge base, and adopts the edit when the learning loss decreases. The retained structure is evaluated on other questions about the same sources. By randomizing over candidate edits, we express the loss change of an edit as a derivative with respect to the probability of selecting it, and we evaluate joint edits as single candidates because their changes interact. Schema Descent instantiates the method for graph schemas. In experiments on large documents, each a bundle of research papers, the updated knowledge cards and graph schemas obtain higher mean Answer F1 on held-out generated questions than their initial structures, with schema gains in every document. On human questions, mean F1 rises for schemas and falls for cards: the updated structures serve the kind of question they were learned from.

</details>


### [152] [How Much Dependency Can a Marginal Rule See? Total Correlation in Diffusion LM Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31990)
*Dovud Asadov,Rustambek Urokov,Qobiljon Toshnazarov,Abduaziz Kayumov,Mardon Hazratov,Husanboy Mansuraliyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文估计了掩码扩散语言模型并行提交一组token时损失的条件总相关C，并检验仅用逐位置边际概率的预测器能否准确预测该量。结果表明，在四个模型中的三个上，最优边际预测器误差约1比特，且精度主要来自理论界本身；Dream-instruct是例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型每步并行提交多个token，速度很快，但每个token从各自的边际分布采样，token之间的依赖关系被丢弃。这一损失的大小可量化为提交集合的条件总相关C。多数解码器基于逐位置边际来选择提交集合，这类规则可以给出C的界，却无法确定C本身。作者因此研究：基于边际的解码器究竟能在多大程度上预测C。

Method: 作者在两个系列的四个掩码扩散语言模型上，对固定候选集合估计条件总相关C，并比较仅依赖边际概率的预测器与只知道理论上界的基线的预测精度。进一步分析估计值与并行提交的精确KL代价之间的排序一致性和偏差幅度，并比较六种常见边际选择规则（如集合池化、最弱位置门控等，共24组配对比较）。此外，作者指出不同模型将同一位置的预测放在不同的logit索引上，错误的索引会使不一致性诊断量被放大14至41倍，因此给出三项检查来测量并校准这一索引约定。

Result: 在四个模型中的三个上，最优的仅边际预测器误差约1比特（0.74至1.17比特），比仅知道界的基线低1.8至3.7倍，且大部分精度来自该界本身。在子集上，作者的估计对集合的排序与并行提交的精确KL代价一致，但在散文文本以及其余文本仍被掩码的情形下，中位数低0.4至1.1比特。Dream-instruct是例外，主要在散文上，其条件分布的自一致性最差。在六种边际规则的全部24组配对比较中，对集合做池化均优于以其最弱位置做门控。此外，Dream与LLaDA将同一位置的预测置于不同logit索引，错误索引会使不一致性诊断量膨胀14至41倍。

Conclusion: 仅基于边际概率的预测器对固定集合的条件总相关C具有中等但有限的预测能力，其精度很大程度依赖于理论上界；精确KL代价与边际估计之间存在系统性偏差，且模型间差异显著（如Dream-instruct的自一致性较差）。结果仅适用于对固定集合预测C，并不意味着依赖感知解码器能带来多少实际收益。同时，跨模型比较需要校准logit索引约定，否则不一致性诊断会被严重放大。

Abstract: A masked diffusion language model is fast because it commits several tokens per step. Each token is sampled from its own marginal, so the dependency between the tokens is lost, and the size of that loss is the conditional total correlation C of the committed set. Most decoders choose the set from per-position marginals, which can bound C but cannot determine it. We ask how well they predict it. We estimate C for fixed candidate sets on four masked diffusion LMs from two families. On three of the four, the best marginal-only predictor is off by about one bit (0.74 to 1.17), 1.8 to 3.7 times less than a baseline that knows only the bound, and most of this accuracy comes from the bound itself. On a subset, our estimate ranks sets like the exact KL cost of a parallel commit, but sits a median 0.4 to 1.1 bits below it on prose and when the rest of the text is still masked. Dream-instruct is the exception, mainly on prose, where its conditionals are least self-consistent. Among six common marginal rules, pooling over the set beats gating on its weakest position in all 24 paired comparisons. The measurement needs a validated readout: Dream and LLaDA place a position's prediction at different logit indices, and the wrong index inflates an inconsistency diagnostic 14 to 41 times. We give three checks that measure the convention. Our results concern predicting C for a fixed set, not what a dependency-aware decoder gains.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Demystifying Real-world LLM Service Freeloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33218)
*Xinyi Hou,Shenao Wang,Wenhui Pan,Yanjie Zhao,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对513个开源"LLM服务白嫖"(LSF)项目开展系统测量，追踪其1,588条推理路径与64个上游LLM服务，揭示协议逆向与账号轮换等运作机制，并发现62个服务中58个的条款明令禁止相关做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方项目将LLM网页应用/专用客户端的免费推理能力重新封装为独立API，超出提供方许可的访问、分配与再分发条件（即LSF）。此前对其规模与内在机制缺乏认识，需系统测量以支撑防御未授权消耗与再分发。

Method: 测量513个开源项目，追踪1,588条推理路径、覆盖64个上游服务；刻画项目开发、依赖关系与运作机制（调用方式、消息适配、对话状态重建、响应格式转换、账号选择/轮换），并对62个可用服务条款进行政策性分析。

Result: 93.0%仓库创建于2026年；直接HTTP/WebSocket请求占83.2%路径，复现产品协议为主流调用方式；账号选择/轮换出现于304条路径(19.1%)，多账号项目据此分摊额度；62个服务中58个条款明确禁止至少一种相关实践（自动化访问、账号共享、服务再分发），且限制同时覆盖下游API供给与上游推理获取手段。

Conclusion: LSF生态规模化、机制成熟且与主流服务条款系统性冲突，研究结果为防范免费LLM资源的未授权消耗与再分发提供依据；作者已向相关提供方负责任披露。

Abstract: Third-party projects repackage free inference from LLM web applications and dedicated clients into independent APIs. We refer to such use beyond providers' permitted access, allocation, or redistribution conditions as LLM service freeloading (LSF). However, its scale and underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. We present a systematic measurement study of 513 open-source projects, tracing 1,588 inference paths across 64 upstream LLM services and characterizing their development, dependencies, and operating mechanisms. We find that 93.0% of repositories were created in 2026. Direct HTTP or WebSocket requests account for 83.2% of paths, making reproduction of product protocols the dominant invocation method. Projects adapt messages, reconstruct conversation state, and convert responses to support compatible APIs. Account selection or rotation appears on 304 paths (19.1%), allowing projects with multiple eligible accounts to distribute requests across their separate allowances. Our policy analysis finds that 58 of the 62 services with available terms explicitly prohibit at least one examined practice, including automated access, account sharing, or service redistribution. These restrictions cover both downstream API provision and the means of obtaining upstream inference. Our findings inform defenses against unauthorized consumption and redistribution of free LLM resources. We have responsibly disclosed our findings to the relevant providers.

</details>


### [154] [Real Package, Wrong Controller: Static Auditing of Vendor--Protocol Mismatches in LLM-Generated PLC Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33227)
*S M Zia Ur Rashid,Sazidul Islam Hira,Suman Rath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文定义了"元数据条件下、导入级的厂商—协议不兼容"问题，构建无标注静态验证器 Proteus-OT，发现883份生成脚本中20.4%存在导入级不匹配，而现有"存在性"检查无法识别；同时指出机器/人类分类实验（字符n-gram达macro-F1 1.000）实际由尾随换行等表层特征驱动，不构成作者身份检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型已能生成驱动PLC工业协议的Python代码，但既有代码—环境接地检查只判断导入的包或符号"是否存在"，不判断其是否与声明的工业目标（厂商/协议）相适配。因此需要一个以元数据（声明目标）为条件、针对导入级厂商—协议不兼容的审计手段，并澄清机器生成文本分类在仓库标注语料上的真实含义。

Method: 形式化定义"元数据条件下、导入级厂商—协议不兼容"；实现 Proteus-OT——基于版本固定、有文档记载绑定的依赖图的无标签静态验证器，对每个正向样本给出可检查的见证（witness）。在883份生成脚本上与28份人类控制文件对比，报告多重收紧口径下的比例（全部、单一厂商目标、附加"触达协议通信调用"），并与"存在性基线"对比。另在同一语料上做机器—人类分类审计：字符n-gram在任务、模型、厂商不相交划分下评估，并做注释移除、AST往返、标识符匿名化等消融/扰动测试。

Result: 883份脚本中180份（20.4%）存在导入级不匹配；限定单厂商目标为16.3%，再要求冲突库触达协议通信调用为14.2%（作者将其解读为条件于语料元数据而非经核实意图）。28份可裁决的人类控制文件均无此类不匹配，但样本过小不足以估计流行率。在平衡子集中，96.6%的不匹配脚本所导入的包与方法确实存在，说明"存在性基线"无法刻画该类问题。分类审计中字符n-gram在任务、模型、厂商不相交划分下macro-F1达1.000，且在注释移除、AST往返与标识符匿名化后仍成立；95.2%的人类文件带尾随换行而生成文件均无，该单一特征即可区分两类。

Conclusion: 厂商—协议一致性是有价值的静态审计目标，Proteus-OT提供了可解释、可见证的检测途径；但在仓库标注语料上做"来源/生成"分类容易退化为表层伪迹（如尾随换行），因此不能等同于作者身份检测。

Abstract: Language models can generate Python that drives industrial protocols on programmable logic controllers, yet existing code-grounding checks ask whether an imported package or symbol exists rather than whether it suits the declared industrial target. We define \emph{metadata-conditioned, import-level vendor--protocol incompatibility} and build \textsc{Proteus-OT}, a label-free static verifier over a version-pinned graph of documented bindings. Across 883 generated scripts, 180 carry an import-level mismatch against the declared target (20.4\%); restricting to single-vendor targets gives 16.3\%, and additionally requiring the offending library to reach a protocol communication call gives 14.2\%, which we read as conditional on the corpus metadata rather than on verified intent. None of the 28 adjudicable human control files carries one, a sample too small to estimate a prevalence. Within the balanced subset, 96.6\% of mismatching scripts import packages and call methods that exist, so an existence baseline does not represent them, and every positive ships with an inspectable witness. We also audit machine-versus-human classification on the same corpus, where character $n$-grams reach macro-F1 1.000 under task, model and vendor-disjoint partitions and survive comment removal, an AST round trip and identifier anonymisation. The trailing newline present in 95.2\% of human files and in none of the generated ones separates the classes on its own. Vendor--protocol consistency is a useful static audit target, whereas provenance classification on repository-labelled corpora is not authorship detection.

</details>


### [155] [Beyond the Efficiency-Efficacy Dilemma: A Critical Analysis of Transferability in Adversarial Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33243)
*Veysel Alevcan,Mohammad Furqan Ali,Teresa Sousa,Luís Miguel Campos,Christoforos Ntantogian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出自动化对抗安全压力测试平台AAIE，在EMBER2018上对随机森林做迁移攻击，发现单步FGSM攻击成功率(52.9%)高于迭代PGD(45.2%)，因其扰动更具迁移性，并用多项指标量化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML模型已大规模用于恶意软件检测，亟需严谨研究其对抗鲁棒性，并提供自动化、可对标AI治理标准的对抗审计手段。

Method: 构建Adversarial AI Engine (AAIE)平台进行自动化安全压力测试；基于EMBER2018数据集对随机森林目标模型开展迁移攻击（FGSM与PGD），以ASR、迁移性得分、逃逸率、韧性指数四项指标进行基准评测（与AI治理新兴标准相呼应）。

Result: 单步FGSM的ASR为52.9%，显著高于迭代PGD的45.2%(p<0.002)；迭代攻击易过拟合替代网络产生脆弱扰动，而FGSM生成的样本迁移性更好；受限扰动下迁移性得分最高约0.8，暴露模型不足。

Conclusion: 简单单步攻击可优于迭代攻击的迁移效果，凸显实际安全风险；AAIE可作为自动化对抗审计工具，并建议采用与治理标准一致的多指标体系评估鲁棒性。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) models are already being deployed at scale for malware detection. Therefore there is a pressing need to rigorously study adversarial robustness. To that end, we introduce the Adversarial AI Engine (AAIE): a platform for automated security stress-testing. We performed transfer attacks, via EMBER2018, against a Random Forest target. Counterintuitively, we found single-step FGSM to have the highest attack success rate (ASR) of 52.9% compared to iterative PGD with 45.2% (p < 0.002). Compared to iterative attacks which tend to overfit surrogate networks creating brittle perturbations, FGSM created more transferable examples. We benchmarked our attacks using ASR, transferability score, evasion rate, and resilience index. Through these metrics, proposed in concordance with emerging standards in AI governance, we expose shortcomings as our transferability scores peak at ~0.8 with constrained perturbations. We highlight the practical security implications of these findings and demonstrate AAIE for automated adversarial auditing.

</details>


### [156] [Onnx2TEE: Automated Graph-Aware Model Partitioning for TEE-based Edge AI Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33262)
*Daniela M. Vela Hernandez,Samuel Hildebrand,Sean Oesch,Brian Weber,Shayan Monadjemi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Onnx2TEE：面向非序列前馈网络的自动化模型无关分区流水线，用图感知分割将ONNX模型切分到富TEE中，最小化边界通信并降低TEE引起的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型研发成本高、常部署于潜在敌对环境且下游依赖其准确输出，需防窃取与篡改；TEE可硬件隔离保护权重，但资源受限，难支撑大模型全量执行。

Method: 提出Onnx2TEE自动化、模型无关的ONNX分区流水线，针对非序列前馈网络采用图感知分割，在保持计算依赖的同时最小化跨边界通信，生成TEE内可信应用。

Result: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上基于Arm TrustZone TEE评估：模型参数可高度集中于个别分段，支持对参数密集组件的选择性保护；可信应用内存需求同时取决于计算量与中间张量规模。

Conclusion: 部分执行式分区是资源受限TEE上保护大模型权重的可行路线；参数集中度揭示了选择性保护的可能，但TEE内存预算需同时权衡计算与中间张量。

Abstract: AI models are increasingly being deployed at the edge. Since these models often require significant research and development to produce, are often deployed in potentially hostile environments, and downstream systems often rely on accurate data; protecting them from theft or manipulation is worthy of serious consideration. Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), which provide hardware-enforced separation of software execution, can protect model weights but impose strict resource constraints, making full-model execution impractical for large neural networks. We present Onnx2TEE, an automated, model-independent pipeline that partitions ONNX models across rich, trusted execution environments for non-sequential feed-forward networks using graph-aware segmentation to preserve computational dependencies while minimizing boundary communication and reducing TEE-induced inference latency. Evaluation on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin using an Arm TrustZone TEE demonstrates that model parameters can be highly concentrated in individual segments, supporting selective protection of parameter-dense model components. The evaluation further shows that the generated trusted app's memory requirements depend on both computation and intermediate tensor sizes.

</details>


### [157] [RegionAlign: Manipulating Parameter Layouts for Localized Fault Injection on LLMs and VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33370)
*Xiaomeng Wang,Peter Horvath,Zhengyu Zhao,Martha Larson,Zhuoran Liu,Lejla Batina*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RegionAlign：恶意供应商仅通过部署前的参数布局操纵（位敏感度搜索＋通道置换），将脆弱权重位集中到同一存储区域，在不改变权重数值与干净性能的前提下，放大物理故障注入对LLM/VLM的破坏效果，揭示了一种隐蔽的供应链威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理故障注入（如EMFI）可翻转存储的权重位并导致LLM/VLM性能退化，但现有工作大多忽略供应链操纵会如何影响模型的故障脆弱性——即攻击者能否在部署前通过布局设计主动放大物理故障危害。

Method: 提出RegionAlign：恶意供应商在部署前仅操纵参数布局。方法包含（1）位敏感度搜索，识别对性能影响最大的脆弱位；（2）通道置换（channel permutations），在不改变权重取值的前提下重排参数位置，使脆弱位集中落入同一存储区域。

Result: 在同等翻转预算下，软件实验表明经RegionAlign处理后的布局中，由搜索选定的区域内位翻转所导致的性能退化，显著大于原始布局或随机布局；EMFI可行性研究验证了权重损坏确实可被诱发。

Conclusion: RegionAlign揭示了一种隐蔽的供应链威胁：仅靠布局操纵即可削弱模型的物理故障韧性，因此模型部署需将参数布局纳入安全性评估与防护范围。

Abstract: Physical fault injection can flip stored weight bits, degrading LLMs and VLMs. Existing work largely overlooks how supply-chain manipulation affects fault vulnerability. We introduce RegionAlign, which allows a malicious provider to manipulate only the parameter layout before deployment, using bit-sensitivity search and channel permutations to concentrate vulnerable bits in one memory region while preserving weight values and clean performance. Under matched budgets, software experiments show greater degradation from search-selected in-region flips with RegionAlign than with original or random layouts. EMFI feasibility study confirms weight corruption. RegionAlign reveals a stealthy supply-chain threat that layout manipulation weakens physical fault resilience.

</details>


### [158] [From Conversations to Commitments: A Design Study of Shared Records for Agent-to-Business Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33373)
*Carlos Eberhardt,Mansura Habiba*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文通过设计研究考察共享、可归属的交互记录如何跨独立运营组织关联"已接受条款—授权—预留资源—持久证据"，基于 Talea（签名 AT Protocol 记录 + A2A 任务执行）在航空预订/延误旅客改订三家竞争酒店的沙盒案例中得出四点观察：AT Protocol 支持六项协调需求中的五项；保密约束使每家酒店仅见双边交换、仅航空公司可关联三方交易；信任边界内后加入者可能获益但未证明优于等强度点对点方案；确定性发布接口无法约束依赖私有状态协商的披露。实验仅复现规格行为与反例，未测量比较收益，最终以可检验假设与实验设计收束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业承诺需要把已接受条款与授权、预留资源、持久证据绑定，而这类绑定往往要跨独立运营的组织完成；现有交互记录缺乏跨组织的可归属共享基础，因此需要考察共享记录能否支撑该链接并明确其能力边界（而非直接宣称优越性）。

Method: 设计研究：将签名 AT Protocol 记录与 A2A 任务执行组合为 Talea，使用其协调沙盒及一个拟议的商业扩展，构建"航空公司将延误旅客改订至三家竞争酒店"的三方案例；以六项协调需求为对照做能力分析，并结合有限模型探索（finite-model exploration）与确定性接口检查（移除选定控制以产生反例）。

Result: 四项发现：(1) AT Protocol 机制覆盖六项协调需求中的五项，记录级访问与释放控制仍需额外强制执行；(2) 案例保密约束使各酒店授权视图局限于其双边交换，仅航空公司能关联全部三项交易；(3) 仅在航空公司信任边界内对后加入参与者存在潜在收益，但不构成相对"同等仪表化"点对点设计的优势；(4) 确定性释放接口可约束固定、已批准的载荷，但当协商值依赖私有状态时无法界定披露范围。有限模型与接口检查在移除选定控制时可复现规定行为与反例，但不测量比较收益，也不验证已部署商业系统。

Conclusion: 论文不主张共享记录已被证明更优，而将剩余命题表述为假设：在同一信任边界内，相对于具备等价授权、预留、留存与关联证据的点对点交换，共享记录能在参与方变动时改善决策重构与恢复；并给出用于检验该假设的实验设计。整体贡献是具有明确边界的机制设计与可反驳的评估议程。

Abstract: Business commitments require accepted terms to be linked to delegated authority, reserved resources and durable evidence. We examine how a shared, attributable interaction record can support these links across independently operated organisations. Talea combines signed AT Protocol records with A2A task execution. We use its coordination sandbox and a proposed commercial extension in a design study of an airline booking disrupted passengers into three competing hotels. Four observations follow. First, AT Protocol provides mechanisms supporting five of six coordination requirements, while record-level access and release controls require additional enforcement. Second, the case's confidentiality constraints restrict each hotel's authorised view to its bilateral exchange; only the airline can correlate all three deals. Third, this leaves a possible benefit for late-joining participants inside the airline's trust boundary, but does not establish an advantage over an equally instrumented point-to-point design. Fourth, a deterministic release interface constrains fixed, approved payloads but cannot bound disclosure when negotiated values depend on private state. Finite-model explorations and deterministic interface checks reproduce the specified behaviours and counterexamples when selected controls are removed. They do not measure comparative benefit or validate a deployed commercial system. The remaining hypothesis is that, within one trust boundary, shared records improve decision reconstruction and recovery under changing participation relative to point-to-point exchange with equivalent authority, reservation, retention and correlation evidence. We specify an experiment to test it.

</details>


### [159] [Poisoning Self-Evolving Security Policies in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33385)
*Aymen Noor,Honglu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了自演化防御（SED）自身的学习过程可被攻击：仅需一次精心构造的提示注入即可诱导其学出过宽策略，进而过度拒答合法请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化防御通过从观测到的攻击中学习可复用策略来提升LLM-agent安全性，但作者想检验这一"学习"环节本身是否构成新的攻击面（即数据投毒/策略中毒）。

Method: 在AgentDojo基准上使用SED，构造单条提示注入样本进行投毒；在10次Nemotron运行中统计过度拒答率，并将攻击迁移测试到gpt-oss-120b；同时尝试针对SED的judge、策略合成、检索与组织机制发起完整性导向攻击作为对照，并考察无误报情形下的自我投毒，最后评估对比式策略合成与自动宽度检查门控两类缓解措施。

Result: 10次Nemotron运行中所有被投毒的运行均至少过度拒答一个合法任务，同时无关任务性能不变、原始提示注入防御仍有效；攻击可迁移至gpt-oss-120b，但依赖初始注入成功，当目标agent抵御底层注入时失效；针对judge、策略合成、检索、组织的完整性攻击在实验设定下均失败，表明过度拒答是主要可利用失效模式；另发现无需攻击者的假阳性危害判断引发的自我投毒；对比式策略合成与宽度检查门控可显著但无法完全消除过度拒答，且不损害安全性。

Conclusion: 自演化防御的学习过程是现实攻击面，过度拒答（可用性/过度泛化）是主要可利用失效模式而非完整性失守；现有缓解手段有效但非完备，需在自演化防御设计中引入策略宽度约束与投毒鲁棒性考量。

Abstract: Self-evolving defenses improve LLM-agent security by learning reusable policies from observed attacks. However, we demonstrate that this learning process itself can become an attack surface. Using SED (Self-Evolving Defense) in the AgentDojo benchmark, we construct a single prompt-injection episode that causes the defense to learn an over-broad policy and subsequently refuse legitimate requests. Across 10 Nemotron runs, every poisoned run over-refuses at least one legitimate task, while unrelated-task performance remains unchanged and the original prompt-injection defense remains effective. The attack also transfers to gpt-oss-120b; however, its poisoning mechanism depends on a successful initial prompt injection and therefore fails to activate when the target agent resists the underlying injection. In contrast, integrity-oriented attacks targeting SED's judge, policy synthesis, retrieval, and organization mechanisms fail in our evaluated settings, identifying over-refusal as the primary exploitable failure mode observed in our experiments. We further identify self-poisoning from false-positive harm judgments without an attacker. Finally, contrastive policy synthesis and an automatic broadness-check gate substantially reduce, but do not eliminate over-refusal, without compromising security in our evaluated settings.

</details>


### [160] [SecID: An LLM-Guided Framework for Threat-Aware Secondary Asset Identification with Quantitative RTL Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33453)
*Md Ajoad Hasan,Dipayan Saha,Upoma Das,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecID：多智能体框架，结合确定性RTL分析、有界动态评估与LLM推理，针对特定威胁识别二级安全资产，并在7个开源SoC/IP设计上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代硬件设计复杂度上升使安全保证日益困难。前端安全验证的基础步骤之一是识别安全资产：一级资产（primary assets）通常可从设计功能中直接看出，但可能导致其被攻破的内部信号（二级资产）难以辨识。复杂的数据流与控制流依赖产生大量内部安全关键位置，其可利用性取决于具体威胁的攻击机制，现有方法难以精确识别这类威胁相关的二级资产。

Method: 提出SecID多智能体框架，融合三类手段：(1) 确定性RTL分析；(2) 有界动态评估；(3) LLM推理。每个资产判定均由功能影响（functional influence）、威胁特征（threat features）与显式攻击机制（explicit attack mechanisms）共同支撑，从而输出威胁特定的二级资产集合。

Result: 在七个开源SoC与IP设计（含openMSP430、NEORV32、CV32E40P）上评估。结果通过与先前工作对比、故障仿真和形式化分析相互印证；并进一步通过流水线级与末级消融、阈值敏感性研究以及末级可重复性分析进行评估，表明SecID能针对给定一级资产识别出高度威胁相关的二级资产。

Conclusion: SecID可精确识别威胁相关的二级安全资产，为硬件安全验证提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: The growing complexity of modern hardware designs is making security assurance increasingly difficult. One of the foundational steps in pre-silicon security verification is identifying security assets. While primary assets are often evident from design functionality, the internal signals that can facilitate their compromise are much less apparent. Complex data- and control-flow dependencies create many such internal security-critical locations, whose exploitation depends on the threat-specific attack mechanism---these are the secondary assets. To precisely identify these, we propose SecID, a multi-agent framework that combines deterministic RTL analysis with bounded dynamic evaluation and LLM reasoning to identify threat-specific secondary assets. Each asset decision is supported by functional influence, threat features, and explicit attack mechanisms. We demonstrate SecID on seven open-source SoC and IP designs, including openMSP430, NEORV32, and CV32E40P. The experimental results are corroborated through comparison with prior work, fault simulation, and formal analysis, and further evaluated through pipeline- and final-stage ablation, threshold-sensitivity studies, and final-stage repeatability analysis. Together, these evaluations show that SecID can identify highly threat-relevant secondary assets with respect to a given primary asset, providing a scalable path toward hardware-security verification.

</details>


### [161] [Benchmarking Open-Ended Vulnerability Discovery in Windows Kernel Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33486)
*Jongseong Kim,Hwiwon Lee,Dongjun Kim,Lingming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 DeepBinary，首个面向 Windows 内核二进制的开放式漏洞发现基准；对 12 个目标二进制评测显示，前沿模型累计发现 200+ 漏洞、估算赏金总额约 66.1 万美元，其中 146 个为此前未知漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准主要针对开源项目中的已知漏洞，基本忽略了支撑大量软件产业的闭源二进制；因此需要系统评估前沿模型在真实生产二进制中挖掘深层漏洞的能力。

Method: 构建 DeepBinary 基准：给定目标二进制，智能体在断网隔离虚拟机中使用反汇编器与内核调试器进行分析，每个漏洞需提交可触发崩溃的 PoC；框架在全新虚拟机上复现每份报告，并用 LLM 评审判定其有效性、唯一性与影响（以漏洞赏金估值衡量），同时支持未知（0-day）漏洞的发现与验证。在 12 个目标二进制上，对代表性开源/闭源权重模型（GPT-6 Sol、Claude Opus 5、DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 等）与多种编码智能体（Codex、Claude Code 等）进行综合评测。

Result: 共发现 200 余个漏洞，估算合计赏金 661,000 美元，其中 146 个为此前未知的独立漏洞；结果还揭示模型在二进制分析、实验执行以及长时程漏洞发现持续性方面存在显著差异。

Conclusion: DeepBinary 使复杂二进制上的长时程、开放式漏洞发现变得可度量，并揭示了前沿模型在分析与探索此类系统时的根本性差异。

Abstract: Frontier models are increasingly used to discover real-world security vulnerabilities, making it critical to systematically evaluate their cybersecurity capabilities. However, existing security benchmarks primarily focus on known vulnerabilities in open-source projects, largely overlooking closed-source binaries, which underpin much of the software industry. In this paper, we ask: how effective are frontier models at uncovering deep vulnerabilities in production binaries? To answer this question, we present DeepBinary, the first comprehensive benchmark for open-ended vulnerability discovery in Windows kernel binaries. Given a target binary, an agent analyzes it using a disassembler and kernel debugger in network-isolated VMs and reports each vulnerability with a crashing proof of concept (PoC). Unlike existing benchmarks, DeepBinary replays every report on fresh VMs and employs an LLM-based judge to assess its validity, uniqueness, and impact (estimated by bug bounty values). Beyond known vulnerabilities, DeepBinary also enables the discovery and validation of previously unknown (0-day) vulnerabilities. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of representative open- and closed-weight models (including GPT-6 Sol, Claude Opus 5, and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash) across diverse coding agents (including Codex and Claude Code) on 12 target binaries. In total, they discover over 200 vulnerabilities with an estimated aggregate bounty of $661,000, including 146 distinct previously unknown vulnerabilities across all experiments. Our results further reveal substantial differences in models' ability to analyze binaries, conduct experiments, and sustain vulnerability discovery over long horizons. Overall, DeepBinary makes long-horizon, open-ended vulnerability discovery in complex binaries measurable, revealing fundamental differences in how frontier models analyze and explore such systems.

</details>


### [162] [Merkle Trees Minimize Verifier Complexity in the ROM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33508)
*Miranda Christ,Oliver Korten,Tal Malkin,Noam Mazor,Shyamal Patel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文证明：基于哈希的向量承诺若仅黑盒使用哈希函数，则验证者的亚对数级哈希评估复杂度不可实现；即使在随机谕示模型中也成立——任何确定性验证者的查询复杂度为 Ω_θ(log n)（θ 为随机谕示的输入长度），而高元 Merkle 树可常数因子内达到该下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Merkle 树作为最有效的基于哈希的向量承诺，验证者需 O(log n) 次哈希评估，其在 SNARK、凭证、认证字典等应用中构成性能瓶颈。能否构造验证者亚对数哈希评估的哈希型向量承诺，是长期未决问题。

Method: 在随机谕示模型下进行下界分析：证明亚对数验证复杂度的承诺方案不可实现，从而更强地排除了基于抗碰撞哈希的构造。核心技术引理是“不相交乘积引理”（disjoint product lemma），可视为 van den Berg–Kesten–Reimer（BKR）不等式的计算类比。

Result: 任何确定性验证者的向量承诺其验证查询复杂度为 Ω_θ(log n)；该下界被高元（high-arity）Merkle 树在常数因子内匹配。黑盒使用哈希函数无法获得亚对数验证复杂度。

Conclusion: 亚对数验证者复杂度的哈希型向量承诺在黑盒意义下不可能实现，Merkle 树类方案在渐近意义下已近最优。所提出的不相交乘积引理具有独立价值，或可用于随机谕示模型中的其他下界与分离结果。

Abstract: Nearly five decades after their introduction, Merkle trees remain the most efficient hash-based vector commitments. They compress a vector $x \in \{0,1\}^n$ into a commitment whose size is independent of $n$, such that a verifier can open any bit $x_i$ using $\log n$ hash evaluations. The security of the scheme (intractability of finding two distinct openings for a given bit using the same commitment) follows from the collision resistance of the hash function. The verifier's runtime complexity is a bottleneck in many key applications of vector commitments, including SNARKs, credentials, and authenticated dictionaries. As such, a longstanding open question has been whether it is possible to construct hash-based vector commitments where the verifier makes a sublogarithmic number of hash evaluations.
  We prove that sublogarithmic verifier complexity is impossible to achieve through black-box use of a hash function; we not only rule out constructions from collision resistant hash functions, but in fact show that commitment schemes with sublogarithmic verifier complexity are unachievable in the random oracle model. In particular, any vector commitment with deterministic verifiers has verifier query complexity $Ω_θ(\log n)$, where $θ$ is the input length of the random oracle. This bound is matched up to constant factors by high-arity Merkle trees. The key technical lemma in our proof is a disjoint product lemma, which can be viewed as a computational analog of the van den Berg-Kesten-Reimer (BKR) inequality. We believe this lemma is of independent interest, and in particular may be useful for showing other lower bounds and separations in the random oracle model.

</details>


### [163] [PRECEPT: Security Self-Improvement of Coding Agents via Evolving Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33519)
*Keyan Guo,Rupam Patir,Haipeng Cai,Hongxin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AI编程智能体在人类审查有限的情况下提交代码，大量代码通过功能测试却不安全。作者将"从自身安全失误中学习并跨会话持久化、迁移到新代码且不破坏功能"的能力定义为安全自我改进（security self-improvement），并提出PRECEPT，通过从权威安全编码原则出发、依据智能体自身失败持续修订的演化原则库赋予智能体该能力，其最弱智能体在未见过任务族上达到97.1%安全可用代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编程智能体即使被展示失败的安全测试也能修复漏洞（85.7%），但换到新会话后以26.7%的比例重现同类漏洞，与从未见过该漏洞的智能体（27.5%）几乎无差别——即能力存在但无法保持。记录修复到持久上下文或提供面向人类的安全编码原则均未能弥补该差距：前者绑定于原任务族，后者过于笼统难以落地。因此缺失的是一种让经验跨会话保持、迁移到新代码且不损害功能性的"安全自我改进"能力。

Method: 提出PRECEPT：为编程智能体构建一个演化中的安全编码原则库。该库以权威安全编码原则为起点，针对智能体自身实际出现的失败进行修订，使每条原则最终"触发"于该智能体真正出错的代码位置。修订采用严格筛选机制——仅当新的执行结果显示代码更安全、且功能成功率相对无原则库的智能体保持在小的容差范围内时才予以保留，从而在安全性与功能性间维持平衡，并支持多步持续演化而非首步即饱和。

Result: 在14个任务序列上，Codex、Claude Code与Qwen Code展示失败测试后修复漏洞率达85.7%，跨会话重现同类漏洞率26.7%（对照未见过组27.5%）。PRECEPT在演化阶段从未见过的任务族上，最弱智能体达到97.1%的安全可用代码，高于最强无辅助智能体的81.9%与最强基线的87.7%。

Conclusion: 安全的编程智能体缺的不是知识而是知识的"激活"与"保持"。通过将权威原则与智能体自身失败对齐并迭代验证，PRECEPT显著提升了跨会话、跨任务族的安全泛化能力，且安全性提升不牺牲功能性。该工作将安全自我改进确立为编程智能体的一项独立且可工程化的能力。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly write and submit code with limited human review, and much of it passes functional tests while remaining insecure. We show that these failures persist. Across 14 task sequences, Codex, Claude Code, and Qwen Code fixed a vulnerability 85.7% of the time when shown the failing security test, then reintroduced the same vulnerability class in a new session 26.7% of the time, about as often as agents that had never seen it (27.5%). The capability is present; the retention is not. Neither recording the repair in the agent's persistent context nor supplying published secure coding principles closed the gap: the recorded fix stayed tied to the family of the task it came from, and the principles, written for human developers, remained too general to apply to the code at hand. The knowledge exists; it is not activated. We call the missing ability security self-improvement: an agent learns from its own security failures so that each lesson persists across sessions, applies to new code, and does not break what works. We present PRECEPT, which equips coding agents with this ability through an evolving library that starts from authoritative secure coding principles and revises them against the agent's own failures, so that each principle comes to fire on the code where that agent actually goes wrong. A revision is kept only if fresh executions show it makes code more secure while keeping functional success within a small tolerance of the agent without the library. Improvement continues across evolution steps rather than saturating after the first. On task families never seen during evolution, PRECEPT's weakest agent reaches 97.1% secure working code, above the strongest unaided agent at 81.9% and the strongest baseline at 87.7%.

</details>


### [164] [Small Leaks Sink Great Ships: Automated Analysis of Protocols That Tolerate Low-Entropy Keys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33595)
*Yongkang Xiao,Jing Chen,Min Shi,Kun He,Mei Wang,Ruiying Du*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出首个协议无关的低熵密钥自动化符号分析方法，以MDerive事实、三层破解预言机和分级比特级攻击者模型扩展Tamarin（EntropyVerif，无需修改内核），自动重现PMKID、KNOB与Method Confusion攻击，并发现蓝牙经典新攻击UMD，将128比特会话密钥恢复复杂度从2^128降至最低2^12。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WPA2-PSK、BLE、蓝牙经典等协议为易用性、向后兼容或资源受限部署而容忍低熵密钥，但现有符号验证器基于完美加密假设，无法刻画暴力破解攻击者；既有工作仅靠协议特定、手工编码的reveal规则，难以跨协议推广，也未系统探索复合密钥分解。

Method: 构建三项抽象：(1) 消息推导事实MDerive，独立于具体函数符号刻画暴力破解前提；(2) 三层破解预言机（值破解、关系破解、消息推导），与标准Dolev-Yao攻击者组合；(3) 分级比特级攻击者模型，限定攻击者可穷举的密钥长度。实现为Tamarin的语法糖扩展EntropyVerif，无需修改内核。

Result: 自动重现PMKID、KNOB和Method Confusion攻击；发现蓝牙经典新攻击Unilateral Multi-Downgrade (UMD)：反复降级会话一侧并重组已恢复密钥片段，使采用最大128位密钥设备的会话密钥恢复复杂度由2^128降至 n·2^(128/n)，n=16时降至2^12（降低2^116倍），已负责任披露给Bluetooth SIG；在三种真实协议与六种BC设备配置上验证。

Conclusion: 熵感知的符号分析在真实协议上既可行又必要；协议无关、自动化的低熵密钥符号方法论可系统发现暴力破解类攻击，弥补完美加密假设的盲区。

Abstract: Modern cryptographic protocols such as WPA2-PSK, Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE), and Bluetooth Classic (BC) tolerate low-entropy keys for user-friendliness, backward compatibility, or resource-constrained deployment, yet existing symbolic protocol verifiers rely on the perfect-encryption assumption and therefore cannot reason about brute-force attackers. Prior work addresses this gap only through protocol-specific, manually encoded reveal rules, which neither generalize across protocols nor systematically explore compound-key decomposition. We introduce the first protocol-agnostic, automated symbolic methodology for low-entropy keys, built on three abstractions: the message-derivation fact MDerive, which captures brute-force prerequisites independently of concrete function symbols; a three-tier breaking-oracle design (value-breaking, relationship-breaking, message-derivation) that composes with the standard Dolev-Yao attacker; and a graded bit-level attacker model that bounds the key length an attacker is allowed to exhaust. We realize the methodology as EntropyVerif, a syntax-sugar extension of Tamarin that requires no kernel modification. Using EntropyVerif we automatically rediscover the PMKID, KNOB, and Method Confusion attacks, and uncover a new attack on BC which we call the Unilateral Multi-Downgrade (UMD) attack: by repeatedly downgrading one side of a BC session and recombining the recovered key fragments, an attacker reduces session-key recovery against devices using the maximum 128-bit key from $2^{128}$ to $n \cdot 2^{128/n}$, bottoming out at $2^{12}$ for $n=16$---a factor of $2^{116}$ reduction that has been responsibly disclosed to the Bluetooth SIG. Across three real-world protocols and six BC device configurations, our results show that entropy-aware symbolic analysis is both feasible and necessary.

</details>


### [165] [The Detour Is the Danger: Preserving Security During LLM Agent Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33605)
*Boyang Ma,Jinku Li,Hao Wu,Sheng Gao,Man Zhou,Minghui Xu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出并研究"工作流安全降级"这一失败触发的安全威胁：LLM智能体在执行失败后自主重规划时，可能保留任务功能却静默丢弃原有的安全保护要求；作者提出ARGUS框架，通过资源绑定的安全证据、隔离的重规划验证与执行时强制约束来保全安全义务，并在864次运行评估中阻止了全部观测到的保护丢失事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益依赖外部工具与可复用Skill执行复杂工作流，并在操作失败时自主重规划。然而这种恢复机制可能只保证任务功能得以完成，却在无提示注入、无指令或工具定义篡改的情况下静默放弃原工作流所需的安全防护。现有研究未系统刻画此类由执行失败诱发的安全威胁，因此需要揭示并缓解该风险。

Method: 在可执行的MCP/Skill环境中通过故障注入进行系统评估，覆盖7个LLM、12个任务模板和3个安全敏感领域，以观测失败驱动的恢复决策如何导致保护要求失效。针对该威胁提出ARGUS框架，包含三部分：资源绑定的安全证据、隔离的恢复方案验证，以及执行时强制约束（enforcement）。

Result: 故障注入实验系统性地复现了"工作流安全降级"现象：智能体在恢复过程中会执行敏感操作而未满足原有保护要求。在独立的864次运行评估中，ARGUS在启用强制约束时阻止了所有观测到的保护丢失事件；相较于单纯阻断，恢复辅助在不同模型上带来了依赖模型的受保护任务完成率提升。

Conclusion: 安全可靠的智能体恢复不能仅以恢复任务功能为目标，而必须在工作流变更过程中持续保全安全义务；ARGUS表明，将安全证据、隔离验证与执行时强制相结合，可在保持任务可用性的同时消除恢复引发的保护丢失。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external tools and reusable Skills to execute complex workflows, autonomously replanning when operations fail. However, such recovery may preserve task functionality while silently discarding the security protections required by the original workflow. In this paper, we investigate workflow security downgrade, a failure-induced security threat in which agents execute sensitive operations without satisfying their original protection requirements. Unlike prompt injection, this threat exploits autonomous recovery decisions triggered by execution failures, without modifying instructions or tool definitions. We systematically evaluate this behavior through fault injection in an executable MCP/Skill environment spanning seven LLMs, twelve task templates, and three security-sensitive domains. To address this threat, we propose ARGUS, an obligation-preserving recovery framework that combines resource-bound security evidence, isolated recovery-plan validation, and execution-time enforcement. In a separate 864-run evaluation, ARGUS prevents all observed protection-loss events under enforcement, while recovery assistance yields model-dependent improvements in protected task completion over blocking alone. Our findings demonstrate that secure agent recovery requires preserving security obligations across workflow changes, rather than merely restoring task functionality.

</details>


### [166] [Bounding the security of the FALCON signature scheme in the quantum random oracle model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33666)
*Kelsey A. Jackson,Carl A. Miller*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次给出 FALCON 签名方案在量子随机预言机模型(QROM)下的完整安全性证明，将 FALCON 的安全性归约到 RISIS 问题与 NTRU 学习问题，并据此给出满足 NIST 安全等级的推荐参数集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIST 于 2016 年启动抗量子公钥加密与签名方案征集，2022 年确定 CRYSTALS-Dilithium、FALCON、SPHINCS+ 三个签名方案将标准化。然而 Fouque 等人在 2024 年指出 FALCON 已发表安全性证明中存在疏漏，且其修复仅在经典随机预言机模型(ROM)下有效，缺乏 QROM 下的完整证明，这构成实际部署中的安全性空白。

Method: 在量子随机预言机模型(QROM)下构造 FALCON 的完整安全性证明；利用已有的量子重编程(quantum reprogramming)技术，将 FALCON 的安全性归约到环非齐次短整数解问题(RISIS)与 NTRU 学习问题的困难性上；随后依据该归约给出的具体界，重新选取满足 NIST 安全等级要求的参数集。

Result: 建立了 FALCON 从 RISIS 与 NTRU 学习问题出发的 QROM 安全性归约，弥补了 Fouque 等人仅限 ROM 的修复；并给出在该归约下达到 NIST 规定安全等级的参数集。

Conclusion: FALCON 可在 QROM 下获得严格的安全性保证，其安全基础可归结为 RISIS 与 NTRU 学习问题的困难性；所提参数集在量子敌手模型下满足 NIST 安全要求，为 FALCON 的标准化与部署提供了更可靠的理论支撑。

Abstract: In 2016, the National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) announced their invitation for proposals of public key encryption and signature schemes that are resistant to attacks by quantum computers. Since then, several rounds of proposals and revisions have occurred resulting in the 2022 announcement that three signature schemes - CRYSTALS-Dilithium, FALCON, and SPHINCS+ - would be standardized. However, in 2024, work by Fouque et al. noted an oversight in the published security for FALCON, which they were only able to remedy in a classical computing setting, namely the random oracle model (ROM). In this paper, we provide the first complete security proof for FALCON in the quantum random oracle model (QROM). Specifically, we will prove a reduction to the security of FALCON from the ring inhomogeneous short integer solution (RISIS) problem and the NTRU learning problem utilizing known quantum reprogramming results. We then provide parameter sets which meet the security levels required by NIST under our reduction.

</details>


### [167] [Node Differential Privacy via Node-Stable Graph Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33672)
*Ishaq Aden-Ali,Adam Sealfon,Adam Smith,Thomas Steinke,Jonathan Ullman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出两个工具——近似线性时间的随机节点稳定图投影（度数裁剪）与确定性近线性时间的边权和 Lipschitz 扩展，用于设计高精度、计算高效的节点差分隐私算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 节点差分隐私算法需保护网络中每个个体及其全部关联记录，而真实图大多节点度数很低。核心挑战是如何利用这种稀疏结构提升精度，同时不削弱最坏情况下的隐私保证；现有方法在输入违反度数界时只能弃用。

Method: (1) 近线性时间随机节点稳定图投影：最大度数为 D 的图保持不变，任意图映射为最大度数约 D 的子图，单节点改动对剩余顶点间边选择分布影响很小；其行为类似度数裁剪，即使 D 远低于真实最大度数，仍保留不少于极大 D-有界子图的边数。(2) 对边权有界的图，给出确定性近线性时间的边权总和 Lipschitz 扩展：在最大度数为 D 的图上与原和一致，且在所有图上满足 D-Lipschitz。

Result: 投影在高斯外点处引入截断偏差以换取更低隐私噪声，同时保持边数不劣于极大 D-有界子图；Lipschitz 扩展提供了可替代敏感度分析的确定性工具。两者结合可构造高维向量求和与图边关联记录（如邮件线程）上梯度下降的节点隐私算法。

Conclusion: 利用真实图低度数结构可在不牺牲最坏情况隐私的前提下获得高精度；所提投影与 Lipschitz 扩展均为近线性时间、计算高效，适用于节点隐私的高维求和与梯度下降任务。

Abstract: Node-differentially private algorithms enable learning on network-structured datasets in which records reflect interactions among individuals. They protect the privacy of each individual's information, including all their associated records. A key challenge in the design of such algorithms is to exploit the structure of realistic graphs---where most nodes have low degree---to achieve high accuracy, without compromising on the worst-case privacy guarantee.
  In this work we give two new tools for designing accurate, computationally efficient node-differentially private algorithms. First, we give a nearly linear time, randomized, node-stable graph projection: the projection leaves graphs of maximum degree $D$ unchanged, maps arbitrary graphs to subgraphs of maximum degree $\hat D \approx D$, and ensures that changing one node of the input graph has only a small effect on the distribution of the edges selected among the remaining vertices. Unlike prior approaches, which abstain when the input violates the degree bound, our projection acts as degree clipping: even when $D$ is set well below the true maximum degree (trading truncation bias at high-degree outliers for lower privacy noise), it retains at least as many edges as a maximal $D$-bounded subgraph.
  Second, for graphs with bounded edge weights, we give a deterministic nearly linear time Lipschitz extension for the total edge weight that agrees with the original sum on graphs of maximum degree $D$ yet is $D$-Lipschitz on all graphs.
  Finally, we show how to apply these methods to design node-private algorithms for high-dimensional vector summation and gradient descent on datasets where records are associated with graph edges, such as email threads.

</details>


### [168] [FlashProve-Next: Verifiable Inference for Production LLM APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33741)
*Liang Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlashProve-Next 提出一种在不修改原生浮点服务栈的前提下，为闭源 LLM API 的每条响应生成零知识掩蔽可验证证明的方案，并给出 Lean 4 机械化形式化与多模型 GPU 实测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源权重 LLM API 的客户端无法核实服务端是否真的运行了宣称的模型、精度、隐藏提示与推理预算；现有方案要么依赖硬件或第三方信任，要么对整数代理模型逐层重证、代价高昂且偏离原生浮点服务。

Method: 采用非耗尽的 Reed-Solomon 权重承诺（model_id 通过隐藏列打开在证明内展开），以局部逐算子舍入契约替代整数代理与拒绝位泄漏；支持基于代价加权的抽样抽检，提供 NI-315 非交互公开验证与 ExtSeed-153 外部种子模式；用 28 个 Lean 4 模块（696 条声明、零 sorry、仅标准公理）机械化逐轮健全性、分桶抽样、F_p^3 约束掩码绑定、因果掩码、六个 IR gadget 与可执行 FPN2 验证器；实现零 Phase-2 折叠后端（折叠式 ZK 模拟器打开）。

Result: 五模型 Blackwell 校准在 1.85M GEMM/MoE 块与 1.96M 非 GEMM 行上零误拒；Qwen3-0.6B ExtSeed-153（1.47B 单元、8 MiB）在 GPU 上 450.2 s（CPU 10.76 s）完成证明（Q=128），通过 14/14 作弊矩阵，并扩展至 36 层 Qwen3-4B 与实时 vLLM Qwen3-30B-A3B-FP8 Relation 6（证明 1.49 s、Rust 验证 23.4 ms）；Rust fpn-verify 在 CPU 上 10.1 ms 校验 8 MiB FPN2 Relation 2 证明。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了与原生 bf16/fp8/mxfp4 服务兼容、不泄露权重/隐藏提示/隐藏推理/激活的可验证推理证明，兼顾形式化健全性保证与实际部署可行性；跨架构隐藏与密集 profile 下的粗粒度几何等限制仍有待后续解决。

Abstract: Clients of closed-weight LLM APIs cannot verify whether a provider ran the advertised model, precision, hidden prompt, and reasoning budget. Existing approaches either trust hardware or third parties, or re-prove every layer of an integer surrogate at high cost while diverging from native floating-point serving.
  FlashProve-Next produces verifiable, zero-knowledge-masked proofs for individual LLM API responses without modifying native serving (bf16/fp8/mxfp4, random sampling, FlashAttention, CUDA graphs, batching, prefix caching) and without disclosing weights, hidden prompts, hidden reasoning, or activations (dense profiles fix coarse geometry; cross-architecture hiding is deferred). A non-depleting Reed-Solomon weight commitment model_id opens inside proofs via hidden column openings, while local per-operator rounding contracts remove integer surrogates and the refusal-bit leak. Cost-weighted spot-checking supports non-interactive public verification (NI-315, $\varepsilon \le 2^{-40}$, $Q \le 2^{64}$) and external-seed C_pre mode (ExtSeed-153, $\varepsilon \le 2^{-20}$, $Q_{\rm samp} \le 2^{30}$).
  Twenty-eight Lean 4 modules (696 declarations, zero sorry, standard axioms only) mechanize round-by-round soundness, bucketed sampling, $\mathbb{F}_{p^3}$ constrained-mask binding, causal masking, six IR gadgets, and executable FPN2 verifiers. Five-model Blackwell calibration gives zero false rejections across 1.85M GEMM/MoE blocks and 1.96M non-GEMM rows. Our zero-Phase-2 folded backend (folded ZK simulator open) proves Qwen3-0.6B ExtSeed-153 (1.47B cells, 8 MiB) in 450.2 s GPU (10.76 s CPU) at unique-decoding $Q=128$, passes a 14/14 cheating matrix, and scales to 36-layer Qwen3-4B (dev $4+2$) and live vLLM Qwen3-30B-A3B-FP8 Relation 6 (1.49 s prove, 23.4 ms Rust verify, public weight tiles). Rust fpn-verify checks 8 MiB FPN2 Relation 2 proofs in 10.1 ms on CPU.

</details>


### [169] [Agentic-Level Adversarial Purification: Revisiting Diffusion Purifiers on Native-Resolution Agentic Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34181)
*Yan Lin,Zhongliang Guo,Jingyu Lin,Daizong Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首个在"智能体层面"研究对抗净化的论文：以 Qwen3-VL-8B 驱动的视觉语言智能体为对象，在多元素原生分辨率输入上评测扩散净化器，指出把智能体输入当作分类器输入会同时误导评估与设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言智能体基于原生分辨率的多元素页面渲染做决策，单张被扰动的图片或几笔改动的文字即可改变其判断；由于开放模型可被任意内容提供方攻击，插件式净化器是天然的第一道防线。然而现有扩散净化器只在扩散模型固定分辨率下、面向分类器或自然图像基准设计并评估；而智能体的防御评估只测试 JPEG、模糊、噪声，从未测试扩散净化器。

Method: 构建智能体级净化评估协议：净化器在原生分辨率下处理多元素输入；攻击者为灰盒（知晓智能体模型、不知防御）；对比在匹配的干净成本下进行。以 Qwen3-VL-8B 智能体为对象，考察 Divide-and-Conquer（DC）净化器，并在网页购物页面与 TextVQA 上评估；在独立留出集与第二个 VLM 上做验证。

Result: 把输入缩放到扩散模型分辨率（现有净化器的做法）会使 DC 在 TextVQA 上的干净成本从 4.0 点升至 35.7 点；在网店页面上，DC 面对网页加固扰动保持 85.0% 灰盒鲁棒准确率，而同等干净成本下的廉价输入变换最多仅 40.0%；该鲁棒性来自全局噪声水平，输入估计的掩码与噪声水平没有带来增益。

Conclusion: 智能体输入不能按分类器输入处理，否则评估与设计均会失真；提出防御的三项要求，并公开评估协议。

Abstract: Vision-language agents act on native-resolution renders of multi-element pages, where one perturbed photo or a few altered text strokes can redirect the decision. Any content provider can craft such a perturbation on the open model behind the agent, so a plug-in purifier is the natural first line of defense. Yet diffusion purifiers were designed and evaluated at the fixed resolution of the diffusion model on classifier or natural-image benchmarks, and defense evaluations for agents test JPEG, blur and noise but no diffusion purifier. We present the first study of adversarial purification at the agentic level, for an agent built on Qwen3-VL-8B: purifiers run at native resolution on multi-element inputs, face grey-box attackers who know the agent's model but not the defense, and are compared at matched clean cost. We show that treating agentic inputs as classifier inputs misleads both evaluation and design. Resizing the input to the resolution of the diffusion model, as existing purifiers do, raises the TextVQA clean cost of the Divide-and-Conquer purifier (DC) from 4.0 to 35.7 points. On web-shop pages, DC keeps 85.0% grey-box robust accuracy against web-hardened perturbations, where cheap input transformations at matched clean cost keep at most 40.0%, and this robustness comes from the global noise level: input-estimated masks and noise levels add none. These findings hold on a disjoint held-out set and on a second VLM and yield three requirements for defenses, we release the protocol.

</details>


### [170] [Evaluating Post-Quantum and Hybrid Signatures for Blockchains: Performance, Storage and Ledger Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34229)
*Anthony Mikhaiel,Essam Ghadafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在统一模拟区块链框架中实证评估13种签名配置的成本开销，发现后量子迁移的主要瓶颈是存储而非验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算对依赖ECDSA等经典数字签名方案的区块链构成长期威胁，但迁移到后量子密码学除安全性外还带来更大交易体积、更高计算开销和更快账本膨胀等实际成本，现有研究缺乏跨方案、跨链的统一实证对比。

Method: 构建统一模拟区块链框架，对比13种签名配置（ECDSA、ML-DSA-44/65/87、Falcon-512/1024、SLH-DSA-SHA2-128s及ECDSA与各后量子方案组成的6种混合配置），测量签名/验证耗时、交易处理吞吐量、签名与公钥尺寸，并将实测开销外推到比特币、以太坊、Solana、BNB Smart Chain和TRON五条公链的模型假设中。

Result: 格基方案验证开销较低，Falcon-512验证时间与ECDSA相当（30.1 μs vs 32.8 μs）；但存储开销显著，若历史交易改用最紧凑的Falcon-512，各链账本体积将膨胀79%–229%。混合配置相比独立后量子方案仅增加温和的额外存储，但ECDSA+Falcon-512的验证成本约为独立Falcon-512的两倍。比特币和Solana现有的交易与脚本尺寸限制也构成迁移障碍。

Conclusion: 区块链向后量子密码学迁移时，存储开销可能比签名验证成本构成更大挑战；迁移方案设计需优先解决账本膨胀和交易尺寸上限问题。

Abstract: Advances in quantum computing pose a long-term threat to blockchain systems that rely on classical digital signature schemes such as ECDSA. Migrating these systems to post-quantum cryptography introduces practical costs beyond cryptographic security, including larger transactions, higher computational costs and faster ledger growth. We present an empirical evaluation of 13 signature configurations in a common simulated blockchain framework: ECDSA, ML-DSA-44/65/87, Falcon-512/1024, SLH-DSA-SHA2-128s, and six hybrids combining ECDSA with each post-quantum scheme. We measure signing and verification costs, transaction-processing throughput, and signature and public-key sizes, and project the measured overheads onto five public blockchains: Bitcoin, Ethereum, Solana, BNB Smart Chain and TRON. Verification remains inexpensive for the evaluated lattice-based schemes, with Falcon-512 achieving a verification time comparable to ECDSA (30.1 versus 32.8 $μ$s). Storage overheads, however, are substantial. Under our modelling assumptions, had the recorded transaction histories used Falcon-512, the most compact post-quantum scheme evaluated, ledger sizes would have been 79--229% larger, depending on the blockchain. Hybrid configurations introduce relatively modest additional storage overhead compared with their standalone post-quantum counterparts, while ECDSA+Falcon-512 approximately doubles the verification cost of standalone Falcon-512. Existing transaction- and script-size limits also present obstacles to straightforward migration in Bitcoin and Solana. Overall, our findings suggest that storage overhead may pose a greater challenge than signature verification costs when migrating blockchain systems to post-quantum cryptography.

</details>


### [171] [Latent-in-the-Middle: On the Security of Cross-Model Latent Communication in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34278)
*Manit Baser,Vyoman Jain,Rachel Yeo Hui Min,Xianglin Yang,Meenatchi Sundaram Muthu Selva Annamalai,Dinil Mon Divakaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Chasm框架，首次系统评估跨模型KV缓存潜在传递（latent handoff）带来的安全风险，发现该机制会显著削弱目标模型的安全对齐与提示注入防御，并提出轻量级目标侧缓存守卫进行缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中跨模型KV缓存传递可降低通信与prefill开销，但目标模型因此消费了并非自身编码的上下文，其安全对齐是否仍然有效存疑，此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建Chasm框架，从三个新攻击面（源模型、映射器mapper、传递机制本身）刻画跨模型handoff攻击；实验覆盖提示注入与越狱（含迁移攻击与通过mapper的白盒优化，如对GCG的改造）；并提出轻量级目标侧cache guard，对传递来的缓存进行修正以恢复目标模型的拒答行为。

Result: 原生仅遵循0.4%基准提示注入的目标模型，经mapper从源模型传递后遵循率最高达44.2%；越狱方面，仅迁移即使同族模型攻击成功率提升至多24.6pp，零样本攻击提升至多69.2pp；通过mapper的白盒优化将攻击成功率推高至92.5%，而同一目标上普通GCG仅为37.5%。

Conclusion: 潜在handoff构成实质且被低估的安全威胁，可大幅绕过目标模型的安全对齐；轻量级目标侧缓存守卫能显著缓解此类攻击，为多智能体潜在通信的安全部署提供可行防护方向。

Abstract: As large language models increasingly collaborate in heterogeneous multi-agent systems, latent communication has emerged as an efficient alternative to exchanging natural-language messages. By sharing internal representations, agents can avoid repeatedly processing the same context, reducing communication overhead and prefill computation. One such mechanism is cross-model Key-Value (KV) cache transfer, which uses a learned mapper to translate a source model's KV cache into one a target model can use. However, this handoff means the target model consumes context it did not encode itself, thus calling into question whether its safety alignment still holds.
  In this work, we investigate the security implications of latent handoffs by introducing Chasm, a framework characterizing cross-model handoff attacks across three new attack surfaces: the source model, mapper, and handoff mechanism itself. Our experiments reveal that latent handoffs substantially undermine safety alignment and prompt-injection defenses. Notably, a target model that follows only 0.4% of benchmark prompt injections natively, follows up to 44.2% when transferred through a mapper from a source model. On the other hand, for jailbreak attacks, transfer alone raises attack success by up to 24.6 pp within a model family and zero-shot attacks by up to 69.2 pp, whereas white-box optimization through the mapper boosts the attack success rate to 92.5%, against 37.5% for ordinary GCG on the same target. To address these vulnerabilities, we propose a lightweight target-side cache guard that corrects transferred caches to restore the target model's refusal, considerably mitigating these attacks.

</details>


### [172] [Poisoning Attacks to Local Differential Privacy Protocols for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34340)
*Shang Liu,Yuhan Bao,Hongbin Zhao,Yanmei Zhang,Guan Yuan,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究本地差分隐私（LDP）框架下动态图的数据投毒攻击问题，提出绝对增益度量与统一投毒攻击框架（目标节点选择、动态虚假规模调度、虚假边注入），并设计时间增强攻击（TEA）及两个基线策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDP已被广泛用于去中心化环境下的动态图隐私保护，但现有LDP动态图协议仍易受恶意数据注入型投毒攻击；动态图的时间演化特性使攻击资源调度与累积影响度量成为设计与评估的关键难题。

Method: 1）提出绝对增益（absolute gain）指标，量化攻击前后图统计指标跨时间的整体偏差，用于衡量累积攻击影响；2）构建统一投毒攻击框架，包含目标节点选择、动态虚假节点规模调度与虚假边注入三个环节；3）在该框架下设计TEA（时间增强攻击）策略以动态调度攻击资源，并给出等规模攻击（ESA）与随机规模攻击（RSA）两个基线用于对比。

Result: 在真实世界与合成数据集上的大量实验表明，所提攻击能够显著降低目标节点图指标估计的准确性。

Conclusion: LDP动态图协议在恶意注入下存在实质性投毒风险；时间维度的资源调度对提升攻击效果至关重要，TEA优于等规模与随机规模基线。该工作为后续鲁棒性防御研究提供了评估度量与攻击基准。

Abstract: With the increasing adoption of privacy-preserving techniques in dynamic graph analysis, local differential privacy (LDP) has become a fundamental paradigm for protecting user data in decentralized environments. However, despite its strong privacy guarantees, LDP-based dynamic graph protocols remain vulnerable to data poisoning attacks through malicious data injection. In this paper, we present the first systematic study of data poisoning attacks against privacy-preserving dynamic graphs under the LDP framework. Considering the temporal evolution of dynamic graphs, we identify key challenges in attack design and evaluation, including how to schedule attack resources across time and how to measure cumulative attack impact. To address these challenges, we introduce an absolute gain metric to quantify the overall deviation of graph statistical metrics before and after attacks across time. Based on this metric, we further propose a unified poisoning attack framework for dynamic graphs, which consists of target node selection, dynamic fake node scale scheduling, and fake edge injection. Within this framework, we design a temporal enhanced attack (TEA) strategy that dynamically schedules attack resources to maximize attack effectiveness, along with two baseline strategies, equal-scale attack (ESA) and random-scale attack (RSA), for comparison. Extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic datasets demonstrate that the proposed attacks can significantly degrade the accuracy of graph metric estimation for target nodes.

</details>


### [173] [AgentLens: Graph-guided Security Auditing for LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34380)
*Peizhuo Lv,Tao Chen,Mengya Shi,Ziyu Lin,Yuxuan Chen,Ruigang Liang,Tianwei Zhang,Wei Dong,XiaoFeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AgentLens——基于审计知识图谱+路径特定载荷+多轮审计场景的LLM智能体安全审计框架，在5个高星智能体应用中发现44个漏洞且全部可执行复现，检出量约为AgentFuzz的3.67倍，12项获CVE编号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体将决策与外部工具、文件、API、执行环境耦合，其实现缺陷与不安全交互可能导致RCE、任意文件访问、SQL注入与数据泄露，因此部署前审计必不可少。现有方法要么只针对特定漏洞类别，要么无法验证静态弱点是否对应可执行利用。

Method: AgentLens分析目标智能体的实现、组件交互与内建防御，构建"审计知识图谱"刻画这些要素如何交互暴露风险；据此推导候选攻击路径，生成路径特定验证载荷，并构建任务相关上下文下的真实多轮审计场景，以在执行过程中验证疑似风险是否表现为违规安全行为。

Result: 在5个GitHub星标均超24,000的常用智能体应用中发现44个漏洞，全部在执行中被触发验证；相较SOTA方法AgentFuzz检出漏洞数提升3.67倍；迄今12项报告已获CVE编号。

Conclusion: 将静态实现分析与动态可执行验证通过审计知识图谱衔接，可显著提升LLM智能体安全审计的覆盖度与可信度，实现"发现即可复现"的漏洞确认。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are emerging as a key paradigm for automating software engineering by coupling LLM-driven decision-making with external tools, files, APIs, and execution environments. However, weaknesses in agent implementations and insecure interactions between the agent and external resources can lead to severe consequences, including remote code execution, arbitrary file access, SQL injection, and data leakage. This makes pre-deployment auditing essential for systematically identifying implementation weaknesses and validating whether they can manifest as concrete security risks in realistic agent workflows. Existing approaches either focus on specific vulnerability classes or lack the capability to validate whether identified static weaknesses correspond to executable exploits. In this paper, we propose AgentLens, a systematic auditing framework for LLM-based agents. AgentLens analyzes the target agent's implementation, component interactions, and built-in defenses, and organizes them into an Audit Knowledge Graph that captures how these factors interact to expose potential security risks. Based on this graph, AgentLens derives candidate attack paths, generates path-specific validation payloads, and constructs realistic multi-turn audit scenarios that place these payloads in task-relevant contexts to validate whether suspected risks manifest as security-violating behaviors during execution. Across five widely-used agent applications, each with over 24,000 GitHub stars, AgentLens identifies 44 vulnerabilities, and every vulnerability is validated by being triggered during execution. Compared with the state-of-the-art approach (AgentFuzz), AgentLens detects 3.67$\times$ more vulnerabilities. To date, 12 reported issues have been assigned CVE IDs.

</details>


### [174] [Northstar: Efficient Verifiable Negacyclic Polynomial Arithmetic with Applications to Encrypted Lattice-based Multi-Asset ERC-20 and Verifiable FHE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34417)
*Yeoh Wei Zhu,Shaltiel Eloul,Yash Satsangi,Rob Otter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Northstar，一种原生证明负循环（negacyclic）算术的高效技术，结合STARK框架在可验证FHE中实现证明时间、验证时间与证明大小的综合最优性能，并首次构建可被智能合约高效验证的加密多资产（约1000资产）ERC-20变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格密码可构建从数字签名、加密到全同态加密（FHE）的多种后量子方案，但验证格计算的正确性代价较高，尤其是计算密集的FHE运算，以及智能合约链上验证等资源受限场景。

Method: 设计Northstar技术，针对负循环算术提供原生（natively）证明能力，并与现有STARK证明框架集成。

Result: 在证明时间、验证时间和证明大小三项指标上，综合优于现有可验证FHE构造的先进方案；并据此构建首个可由智能合约高效验证的加密多资产（约1000种资产）ERC-20变体。

Conclusion: Northstar在可验证FHE场景下取得整体最佳性能，且所构建的多资产加密ERC-20在当前区块链生态中具备实际可部署性。

Abstract: Lattice-based cryptography can be used to construct a variety of post-quantum cryptographic schemes, ranging from standard cryptographic primitives such as digital signatures and encryption, to fully homomorphic encryption (FHE). However, the verification of correct lattice computations remains relatively expensive, particularly for computationally intensive FHE operations and for resource-constrained environments such as on-chain verification via smart contracts. We propose Northstar, an efficient technique for proving negacyclic arithmetic natively. When integrated with existing STARK proving frameworks, Northstar achieves the best overall performance across prover time, verifier time, and proof size compared to prior state-of-the-art approaches for verifiable FHE constructions. Furthermore, Northstar is used to construct the first verifiable encrypted multi-asset (~ 1000 assets) variant of ERC-20 that is efficiently verifiable by a smart contract, and is practically deployable in the current blockchain ecosystem.

</details>


### [175] [Fuel as Identity: Authenticating Inter-Satellite Laser Links after Total Key Compromise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34470)
*Leon Birkel,Mohammad Hamad,Sebastian Steinhorst*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出OrbitLock协议：以卫星轨道位置作为信任锚，结合ML-KEM与历史链式认证，在密钥完全泄露后仍能自愈，敌手需承担持续推力成本才能伪装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨星座的星间光链路安全完全依赖密钥；一旦攻击者获取卫星全部密钥便无剩余秘密可再认证。地面重加密只是用泄露密钥加密新密钥，而抗妥协协议又预设初始认证，均无法奏效。

Method: 将轨道位置作为信任锚：伪装者必须阻挡并转发光束，其位置偏离自由落体轨道，需持续推力。形式化"位置门控敌手"模型，对共享圆轨道上的质点飞行器证明维持阻挡所需delta-v的紧致下界。设计OrbitLock：位置门控+ML-KEM的历史链式协议，每条链路独立认证，链式组合迫使伪装者出现在每条链路上。

Result: 链长超过伪装者推进剂寿命后，所有历史密钥可被追溯认证；被动攻击者在下一轮被排除，主动攻击者分裂链条并在推进剂耗尽时暴露，操作员重置后链条可自愈。Starlink每颗卫星均有配对卫星，在1 km排除区下，立方星级伪装者约两天耗尽推进剂，而协议仅占用链路带宽不到0.02%。

Conclusion: 卫星发射前无需跨链路密钥，消除供应链泄露风险；该机制在完全密钥妥协后（即便无妥协感知）仍能修复链路，将安全从密钥独占转为轨道物理约束。

Abstract: Optical inter-satellite links form the backbone of entire constellations in low Earth orbit, yet their security rests on keys alone. When an attacker obtains every key of a satellite, no secret is left to authenticate it again. Existing fixes fail, since ground rekeying encrypts new keys under the leaked ones, and post-compromise protocols assume an authenticated start. As a remedy, the satellite's orbit can take over as the trust anchor. The attacker then needs an impostor satellite that blocks and retransmits the beam, a chord beneath the curved orbit. Hence, the blocking point lies off free fall and costs the impostor continuous thrust. We formalize this position-gated adversary and prove, for satellites on a shared circular orbit, a tight lower bound on the propellant cost in delta-v of a point-mass craft that keeps blocking, however it moves. We present OrbitLock, a history-chained protocol that combines this position gate with ML-KEM. Each link authenticates on its own, and we sketch how combining links forces an impostor onto every link. Once the chain outlasts an impostor's propellant, every key back to the first is authenticated retroactively. A satellite thus needs no crosslink key before launch, so none can leak in the supply chain. The same mechanism heals the link after total key compromise, even without compromise awareness. A passive attacker is shut out at the next round, whereas an active one splits the chains and is exposed once its propellant runs out. The chain can then heal after an operator reset. As a result, every Starlink satellite has a partner on whose link a CubeSat-class impostor runs out of propellant in about two days. This holds against one rigid craft per link running the best attack we know, if each terminal enforces a 1 km exclusion zone, while the protocol only uses under 0.02% of the link.

</details>


### [176] [From Models to Services: Systematizing Security Assurance for Model-as-a-Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34623)
*Jianming Zhou,Yang Luo,Guangpu Chen,Zhaoyu Chen,Yifeng Gao,Qingni Shen,Zhonghai Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文从服务视角系统化梳理大模型即服务（MaaS）的安全研究：将MaaS分解为五层架构与五条信任边界，定义六类对手与六个安全目标，据此编码120项研究并对50项防御进行统一评估，指出研究分布失衡与防御落地不足，并提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型主要以云服务形式交付，不同主体的资产耦合于同一服务流水线中，而相关安全研究分散在多个领域且威胁模型各不相同，缺乏统一的服务视角框架来比较与整合。

Method: 构建系统化综述框架：(1) 将MaaS分解为五层堆栈与五条信任边界；(2) 定义六类对手类别与六个安全目标；(3) 用该编码体系对120项研究进行标注；(4) 在统一标准下评估50项防御措施；(5) 与现有标准与法规对比，提出研究议程。

Result: 44.2%的研究集中于行为完整性（behavioral integrity），而服务完整性、可用性与成本、可问责性合计仅占13.3%；防御在生产服务上被评估的比例不到攻击评估比例的一半；针对服务提供方的密码学或硬件防护机制均无法由客户单方面采用。

Conclusion: MaaS安全研究存在明显失衡：过度聚焦模型行为层面，忽视服务层面与责任追溯；防御验证滞后于攻击研究，且缺乏客户可独立部署的对抗提供方的机制。作者据此结合标准与法规提出后续研究议程。

Abstract: Large language models are mainly delivered as cloud services, in which the assets of different principals are coupled within a single service pipeline, while the related security research is scattered across several fields with differing threat models. This paper systematizes this research from a service perspective. We decompose Model-as-a-Service (MaaS) into a five-layer stack and five trust boundaries, define six adversary classes and six security goals, use them to code 120 studies, and evaluate 50 defenses under a unified set of criteria. The results show that 44.2% of the studies concentrate on behavioral integrity, while service integrity, availability and cost, and accountability together account for only 13.3%. Defenses are evaluated on production services at less than half the rate of attacks, and none of the cryptographic or hardware mechanisms that protect against the provider can be adopted unilaterally by customers. Finally, we propose a research agenda by comparison with current standards and regulations.

</details>


### [177] [BridgeHammer: DNN Backdoor Attack via CPU-iGPU Shared-memory Rowhammer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34684)
*Yufei Wang,Saion K. Roy,Yunsi Fei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 BridgeHammer，一种利用 CPU-iGPU 异构 SoC 共享物理内存的 Rowhammer 后门攻击，通过跨域协调权重放置与故障注入实现深度学习模型完整性破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构 SoC 中 CPU 与 iGPU 共享物理内存，CPU 侧的内存破坏可威胁 iGPU 推理所用模型的完整性，但植入后门面临“在哪注入故障”和“何时干预”两大挑战。

Method: 协调两个阶段：在 zero-copy 绑定前将目标权重放置到脆弱 DRAM 位置，在推理前通过 Rowhammer 注入位翻转，使 iGPU 使用被篡改的模型推理。

Result: 在 Intel CPU-iGPU 平台基于 OpenVINO 评测 ResNet-20、VGG-16、ResNet-50 的 INT8 与 FP32 共六种配置，平均 ASR 88.9%、干净准确率 86.3%，目标页放置与位翻转可稳定复现。

Conclusion: 揭示了异构 CPU-iGPU SoC 上新的完整性漏洞，呼吁跨域内存完整性防护。

Abstract: Heterogeneous systems-on-chips (SoCs) share physical memory between CPUs and integrated GPUs (iGPUs), creating a path for CPU-side memory corruption to compromise the integrity of deep learning models used for iGPU inference. Implanting a backdoor that causes targeted misclassifications on trigger-bearing inputs while retaining accuracy on normal inputs requires carefully selected weight changes. However, realizing these changes presents two challenges: where to induce faults, since vulnerable DRAM locations may not contain the targeted weights, and when to intervene, since memory placement and fault injection must align with different stages of the model deployment lifecycle that involve two distinct computation domains. We present BridgeHammer, a Rowhammer-based backdoor attack that addresses these challenges by coordinating targeted weight placement before zero-copy binding for the iGPU and fault injection before inference. The iGPU then uses the corrupted model for subsequent inferences. We evaluate BridgeHammer on a heterogeneous Intel CPU-iGPU platform using OpenVINO, targeting ResNet-20, VGG-16, and ResNet-50 at both INT8 and FP32 precision. Across all six configurations, BridgeHammer achieves an 88.9% attack success rate (ASR) and 86.3% clean accuracy on average, with reliable target-page placement and bit-flip reproduction. These results demonstrate a new integrity vulnerability on heterogeneous CPU-iGPU SoCs, motivating cross-domain memory integrity protections.

</details>


### [178] [Distance from the Truth: Why Fragmented Merchant Underwriting Misses Risk, and What Source-Attributable Evidence Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34696)
*Russell Lessard,Jeremy Blackburn,Tim Hines,Matthew Koepp,Jason Patrick*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Merchant underwriting is vulnerable to fragmented evidence evaluation. The authors identify three failure modes—distance from source, the correlation gap, and point-in-time controls—map them to identity-proofing, CDD, sanctions, third-party-risk, card-network, and Nacha requirements, and propose a ChainIT Transaction Truth Architecture application (provenance, correlation, persistence) with a unified evidence record, a proposed evidence/decision receipt, a 90-day shadow enrollment pilot, and a ten-question self-assessment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fragmented merchant underwriting cannot establish: (a) the source/freshness/ownership of evidence, (b) that the same business actually operates the account, (c) that its representative may bind it, (d) that settlement instructions are authorized. Individually favorable checks do not compose into these guarantees, creating a gap between decision and justifiable evidence.

Method: Conceptual/architectural proposal: three failure modes (distance from the source; correlation gap; point-in-time controls) mapped to six regulatory/network frameworks (identity-proofing, CDD, sanctions, third-party risk, card-network rules, Nacha). Application of the ChainIT Transaction Truth Architecture based on three properties (provenance, correlation, persistence), yielding a logically unified evidence record that separates evidence validation, applicant verification, organizational authority, and institutional risk judgment; plus an evidence/decision receipt, a defined 90-day shadow enrollment pilot with adjudicated outcome measures and deliberate control-failure tests, and a ten-question self-assessment.

Result: No empirical or production results are reported. Proposed controls, illustrative scenarios, and pilot measures are explicitly flagged as proposals, not observed production outcomes.

Conclusion: Underwriting integrity requires evaluating evidence jointly—by provenance, correlation across person/entity/owner/representative/activity/settlement destination, and persistence over time—rather than as independent point-in-time checks. The proposed ChainIT-based unified evidence record and receipt, validated via the 90-day shadow pilot, offer an auditable route to close the three failure modes.

Abstract: Merchant underwriting becomes vulnerable when evidence is evaluated without enough visibility into its source, freshness, relationship to the applicant, or meaning for the proposed activity. Individually favorable checks do not necessarily establish that the same business is operating the merchant account, that its representative may bind it, or that its settlement instructions are authorized. We describe three failure modes of fragmented underwriting: distance from the source, the gap between a decision and the evidence needed to justify it; the correlation gap, in which material relationships among the person, legal entity, owners or controllers, representative, business activity, and settlement destination are not evaluated when each signal is considered alone; and point-in-time controls, in which a historical verification is treated as present eligibility. We map these failure modes to identity-proofing, customer-due-diligence, sanctions, third-party-risk, card-network, and Nacha requirements, and propose a merchant-underwriting application of the ChainIT Transaction Truth Architecture built on provenance, correlation, and persistence. The application keeps a logically unified evidence record that identifies what was checked, the subject and relationship involved, the relevant times, the applicable policy, the decision, and any exceptions, and it keeps evidence validation, applicant verification, organizational authority, and the institution's risk judgment separate. We also set out a proposed evidence and decision receipt, a defined 90-day shadow enrollment pilot with adjudicated outcome measures and deliberate control-failure tests, and a ten-question self-assessment. Proposed controls, illustrative scenarios, and pilot measures are identified as such and are not represented as observed production results.

</details>


### [179] [Make the Adversary Spend More: Stateful Deception as a Cost-Exchange Primitive for AI-Era Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34770)
*Alessandro Cantelli-Forti,Isabella Marasco,Hosam Alamleh,Giada Boschi,Michele Colajanni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出以"代价交换"而非真实性或检出率来评判网络欺骗，并将"按对手隔离的持久状态"确立为该交换有利的核心原语；形式化为欺骗交换率（DER），并在Cowrie+本地LLM的SSH蜜罐中完成防御侧评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM一方面降低了攻击者扫描与自适应攻击的成本，另一方面诱使防御者陷入无上限的算力消耗。既有蜜罐以"逼真度/检出率"为设计目标，忽略了欺骗本质上是一场成本博弈：只有让攻击者付出的努力大于防御者的资源投入，欺骗才有意义。因此需要一个可度量、可优化的代价交换框架。

Method: 提出核心原语"对手特定的持久状态"：不同于会重置或合并会话的传统蜜罐，每个被识别的对手重新进入其自认为已攻陷的世界，该世界与其自身历史行为保持一致、且与其他对手相互隔离；LLM仅作为显式状态的受限渲染器，绝不作为状态的存储。据此形式化欺骗交换率（DER）= 任务加权的对手努力消耗 / 防御者资源投入。实现上以Cowrie搭配本地LLM构建SSH蜜罐，并对提示与确定性层进行优化以降低单会话防御成本；论文明确其范围限于防御侧的交换评估，按对手持久状态与DER的攻击者侧仅作规格说明而尚未测量。

Result: 在五个开源权重模型上，确定性层与提示优化使单会话防御成本相对"全LLM朴素诱饵"降低约63%。注意：该结果仅覆盖DER的防御侧分母，持久状态机制与攻击者侧努力指标尚未实测。

Conclusion: 欺骗系统的评价标准应从"逼真/可检出"转向"代价交换是否有利"，其可操作抓手是将无状态诱饵升级为按对手隔离、记忆自洽的持久状态，而LLM应退居受限渲染器以保证状态可控与成本可约束。DER提供了该交换的统一度量，但当前仅完成防御侧验证；按对手状态的实现与攻击者侧努力量化是后续工作。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are transforming the economics of intrusion by reducing the attacker's cost to scan and adapt, while tempting defenders into unbounded compute. We argue that cyber deception should be judged by the cost exchange it imposes, not by realism or detection, and that the primitive making that exchange favorable is adversary-specific persistent state. Rather than a honeypot that resets or merges sessions, each recognized adversary re-enters the world it believes it has compromised, kept coherent with its own past actions and isolated from every other adversary, while the language model acts only as a constrained renderer of explicit state, never as its store. We formalize the trade-off as a Deception Exchange Rate (DER), relating mission-weighted adversary effort burned to defender resources spent, and instantiate it in an SSH honeypot pairing Cowrie with a local LLM. In this paper, we focus on the defender-side evaluation of that exchange, while specifying, but not yet measuring, per-adversary state and the adversary side of the DER. Across five open-weight models, deterministic-layer and prompt optimizations cut per-session defender cost by around 63% against a naive all-LLM decoy.

</details>


### [180] [Full Structure Recovery of Linearized Reed-Solomon Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34835)
*Valentina Astore,Martino Borello,Alain Couvreur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多项式时间的算法，仅凭任意生成矩阵即可完整恢复线性化Reed-Solomon码（LRS码）的结构，表明LRS码无法直接用于McEliece类加密方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRS码是同时推广RS码与Gabidulin码的最优和秩度量码，解码能力优异，被视为后量子编码密码的潜在候选。但已有工作要么区分器/恢复方法不够一般，要么计算复杂度过高，因此需要判断LRS码能否用于McEliece类加密。

Method: 将Overbeck型攻击与适当的矩阵操作及缩短（shortening）技术相结合，针对不同码维数区间分别恢复LRS码的求值参数，从而仅从一个任意生成矩阵出发完成结构恢复。

Result: 给出一个多项式时间的完整结构恢复过程，可覆盖不同码维数范围，克服了以往方法的一般性不足与复杂度过高的问题。

Conclusion: 与Hamming度量、秩度量下的对应码类似，LRS码不能按其原始形式用于McEliece类加密，需额外设计防护或结构隐藏手段。

Abstract: Linearized Reed-Solomon codes (LRS) are optimal sum-rank-metric codes that generalize both Reed-Solomon and Gabidulin codes, and benefit from excellent decoding capabilities, making them potentially interesting candidates for post-quantum code-based cryptography. Previous works have investigated the underlying geometric structure of LRS codes, identifying geometric invariants that distinguish them from random codes. Other works have proposed approaches to recovering the LRS structure from an arbitrary generator matrix, together with additional data. However, both the distinguishers and the recovery techniques proposed so far are either not fully general, or have a prohibitive computational complexity. In this paper, we propose a polynomial-time procedure for the full structure recovery of LRS codes from the sole knowledge of an arbitrary generator matrix. Our results show that, like their Hamming and rank metric counterparts, LRS codes cannot be used as they are for McEliece-like encryption. Our approach combines Overbeck-type attacks with suitable matrix manipulations and shortening techniques to recover the evaluation parameters across different ranges of code dimensions.

</details>


### [181] [Model Weights, Airlocks and Sandboxes: A Call for Technical Validation of Re-identification Tests under the EDPB Anonymisation Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34859)
*Rickard Brännvall,Henrik Forsgren,Erik Ylipää,Aslak Felin,Michael Popoff,Susanne Stenberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对EDPB《02/2026匿名化指南》，作者提出以"安全处理环境+气闸审查"为框架评估重识别测试，区分三种证据类型，并主张将重识别测试作为测量方法加以验证（须报告检出限、偏差与不确定度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDPB 2026年7月公开征求意见的匿名化指南以"可接触数据的实体"为参照界定匿名性，将AI模型纳入范围并要求测试，但未说明重识别测试应如何设计与验证、其结果在多大程度上构成证据；且测试失败可能仅意味着测试过弱，故需澄清证据可靠性问题。

Method: 基于对征求意见的回复构建分析框架：以输出经发布前审查的安全处理环境（"气闸"）为基础，区分发布形式与三类证据（形式证据、经验证据、间接证据）；提出七个附带情境的开放问题，并在三种场景（模型权重发布、气闸审查、监管沙盒）中考察其技术面向。

Result: 在三种场景中，匿名性评估均取决于测试结果的可依赖性：重识别尝试失败并不等于匿名性成立，可能只是测试灵敏度不足；据此识别出测试设计、验证与证据权重方面的关键缺口。

Conclusion: 应借鉴其他受监管领域的测量实践，将重识别测试作为测量方法予以验证，明确其检出限、偏差与测量不确定度，从而使匿名性判断具备可比较、可审查的证据基础。

Abstract: The Guidelines 02/2026 on anonymisation of the European Data Protection Board (EDPB), published for consultation in July 2026, assess anonymity relative to the entities that may access data. They bring AI models into scope and call for testing, but do not say how a re-identification test should be designed or validated, or how far its result counts as evidence. Building on our reply to the consultation, we describe a framework based on a secure processing environment whose outputs are reviewed before release (an airlock), and distinguish forms of release and three types of evidence: formal, empirical and indirect. We raise seven open questions, each with a scenario, and examine their technical side in three settings: the release of model weights (the trained parameters of a machine learning model), review at an airlock, and regulatory sandboxes, in which an authority supervises development and testing. In each setting the assessment depends on how far a test result can be relied on, since a failed re-identification attempt may mean only that the test was too weak. Drawing on measurement practice in other regulated fields, we call for re-identification tests to be validated as measurement methods, with stated detection limit, bias and uncertainty.

</details>


### [182] [A Survey of Security, Privacy, and Ethics of Large Language Models in Telecom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34868)
*Joy Bose,Srinivas Adyapak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文是一篇综述，系统梳理大语言模型在电信领域应用中的隐私、安全与伦理风险及其防护措施，提出电信专属风险分类体系并列出开放研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被越来越多地集成到电信的客户服务、网络分析与运营支持中，带来效率与创新，但电信数据具有独特的高敏感性和纵向长期性，其部署引发关键的隐私、安全与伦理挑战，而现有研究缺乏针对电信场景的系统性风险与防护梳理。

Method: 综述性研究：构建一个电信特定的风险分类体系（taxonomy），将隐私、安全、伦理三个维度与具体应用场景（客户服务、网络分析、运营支持）相对应，并对相应的防护措施（safeguards）进行归纳整理。

Result: 提出电信场景下的LLM风险分类框架，实现隐私—安全—伦理与用例之间的映射，并识别出若干开放研究挑战，包括纵向数据隐私、跨境合规以及可扩展的防护机制（scalable guardrails）。

Conclusion: 电信领域LLM的安全可信部署需依托场景化风险分类与系统化防护设计；未来研究应重点关注纵向数据隐私保护、跨司法辖区合规以及可规模化落地的护栏技术。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into telecommunications for customer service, network analytics, and operational support. While offering efficiency and innovation, their deployment raises critical privacy, security, and ethical challenges due to the uniquely sensitive and longitudinal nature of telecom data. This paper presents a survey of LLM risks and safeguards in telecom context. Our survey introduces a telecom-specific taxonomy linking privacy, security, and ethics with use cases and outlines open research challenges such as longitudinal data privacy, cross-border compliance, and scalable guardrails.

</details>


### [183] [SoK: When FL Clients Cannot Train -- Delegated Federated Learning for IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34876)
*Hamed Mohammed Alkharsh,Aydin Abadi,Jakub Szelag,Suleiman Muhammad Sabo,Ishak Semuel Beno*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文将"客户端将训练外包给能力更强的第三方"这一常见设定系统化为Delegated FL，并从委托视图隐私、委托归因、受限拥有者可行性三个维度审计29个系统，指出普遍存在的"组合缺口"（composition gap）：单项机制各自成立，却无法支撑同一端到端声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习默认客户端具备完成自身训练贡献所需算力，但资源受限的IoT设备难以满足该假设，实践中普遍把部分或全部客户端训练委托给更强能力方。这种"数据所有权与以所有者名义执行的训练相分离"的设定缺乏统一分析框架，现有系统虽各自提供隐私、认证等机制，却缺少对同一次委托转移上端到端保证的评估。

Method: 作者提出Delegated FL的系统化框架，围绕同一"委托转移"考察三个维度：委托视图隐私（delegate-view privacy）、委托归因（delegated attribution）与受限拥有者可行性（constrained-owner feasibility）。据此审计29个系统，并设计两项在Raspberry Pi上的受控实验：其一刻画加密输入与暴露残差导致的信息泄露共存问题，以及从split-learning梯度中推断本地保留标签；其二考察消息认证能拒绝篡改但不能证明执行者确实完成了规定计算。

Result: 审计发现重复出现的组合缺口：隐私机制只保护选定的中间产物，未刻画攻击者累积获得的视图；归因机制只建立个体执行关系，未绑定完整的委托转移链条；计算卸载也未验证剩余工作负载是否落在所有者的资源预算内。Raspberry Pi实验证实：加密输入可与暴露残差造成的信息泄露并存；本地保留的标签仍可从split-learning梯度中推断；消息认证虽能拒绝篡改，却无法确立执行者执行的正是规定计算。

Conclusion: 各项分别成立的保证不一定能支撑同一端到端声明，因此隐私、归因与剩余拥有者开销应在同一次委托转移上统一评估。该框架为Delegated FL提供了可比的分析口径，并指出组合层面的保证缺口是当前系统设计的核心薄弱点。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) assumes that a client can perform the computation required to produce its own contribution. This assumption is often unrealistic for resource-constrained Internet of Things (IoT) devices, motivating designs that delegate some or all client-side training to more capable parties. We systematize this setting as delegated federated learning (Delegated FL), where data ownership and execution of owner-attributed learning are separated. We develop a framework that examines three dimensions over the same delegated transition: delegate-view privacy, delegated attribution, and constrained-owner feasibility. Our audit of 29 systems reveals a recurring composition gap. Privacy mechanisms protect selected artifacts without necessarily characterizing the accumulated view. Attribution mechanisms establish individual execution relations without necessarily binding the complete delegated transition. Furthermore, computational offloading does not establish whether the remaining workload fits the owner's resource budget. Two controlled studies on Raspberry Pi devices illustrate these gaps. We show that encrypted inputs can coexist with information leakage through an exposed residual and that locally retained labels remain inferable from split-learning gradients. We also show that message authentication can reject tampering without establishing that the executor performed the prescribed computation. Our findings demonstrate why individually established guarantees do not necessarily support the same end-to-end claim and motivate evaluating privacy, attribution, and residual owner cost over the same delegated transition.

</details>
